\|F\|_{s,u}\lesssim\varrho(s,u)^{1/2}\int_{t_{0}}^{s}\frac{1}{\varrho(\tau,u)^{1/2}}\|f\|_{\tau,u}d\tau,

(3.108)

where
\|F\|_{s,u}
denotes the
L^{2}
norm of
F
over
\Sigma_{s}^{u}
which is defined in Definition
2.3
.

One can now derive
L^{2}
estimates for the quantities such as

{\check{\chi}}
,
{\vphantom{\not{\pi}}}{}^{(Z)}\not{\pi}_{\mathring{L}}
,
{\vphantom{\not{\pi}}}{}^{(R)}\not{\pi}
,
\check{L}
,
\upsilon
,
x^{j}

(j=1,2)
and
R
similarly as in
[
10
, Proposition 8.1]
. However, here one has to pay more attentions to the
precise orders of smallness for
\delta
and the time decay or increasing rates in each involved
quantity, one can get

Proposition 3.1
.

Under the assumptions
(\star)
, when
\delta>0
is small, it holds that for
{m\leq 2N-6}
,

\displaystyle\delta^{l}\|Z^{m+1}\mu\|_{s,u}\lesssim\delta^{k(1-\varepsilon_{0})-1/2}s^{1/2}\ln s+\delta^{(k-1)(1-\varepsilon_{0})}s^{1/2}\Big(\sqrt{\tilde{E}_{1,\leq m+2}(s,u)}

\displaystyle\qquad\qquad\qquad\quad+\ln s\sqrt{\tilde{E}_{2,\leq m+2}(s,u)}+\delta^{k(1-\varepsilon_{0})-1}\sqrt{\int_{0}^{u}\tilde{F}_{1}(s,u^{\prime})du^{\prime}}\Big),

\displaystyle\delta^{l}\|\not{\mathcal{L}}_{Z}^{m}\check{\chi}\|_{s,u}\lesssim\delta^{k(1-\varepsilon_{0})+1/2}s^{-3/2}\ln s+s^{-1}\mathscr{E}_{m+2}(s,u),

\displaystyle\delta^{l}\|\not{\mathcal{L}}_{Z}^{m+1}\not{g}\|_{s,u}+s^{-1}\delta^{l}\|{Z}^{m+2}x\|_{s,u}\lesssim\delta^{1/2}s^{1/2}+\mathscr{E}_{m+2}(s,u),

\displaystyle\delta^{l}\|\not{\mathcal{L}}_{Z}^{m+1}R\|_{s,u}+\delta^{l}\|Z^{m+1}\upsilon\|_{s,u}\lesssim\delta^{k(1-\varepsilon_{0})+1/2}s^{1/2}\ln s+s\mathscr{E}_{m+2}(s,u),

\displaystyle\delta^{l}\|\not{\mathcal{L}}_{Z}^{m}{\vphantom{\not{\pi}}}{}^{(R)}\not{\pi}_{T}\|_{s,u}\lesssim(1+\delta^{k(1-\varepsilon_{0})-1}\ln s)\Big(\delta^{k(1-\varepsilon_{0})+1/2}s^{-1/2}\ln s+\mathscr{E}_{m+2}(s,u)\Big),

\displaystyle\delta^{l}\|Z^{m+1}\check{L}^{j}\|_{s,u}+\delta^{l}\|Z^{m+1}\check{\varrho}\|_{s,u}+\delta^{l}\|\not{\mathcal{L}}_{Z}^{m}{\vphantom{\not{\pi}}}{}^{(R)}\not{\pi}\|_{s,u}+\delta^{l}\|\not{\mathcal{L}}_{Z}^{m}{\vphantom{\not{\pi}}}{}^{(R)}\not{\pi}_{\mathring{L}}\|_{s,u}

\displaystyle\qquad\qquad\qquad\qquad\qquad\qquad\lesssim\delta^{k(1-\varepsilon_{0})+1/2}s^{-1/2}\ln s+\mathscr{E}_{m+2}(s,u),\quad j=1,2,

\displaystyle\delta^{l}\|\not{\mathcal{L}}_{Z}^{m}{\vphantom{\not{\pi}}}{}^{(T)}\not{\pi}_{\mathring{L}}\|_{s,u}\lesssim\delta^{k(1-\varepsilon_{0})-1/2}s^{-1/2}\ln s+\delta^{(k-1)(1-\varepsilon_{0})}s^{-1/2}\Big(\sqrt{\tilde{E}_{1,\leq m+2}(s,u)}

\displaystyle\qquad\qquad\qquad\qquad+\ln s\sqrt{\tilde{E}_{2,\leq m+2}(s,u)}+\delta^{k(1-\varepsilon_{0})-1}\sqrt{\int_{0}^{u}\tilde{F}_{1}(s,u^{\prime})du^{\prime}}\Big),

\displaystyle\delta^{l}\|\not{\mathcal{L}}_{Z}^{m}{\vphantom{\not{\pi}}}{}^{(T)}\not{\pi}\|_{s,u}\lesssim(\delta^{1/2}+\delta^{k(1-\varepsilon_{0})-1/2}\ln s)s^{-1/2}+\delta^{(k-1)(1-\varepsilon_{0})}s^{-1/2}\Big(\sqrt{\tilde{E}_{1,\leq m+2}(s,u)}

\displaystyle\qquad\qquad\qquad\qquad+\ln s\sqrt{\tilde{E}_{2,\leq m+2}(s,u)}+(s^{-1/2}+\delta^{k(1-\varepsilon_{0})-1})\sqrt{\int_{0}^{u}\tilde{F}_{1}(s,u^{\prime})du^{\prime}}\Big),

where
\mathscr{E}_{m+2}(s,u)=\delta^{k(1-\varepsilon_{0})}s^{-\iota}\sqrt{\tilde{E}_{1,\leq m+2}(s,u)}+\delta^{k(1-\varepsilon_{0})+\varepsilon_{0}}(1+\delta^{k(1-\varepsilon_{0})-1})s^{-1/2}\ln^{2}s\sqrt{\tilde{E}_{2,\leq m+2}(s,u)}+\delta^{(k-1)(1-\varepsilon_{0})}\sqrt{\int_{0}^{u}\tilde{F}_{1}(s,u^{\prime})du^{\prime}}
with
\tilde{E}_{i,\leq m+2}(s,u)=\sup_{t_{0}\leq\tau\leq s}E_{i,\leq m+2}(\tau,u)

(i=1,2)
and
\tilde{F}_{1,\leq m+2}(s,u)

=\sup_{t_{0}\leq\tau\leq s}F_{1,\leq m+2}(\tau,u)
.

Proof.

By Lemma
2.3
, the explicit expressions of
\varrho\mathring{L}\check{L}^{i}
,
T\check{L}^{i}
and
R\check{L}^{i}

can be given since
\varrho\mathring{L}\check{L}^{i}=\varrho\mathring{L}\mathring{L}^{i}-\check{L}^{i}
,
T\check{L}^{i}=T\mathring{L}^{i}+\frac{\mu}{\varrho}g^{0i}+\frac{\mu}{\varrho}\check{L}^{i}+\frac{\mu-1}{\varrho^{2}}x^{i}
and
R\check{L}^{i}={R^{X}}\not{d}_{X}\check{L}^{i}

hold.
One then gets

\begin{split}\delta^{l}\|Z^{m+1}\check{L}^{i}\|_{s,u}\lesssim&\delta^{l_{1}}\|Z^{m_{1}}\check{L}^{i}\|_{s,u}+s\delta^{l_{1}}\|\not{\mathcal{L}}_{Z}^{m_{1}}\check{\chi}\|_{s,u}+\delta s^{-1}\delta^{l_{0}}\|Z^{m_{0}}\mu\|_{s,u}\\
&+\delta^{k(1-\varepsilon_{0})}s^{-2}\ln s\delta^{l_{0}}\|Z^{m_{0}}x\|_{s,u}+\delta^{k(1-\varepsilon_{0})}s^{-1}\ln s\delta^{l_{1}}\|\not{\mathcal{L}}_{Z}^{m_{1}}\not{g}\|_{s,u}\\
&+\delta^{(k-1)(1-\varepsilon_{0})}s^{-(k-1)/2}\delta^{l_{0}}\|Z^{m_{0}}\varphi_{\gamma}\|_{s,u},\end{split}

(3.109)

here
l
and
l_{p}
(
p=0,1
) are the numbers of
T
in
Z^{m+1}
and
Z^{m_{p}}
respectively with
1\leq m_{p}\leq m+1-p
.
Note that
|\check{L}^{i}|\lesssim\delta^{k(1-\varepsilon_{0})}s^{-1}\ln s
by (
2.56
).
Together with
\|1\|_{s,u}\lesssim\delta^{1/2}\varrho(s,u)^{1/2}
, this yields

\|\check{L}^{i}\|_{s,u}\lesssim\delta^{k(1-\varepsilon_{0})+1/2}s^{-1/2}\ln s
. Thus, with the help of (
3.107
),

\begin{split}\delta^{l}\|Z^{m+1}\check{L}^{i}\|_{s,u}\lesssim&\delta^{k(1-\varepsilon_{0})+1/2}s^{-1/2}\ln s+s\delta^{l_{1}}\|\not{\mathcal{L}}_{Z}^{m_{1}}\check{\chi}\|_{s,u}+\delta s^{-1}\delta^{l_{0}}\|Z^{m_{0}}\mu\|_{s,u}\\
&+\delta^{k(1-\varepsilon_{0})}s^{-2}\ln s\delta^{l_{0}}\|Z^{m_{0}}x\|_{s,u}+\delta^{k(1-\varepsilon_{0})}s^{-1}\ln s\delta^{l_{1}}\|\not{\mathcal{L}}_{Z}^{m_{1}}\not{g}\|_{s,u}\\
&+\delta^{k(1-\varepsilon_{0})+\varepsilon_{0}}s^{-(k-1)/2}\Big\{s^{-\iota}\sqrt{E_{1,\leq m+2}(s,u)}+\sqrt{E_{2,\leq m+2}(s,u)}\Big\}.\end{split}

(3.110)

Similarly, due to
\varrho Lx^{i}=\varrho\check{L}^{i}+x^{i}
,
Tx^{i}=\mu(-g^{0i}-\check{L}^{i}-\frac{x^{i}}{\varrho})
and
Rx^{a}=\Omega^{a}+\upsilon(g^{0a}+\check{L}^{a}+\frac{x^{a}}{\varrho})
, then

\begin{split}\delta^{l}\|Z^{m+2}x&\|_{s,u}\lesssim\delta^{\frac{1}{2}}s^{\frac{3}{2}}+\delta^{l_{0}}\|Z^{m_{0}}\upsilon\|_{s,u}+s\sum_{j=1}^{2}\delta^{l_{0}}\|Z^{m_{0}}\check{L}^{j}\|_{s,u}+\delta\delta^{l_{0}}\|Z^{m_{0}}\mu\|_{s,u}\\
&+\delta^{k(1-\varepsilon_{0})+\varepsilon_{0}+1}s^{-(k-1)/2}\ln s\Big\{s^{-\iota}\sqrt{E_{1,\leq m+2}(s,u)}+\sqrt{E_{2,\leq m+2}(s,u)}\Big\}.\end{split}

(3.111)

And it follows from
\not{\mathcal{L}}_{\varrho\mathring{L}}R=\varrho{\vphantom{\not{\pi}}}^{(R)}\not{\pi}_{\mathring{L}}
,
\not{\mathcal{L}}_{T}R={\vphantom{\not{\pi}}}^{(R)}\not{\pi}_{T}
and (
A.4
) in Appendix A that

\begin{split}\delta^{l}\|\not{\mathcal{L}}_{Z}^{m+1}R&\|_{s,u}\lesssim\delta^{k(1-\varepsilon_{0})+1/2}s^{1/2}\ln s+\delta^{k(1-\varepsilon_{0})}s^{-1}\ln s\delta^{l_{0}}\|Z^{m_{0}}x\|_{s,u}\\
&+\delta^{k(1-\varepsilon_{0})}s^{-1}\delta^{l_{1}}\|Z^{m_{1}}\upsilon\|_{s,u}+s^{2}\delta^{l_{1}}\|\not{\mathcal{L}}_{Z}^{m_{1}}\check{\chi}\|_{s,u}+\sum_{j=1}^{2}s\delta^{l_{0}}\|Z^{m_{1}}\check{L}^{j}\|_{s,u}\\
&+\delta^{k(1-\varepsilon_{0})}\ln s\delta^{l_{1}}\|\not{\mathcal{L}}_{Z}^{m_{1}}\not{g}\|_{s,u}+\delta^{k(1-\varepsilon_{0})+1}s^{-1}\ln s\delta^{l_{0}}\|Z^{m_{0}}\mu\|_{s,u}\\
&+\delta^{k(1-\varepsilon_{0})+\varepsilon_{0}}s^{-(k-3)/2}\Big\{s^{-\iota}\sqrt{E_{1,\leq m+2}(s,u)}+\sqrt{E_{2,\leq m+2}(s,u)}\Big\}.\end{split}

(3.112)

Additionally, by (

), (

) and the facts that

and

\begin{split}\delta^{l}\|Z^{m+1}\upsilon\|_{s,u}\lesssim&\delta^{k(1-\varepsilon_{0})}s^{-1}\ln s\delta^{l_{0}}\|Z^{k_{0}}x\|_{s,u}+s\delta^{l_{0}}\|Z^{m_{0}}\check{L}^{1}\|_{s,u}+s\delta^{l_{0}}\|Z^{m_{0}}\check{L}^{2}\|_{s,u}\\
&+\delta^{k(1-\varepsilon_{0})+\varepsilon_{0}}s^{-(k-3)/2}\Big\{s^{-\iota}\sqrt{E_{1,\leq m+2}(s,u)}+\sqrt{E_{2,\leq m+2}(s,u)}\Big\},\qquad\end{split}

(3.113)

\begin{split}\delta^{l}\|Z^{m+1}\check{\varrho}\|_{s,u}\lesssim&\delta^{2k(1-\varepsilon_{0})+1/2}s^{-3/2}\ln^{2}s+\delta^{k(1-\varepsilon_{0})}s^{-2}\ln s\delta^{l_{0}}\|Z^{k_{0}}x\|_{s,u}+\delta^{l_{0}}\|Z^{m_{0}}\check{L}^{1}\|_{s,u}\\

(3.114)

\displaystyle+\delta^{k(1-\varepsilon_{0})+\varepsilon_{0}}s^{-(k-1)/2}\Big\{s^{-\iota}\sqrt{E_{1,\leq m+2}(s,u)}+\sqrt{E_{2,\leq m+2}(s,u)}\Big\},

(3.126)

On the other hand, for the solution

to (

\begin{split}&\delta^{l}\|Z^{m+1}\phi\|_{s,u}\lesssim\delta^{1+l}\|[T,Z^{m+1}]\phi\|_{s,u}+\delta^{1+l}\|Z^{m+1}(\mu\tilde{T}^{i}\varphi_{i})\|_{s,u}\\

which means

\begin{split}\delta^{l}\|Z^{m+1}\phi\|_{s,u}\lesssim&\delta^{5/2-\varepsilon_{0}}+\delta^{2-\varepsilon_{0}}s^{1/2}\delta^{l_{1}}\|\not{\mathcal{L}}_{Z}^{m_{1}}\check{\chi}\|_{s,u}+\delta^{2-\varepsilon_{0}}s^{-1/2}\delta^{l_{0}}\|Z^{m_{0}}\mu\|_{s,u}\\
&+\delta^{2}\Big\{s^{-\iota}\sqrt{E_{1,\leq m+2}(s,u)}+\sqrt{E_{2,\leq m+2}(s,u)}\Big\}.\end{split}

(3.128)

Note that all the terms in the left hand side of (

)-(

and

\displaystyle+\delta^{(2k-1)(1-\varepsilon_{0})})s^{-(k-1)/2}\Big\{s^{-\iota}\sqrt{E_{1,\leq m+2}(s,u)}+\sqrt{E_{2,\leq m+2}(s,u)}\Big\},

\displaystyle\|R^{m+1}\phi\|_{s,u}\lesssim

\displaystyle\delta^{5/2-\varepsilon_{0}}+\delta^{2-\varepsilon_{0}}s^{1/2}\|\not{\mathcal{L}}_{R}^{m_{1}}\check{\chi}\|_{s,u}+\delta^{2-\varepsilon_{0}}s^{-1/2}\|R^{m_{0}}\mu\|_{s,u}

\displaystyle+\delta^{2}\Big\{s^{-\iota}\sqrt{E_{1,\leq m+2}(s,u)}+\sqrt{E_{2,\leq m+2}(s,u)}\Big\},

\displaystyle\|R^{m}\mathring{L}\phi\|_{s,u}\lesssim

\displaystyle\delta^{5/2-\varepsilon_{0}}s^{-1}+(\delta^{2-\varepsilon_{0}}+\delta^{(k+1)(1-\varepsilon_{0})})s^{-1/2}\|\not{\mathcal{L}}_{R}^{m_{1}}\check{\chi}\|_{s,u}+\delta^{2-\varepsilon_{0}}s^{-3/2}\|R^{m_{0}}\mu\|_{s,u}

\displaystyle+\delta^{2}\Big\{s^{-1-\iota}\sqrt{E_{1,\leq m+2}(s,u)}+\sqrt{E_{2,\leq m+2}(s,u)}\Big\}.

We now estimate the
L^{2}-
norm of
\mathring{L}R^{m+1}\mu
.
Note that it follows from (
2.68
), (
2.64
) and (
2.48
) that

\begin{split}\mathring{L}(\varrho^{2}\text{tr}\check{\chi})=&-\varrho^{2}G_{X\mathring{L}}^{\gamma}(\not{d}^{X}\mathring{L}\varphi_{\gamma})+\frac{1}{2}\varrho^{2}G_{\mathring{L}\mathring{L}}^{\gamma}\not{\triangle}\varphi_{\gamma}+\frac{1}{2}\varrho^{2}\not{g}^{XX}G_{XX}^{\gamma}\mathring{L}^{2}\varphi_{\gamma}-\varrho^{2}(\text{tr}\check{\chi})^{2}\\
&+\Big(-\frac{1}{2}G_{\mathring{L}\mathring{L}}^{\gamma}\mathring{L}\varphi_{\gamma}-G_{\tilde{T}\mathring{L}}^{\gamma}\mathring{L}\varphi_{\gamma}+G_{X\mathring{L}}^{\gamma}\not{d}^{X}\varphi_{\gamma}\Big)(\varrho^{2}\text{tr}\check{\chi}+\varrho)+\cdots,\end{split}

(3.130)

where

\displaystyle G_{\mathring{L}\mathring{L}}^{\gamma}\not{\triangle}\varphi_{\gamma}=\mu^{-1}G_{\mathring{L}\mathring{L}}^{\gamma}\Big(L(\mu\mathring{L}\varphi_{\gamma})+\frac{1}{2\varrho}\mu\mathring{L}\varphi_{\gamma}+2LT\varphi_{\gamma}+\frac{1}{\varrho}T\varphi_{\gamma}-H_{\gamma}\Big)

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\mu^{-1}G_{\mathring{L}\mathring{L}}^{\gamma}\Big(L(\mu\mathring{L}\varphi_{\gamma})+\frac{1}{2\varrho}\mu\mathring{L}\varphi_{\gamma}-H_{\gamma}\Big)+2\mu^{-1}\big\{G_{\mathring{L}\mathring{L}}^{0}L(\mu\tilde{T}^{i}\mathring{L}\varphi_{i})+G_{\mathring{L}\mathring{L}}^{\gamma}L(\mu\tilde{T}^{i}g_{\gamma j}\not{d}^{X}x^{j}\not{d}_{X}\varphi_{i})

\displaystyle-G_{\mathring{L}\mathring{L}}^{\gamma}(\mathring{L}\mathring{L}_{\gamma})\tilde{T}^{i}T\varphi_{i}-(G_{\mathring{L}\mathring{L}}^{\gamma}\mathring{L}_{\gamma})(L\tilde{T}^{i})T\varphi_{i}\underline{-(G_{\mathring{L}\mathring{L}}^{\gamma}\mathring{L}_{\gamma})\tilde{T}^{i}(\mathring{L}T\varphi_{i})}+\frac{1}{2\varrho}\big(\mu G_{\mathring{L}\mathring{L}}^{0}\tilde{T}\mathring{L}\varphi_{i}

\displaystyle+\mu G_{\mathring{L}\mathring{L}}^{\gamma}\tilde{T}^{i}g_{\gamma j}\not{d}^{X}x^{j}\not{d}_{X}\varphi_{i}\underline{-(G_{\mathring{L}\mathring{L}}^{\gamma}\mathring{L}_{\gamma})\tilde{T}^{i}T\varphi_{i}}\big)\big\}.

Applying (
2.64
) again for the above two underline terms yields

\displaystyle G_{\mathring{L}\mathring{L}}^{\gamma}\not{\triangle}\varphi_{\gamma}=

\displaystyle-(G_{\mathring{L}\mathring{L}}^{\gamma}\mathring{L}_{\gamma})\tilde{T}^{i}\not{\triangle}\varphi_{i}+G_{\mathring{L}\mathring{L}}^{\gamma}\mathring{L}^{2}\varphi_{\gamma}+2G_{\mathring{L}\mathring{L}}^{0}\tilde{T}^{i}(\mathring{L}^{2}\varphi_{i})

\displaystyle+2G_{\mathring{L}\mathring{L}}^{\gamma}\tilde{T}^{i}g_{\gamma j}\not{d}^{X}x^{j}(\not{d}_{X}\mathring{L}\varphi_{i})-(G_{\mathring{L}\mathring{L}}^{\gamma}\mathring{L}_{\gamma})\tilde{T}^{i}(\mathring{L}^{2}\varphi_{i})

\displaystyle+\mu^{-1}f_{1}(\varphi,\not{d}x,\mathring{L}^{1},\mathring{L}^{2})\left(\begin{array}[]{ccc}G_{\mathring{L}\mathring{L}}^{\gamma}\mathring{L}_{\gamma}\\
\varphi^{k-1}\\
\end{array}\right)\left(\begin{array}[]{ccc}\mathring{L}\varphi\\
\not{d}\varphi\\
\end{array}\right)\left(\begin{array}[]{ccc}\varphi^{k-1}T\varphi\\
\mu\mathring{L}\varphi\\
\mu\not{d}\varphi\\
\mu\varrho^{-1}\end{array}\right)

\displaystyle+\mu^{-1}(\text{tr}\check{\chi})f_{2}(\varphi,\not{d}x,\mathring{L}^{1},\mathring{L}^{2})\left(\begin{array}[]{ccc}(G_{\mathring{L}\mathring{L}}^{\gamma}\mathring{L}_{\gamma})T\varphi\\
\mu\varphi^{k-1}\mathring{L}\varphi\\
\mu\varphi^{k-1}\not{d}\varphi\end{array}\right).

Together with (
2.61
) and Proposition
2.2
, by the analogous proof of (8.43) in
[
10
]
, this deduces

\displaystyle\|R^{m}(\varrho^{2}\mu G_{\mathring{L}\mathring{L}}^{\gamma}\not{\triangle}\varphi_{\gamma})\|_{s,u}+\|R^{m}(\varrho^{2}\mu G_{X\mathring{L}}^{\gamma}\not{d}^{X}\mathring{L}\varphi_{\gamma})\|_{s,u}+\|R^{m}(\varrho^{2}\mu\not{g}^{XX}G_{XX}^{\gamma}\mathring{L}^{2}\varphi_{\gamma})\|_{s,u}

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle\delta^{k(1-\varepsilon_{0})}\|\not{d}R^{\leq m+1}\varphi\|_{s,u}+\delta^{(k-1)(1-\varepsilon_{0})}s^{1/2}\big(s\|\mathring{L}R^{\leq m}\mathring{L}\varphi\|_{s,u}+\|\mathring{L}R^{\leq m+1}\varphi\|_{s,u}\big)

\displaystyle+\delta^{k(1-\varepsilon_{0})}s^{-1}\big(\|R^{\leq m}\mu\|_{s,u}+\|\not{\mathcal{L}}_{R}^{\leq m}{\vphantom{\not{\pi}}}{}^{(R)}\not{\pi}_{\mathring{L}}\|_{s,u}\big)+\delta^{k(1-\varepsilon_{0})-\varepsilon_{0}}s^{1/2}\|R^{\leq m}\text{tr}\check{\chi}\|_{s,u}

(3.131)

\displaystyle+(1+\delta^{k(1-\varepsilon_{0})-1}\ln s)\Big\{\delta^{k(1-\varepsilon_{0})}s^{-1}\big(\sum_{i=1}^{2}\|R^{\leq m}\check{L}^{i}\|_{s,u}+\|R^{\leq m}\check{\varrho}\|_{s,u}+\|\not{\mathcal{L}}_{R}^{\leq m-1}{\vphantom{\not{\pi}}}{}^{(R)}\not{\pi}\|_{s,u}

\displaystyle+s^{-1}\|R^{\leq m}\upsilon\|_{s,u}\big)+\delta^{(k-1)(1-\varepsilon_{0})}s^{-3/2}\big(s\|R^{\leq m}\mathring{L}\phi\|_{s,u}+\|R^{\leq m+1}\phi\|_{s,u}\big)\Big\}

\displaystyle+\delta^{k(1-\varepsilon_{0})+\varepsilon_{0}}(1+\delta^{k(1-\varepsilon_{0})-1})s^{-1/2}(s^{-\iota}\sqrt{E_{1,\leq m+2}(s,u)}+\sqrt{E_{2,\leq m+2}(s,u)}).

Collecting (
3.130
), (
3.131
) and (
3.129
), we have

\begin{split}&\|LR^{m}(\mu\varrho^{2}\text{tr}\check{\chi})\|_{s,u}\\
\lesssim&\delta^{k(1-\varepsilon_{0})+1/2}s^{-1/2}+\delta^{(k-1)(1-\varepsilon_{0})}s^{1/2}\big(s\|\mathring{L}R^{\leq m}\mathring{L}\varphi\|_{s,u}+\|\mathring{L}R^{\leq m+1}\varphi\|_{s,u})\\
&+\delta^{k(1-\varepsilon_{0})}s^{-\iota}\sqrt{E_{1,\leq m+2}(s,u)}+\delta^{k(1-\varepsilon_{0})+\varepsilon_{0}}(1+\delta^{k(1-\varepsilon_{0})-1})s^{-1/2}\sqrt{E_{2,\leq m+2}(s,u)}\\
&+\delta^{k(1-\varepsilon_{0})-\varepsilon_{0}}s^{1/2}\|R^{\leq m}(\mu\text{tr}\check{\chi})\|_{s,u}+\delta^{k(1-\varepsilon_{0})}s^{-1}\|R^{\leq m}\mu\|_{s,u}\\
&+\delta^{k(1-\varepsilon_{0})+1}(1+\delta^{k(1-\varepsilon_{0})-1})s^{-2}\ln s\|R^{\leq m+1}\mu\|_{s,u}.\end{split}

(3.132)

On the other hand, by virtue of (
2.36
) and the commutation of vector fields, one has

\displaystyle\delta^{l}\|\mathring{L}R^{m+1}\mu\|_{s,u}

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle\delta^{k(1-\varepsilon_{0})-1}s^{-1}\big(\|\not{\mathcal{L}}_{R}^{\leq m}{\vphantom{\not{\pi}}}{}^{(R)}\not{\pi}_{\mathring{L}}\|_{s,u}+\|R^{m+1}\check{L}^{1}\|_{s,u}+\|R^{m+1}\check{L}^{2}\|_{s,u}+s^{-1}\|R^{\leq m+1}\upsilon\|_{s,u}\big)

\displaystyle+\delta^{2k(1-\varepsilon_{0})-\varepsilon_{0}}s^{-2}\ln s\|\not{\mathcal{L}}_{R}^{\leq m-1}{\vphantom{\not{\pi}}}{}^{(R)}\not{\pi}\|_{s,u}+\delta^{k(1-\varepsilon_{0})}s^{-2}\ln s\|R^{\leq m+1}\mu\|_{s,u}

\displaystyle+\delta^{(k-1)(1-\varepsilon_{0})-1}s^{-1/2}\big(\|R^{\leq m+1}\varphi\|_{s,u}+\delta\|TR^{\leq m+1}\varphi\|_{s,u}+\delta\|\not{d}R^{\leq m}\mathring{L}\varphi\|_{s,u}\big)

\displaystyle+\delta^{k(1-\varepsilon_{0})}s^{-2}\|\not{\mathcal{L}}_{R}^{\leq m}{\vphantom{\not{\pi}}}{}^{(R)}\not{\pi}_{T}\|_{s,u}

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle\delta^{2k(1-\varepsilon_{0})-1/2}s^{-3/2}\ln s+\delta^{k(1-\varepsilon_{0})-1}\|R^{\leq m}(\mu\text{tr}\check{\chi})\|_{s,u}

\displaystyle+\delta^{(k-1)(1-\varepsilon_{0})}s^{-1/2}\Big\{s^{-\iota}\sqrt{E_{1,\leq m+2}(s,u)}+\sqrt{E_{2,\leq m+2}(s,u)}\Big\}

(3.133)

\displaystyle+\delta^{k(1-\varepsilon_{0})}s^{-2}\ln s\|R^{\leq m+1}\mu\|_{s,u}+\delta^{2k(1-\varepsilon_{0})-1}s^{-2}\ln s\|R^{\leq m}\mu\|_{s,u}.

Set
F(s,u,\vartheta)=\varrho(s,u)^{2}({R}^{m}(\mu\textrm{tr}\check{\chi}))(s,u,\vartheta)-\varrho(t_{0},u)^{2}({R}^{m}(\mu\textrm{tr}\check{\chi}))(t_{0},u,\vartheta)
in (
3.108
).
Since for any 2-form
\xi
on
S_{s,u}
,
|\mathring{L}(\varrho^{2}\textrm{tr}\xi)|\lesssim\varrho^{2}|\not{\mathcal{L}}_{\mathring{L}}\xi|+\delta^{k(1-\varepsilon_{0})}s^{-2}\ln s(\varrho^{2}|\textrm{tr}\xi|)
holds. Then
applying (
3.132
) and Grownwall’s equality simultaneously yields

\displaystyle s^{3/2}\|{R}^{m}(\mu\textrm{tr}\check{\chi})\|_{s,u}

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle\delta^{k(1-\varepsilon_{0})+1/2}\ln s+\delta^{k(1-\varepsilon_{0})+1}(1+\delta^{k(1-\varepsilon_{0})-1})\int_{t_{0}}^{s}\tau^{-5/2}\ln\tau\|R^{\leq m+1}\mu\|_{\tau,u}d\tau

\displaystyle+\delta^{k(1-\varepsilon_{0})}s^{1/2-\iota}\sqrt{\tilde{E}_{1,\leq m+2}(s,u)}+\delta^{k(1-\varepsilon_{0})+\varepsilon_{0}}(1+\delta^{k(1-\varepsilon_{0})-1})\ln s\sqrt{\tilde{E}_{2,\leq m+2}(s,u)}

(3.134)

\displaystyle+\delta^{k(1-\varepsilon_{0})}\int_{t_{0}}^{s}\tau^{-3/2}\|R^{\leq m}\mu\|_{\tau,u}d\tau+\delta^{(k-1)(1-\varepsilon_{0})}s^{1/2}\sqrt{\int_{0}^{u}F_{1,\leq m+2}(s,u^{\prime})du^{\prime}}.

Analogously, (
3.133
) shows that

\begin{split}s^{-1/2}\|R^{m+1}\mu\|_{s,u}\lesssim&\delta^{k(1-\varepsilon_{0})-\varepsilon_{0}+1/2}+\delta^{k(1-\varepsilon_{0})-1}\int_{t_{0}}^{s}\tau^{-1/2}\|{R}^{\leq m}(\mu\textrm{tr}\check{\chi})\|_{\tau,u}d\tau\\
&+\delta^{2k(1-\varepsilon_{0})-1}\int_{t_{0}}^{s}\tau^{-5/2}\ln\tau\|R^{\leq m}\mu\|_{\tau,u}d\tau\\
&+\delta^{(k-1)(1-\varepsilon_{0})}\sqrt{\tilde{E}_{1,\leq m+2}(s,u)}+\delta^{(k-1)(1-\varepsilon_{0})}\ln s\sqrt{\tilde{E}_{2,\leq m+2}(s,u)}.\end{split}

(3.135)

When
m\leq N-1
, it follows from Proposition
2.2
that
|R^{m+1}\mu|\leq\delta^{k(1-\varepsilon_{0})-\varepsilon_{0}}
, which implies
\|R^{m+1}\mu\|_{s,u}\leq\delta^{k(1-\varepsilon_{0})-\varepsilon_{0}+1/2}s^{1/2}
. For
N\leq m\leq 2N-5
, one can make the following induction:

\begin{split}s^{-1/2}\|R^{m}\mu\|_{s,u}\lesssim&\delta^{k(1-\varepsilon_{0})-\varepsilon_{0}+1/2}+\delta^{(k-1)(1-\varepsilon_{0})}\sqrt{\tilde{E}_{1,\leq m+2}(s,u)}\\
+\delta^{(k-1)(1-\varepsilon_{0})}&\ln s\sqrt{\tilde{E}_{2,\leq m+2}(s,u)}+\delta^{(2k-1)(1-\varepsilon_{0})-1}\sqrt{\int_{0}^{u}F_{1,\leq m+2}(s,u^{\prime})du^{\prime}}.\end{split}

(3.136)

Substitute (
3.136
) into the right hands of (
3.134
) and (
3.135
) to obtain

\displaystyle s^{3/2}\|{R}^{m}(\mu\textrm{tr}\check{\chi})\|_{s,u}

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle\delta^{k(1-\varepsilon_{0})+1/2}\ln s+\delta^{k(1-\varepsilon_{0})+1}(1+\delta^{k(1-\varepsilon_{0})-1})\int_{t_{0}}^{s}\tau^{-5/2}\ln\tau\|R^{\leq m+1}\mu\|_{\tau,u}d\tau

\displaystyle+\delta^{k(1-\varepsilon_{0})}s^{1/2-\iota}\sqrt{\tilde{E}_{1,\leq m+2}(s,u)}+\delta^{k(1-\varepsilon_{0})+\varepsilon_{0}}(1+\delta^{k(1-\varepsilon_{0})-1})\ln^{2}s\sqrt{\tilde{E}_{2,\leq m+2}(s,u)}

\displaystyle+\delta^{(k-1)(1-\varepsilon_{0})}s^{1/2}\sqrt{\int_{0}^{u}\tilde{F}_{1,\leq m+2}(s,u^{\prime})du^{\prime}}

and

\displaystyle s^{-1/2}\|R^{m+1}\mu\|_{s,u}

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle\delta^{k(1-\varepsilon_{0})-\varepsilon_{0}+1/2}+\delta^{k(1-\varepsilon_{0})-1}\int_{t_{0}}^{s}\tau^{-1/2}\|{R}^{\leq m}(\mu\textrm{tr}\check{\chi})\|_{\tau,u}d\tau

\displaystyle+\delta^{(k-1)(1-\varepsilon_{0})}\Big(\sqrt{\tilde{E}_{1,\leq m+2}}+\ln s\sqrt{\tilde{E}_{2,\leq m+2}}\Big)+\delta^{(4k-1)(1-\varepsilon_{0})-2}\sqrt{\int_{0}^{u}\tilde{F}_{1,\leq m+2}(s,u^{\prime})du^{\prime}}.

And hence,

\displaystyle\|{R}^{m}(\mu\textrm{tr}\check{\chi})\|_{s,u}

\displaystyle\lesssim\delta^{k(1-\varepsilon_{0})+1/2}s^{-3/2}\ln s+\delta^{k(1-\varepsilon_{0})}s^{-1-\iota}\sqrt{\tilde{E}_{1,\leq m+2}(s,u)}

\displaystyle+\delta^{k(1-\varepsilon_{0})+\varepsilon_{0}}(1+\delta^{k(1-\varepsilon_{0})-1})s^{-3/2}\ln^{2}s\sqrt{\tilde{E}_{2,\leq m+2}(s,u)}

\displaystyle+\delta^{(k-1)(1-\varepsilon_{0})}s^{-1}\sqrt{\int_{0}^{u}\tilde{F}_{1,\leq m+2}(s,u^{\prime})du^{\prime}},

(3.137)

\displaystyle\|R^{m+1}\mu\|_{s,u}

\displaystyle\lesssim\delta^{k(1-\varepsilon_{0})-\varepsilon_{0}+1/2}s^{1/2}+\delta^{(k-1)(1-\varepsilon_{0})}s^{1/2}\Big\{\sqrt{\tilde{E}_{1,\leq m+2}}+\ln s\sqrt{\tilde{E}_{2,\leq m+2}}\Big\}

\displaystyle+\delta^{(2k-1)(1-\varepsilon_{0})-1}s^{1/2}\sqrt{\int_{0}^{u}\tilde{F}_{1,\leq m+2}(s,u^{\prime})du^{\prime}}.

(3.138)

Thus,

\|\not{\mathcal{L}}_{R}^{m}\check{\chi}\|_{s,u}\lesssim\delta^{k(1-\varepsilon_{0})+1/2}s^{-3/2}\ln s+s^{-1}\mathscr{E}_{m+2}(s,u)

(3.139)

and then (
3.136
) hold.

If
\not{\mathcal{L}}_{Z}^{m}\check{\chi}=\not{\mathcal{L}}_{Z}^{m_{1}}\not{\mathcal{L}}_{T}\not{\mathcal{L}}_{R}^{m_{2}}\check{\chi}
with
m_{1}+m_{2}=m-1
, then
\not{\mathcal{L}}_{Z}^{m}\check{\chi}=\not{\mathcal{L}}_{Z}^{m_{1}}[\not{\mathcal{L}}_{T},\not{\mathcal{L}}_{R}^{m_{2}}]\check{\chi}+\not{\mathcal{L}}_{Z}^{m_{1}}\not{\mathcal{L}}_{R}^{m_{2}}\not{\mathcal{L}}_{T}\check{\chi}
holds. Hence
it follows from Lemma
A.1
, (
2.69
) and (
3.116
)-(
3.126
) that

\begin{split}&\delta^{l}\|\not{\mathcal{L}}_{Z}^{m}\check{\chi}\|_{s,u}\\
\lesssim&\delta^{k(1-\varepsilon_{0})+1/2}s^{-3/2}+\delta^{k(1-\varepsilon_{0})}s^{-1}\delta^{l_{1}}\|\not{\mathcal{L}}_{Z}^{m_{1}}\check{\chi}\|_{s,u}+\delta s^{-2}\delta^{l_{0}}\|Z^{m_{0}}\mu\|_{s,u}\\
&+\delta^{k(1-\varepsilon_{0})+\varepsilon_{0}}s^{-(k+1)/2}\Big\{s^{-\iota}\sqrt{E_{1,\leq m+2}}+\sqrt{E_{2,\leq m+2}}\Big\}.\end{split}

(3.140)

If
\not{\mathcal{L}}_{Z}^{m}\check{\chi}=\not{\mathcal{L}}_{Z}^{m_{1}}\not{\mathcal{L}}_{\varrho\mathring{L}}\not{\mathcal{L}}_{R}^{m_{2}}\check{\chi}
with
m_{1}+m_{2}=m-1
, then
\not{\mathcal{L}}_{Z}^{m}\check{\chi}=\not{\mathcal{L}}_{Z}^{m_{1}}[\not{\mathcal{L}}_{\varrho\mathring{L}},\not{\mathcal{L}}_{R}^{m_{2}}]\check{\chi}+\not{\mathcal{L}}_{Z}^{m_{1}}\not{\mathcal{L}}_{R}^{m_{2}}(\varrho\not{\mathcal{L}}_{\mathring{L}}\check{\chi})
, and it follows from (
2.68
) that

\begin{split}&\delta^{l}\|\not{\mathcal{L}}_{Z}^{m}\check{\chi}\|_{s,u}\\
\lesssim&\delta^{k(1-\varepsilon_{0})+1/2}s^{-3/2}+\delta^{k(1-\varepsilon_{0})}s^{-1}\ln s\delta^{l_{1}}\|\not{\mathcal{L}}_{Z}^{m_{1}}\check{\chi}\|_{s,u}+\delta^{k(1-\varepsilon_{0})+1}s^{-3}\ln s\delta^{l_{0}}\|Z^{m_{0}}\mu\|_{s,u}\\
&+\delta^{k(1-\varepsilon_{0})+\varepsilon_{0}}s^{-(k+1)/2}\Big\{s^{-\iota}\sqrt{E_{1,\leq m+2}}+\sqrt{E_{2,\leq m+2}}\Big\}.\end{split}

(3.141)

Therefore, for any
Z\in\{\varrho\mathring{L},T,R\}
, by (
3.139
), (
3.140
) and (
3.141
), we obtain

\begin{split}\delta^{l}\|\not{\mathcal{L}}_{Z}^{m}\check{\chi}\|_{s,u}\lesssim&\delta^{k(1-\varepsilon_{0})+1/2}s^{-3/2}\ln s+\delta s^{-2}\delta^{l_{0}}\|Z^{m_{0}}\mu\|_{s,u}+s^{-1}\mathscr{E}_{m+2}(s,u).\end{split}

(3.142)

For
Z^{m+1}\mu=\bar{Z}^{m+1}\mu
with
\bar{Z}\in\{T,R\}
,
taking
F(s,u,\vartheta)=\delta^{l}\bar{Z}^{m+1}\mu(s,u,\vartheta)-\delta^{l}\bar{Z}^{m+1}\mu(t_{0},u,\vartheta)
in (
3.108
),
meanwhile using

\begin{split}&\delta^{l}\|\mathring{L}\bar{Z}^{m+1}\mu\|_{s,u}\\
\lesssim&\delta^{k(1-\varepsilon_{0})-\varepsilon_{0}}s^{-1}\delta^{l_{1}}\|\not{\mathcal{L}}_{\bar{Z}}^{m_{1}}{\vphantom{\not{\pi}}}{}^{(R)}\not{\pi}_{\mathring{L}}\|_{s,u}+\delta^{k(1-\varepsilon_{0})}s^{-2}\ln s\delta^{l_{0}}\|\bar{Z}^{m_{0}}\mu\|_{s,u}\\
&+\delta^{(k-2)(1-\varepsilon_{0})-\varepsilon_{0}}s^{-1/2}\delta^{l_{0}}\|\bar{Z}^{m_{0}}\varphi\|_{s,u}+\delta^{(k+1)(1-\varepsilon_{0})}s^{-1}\delta^{l_{1}}\|\not{\mathcal{L}}_{\bar{Z}}^{k_{1}}{\vphantom{\not{\pi}}}{}^{(T)}\not{\pi}_{\mathring{L}}\|_{s,u}\\
&+\delta^{k(1-\varepsilon_{0})-1}s^{-k/2}\delta^{l_{0}}\|\bar{Z}^{m_{0}}\check{L}^{i}\|_{s,u}+\delta^{k(1-\varepsilon_{0})}s^{-2}\delta^{l_{1}}\|\not{\mathcal{L}}_{\bar{Z}}^{m_{1}}{\vphantom{\not{\pi}}}{}^{(R)}\not{\pi}_{T}\|_{s,u}\\
&+\delta^{k(1-\varepsilon_{0})-1}s^{-k/2}\delta^{l_{0}}\|\not{\mathcal{L}}_{\bar{Z}}^{m_{0}}\not{d}x^{i}\|_{s,u}+\delta^{(k-1)(1-\varepsilon_{0})}s^{-(k-1)/2}\delta^{l_{0}}\|\mathring{L}\bar{Z}^{m_{0}}\varphi\|_{s,u}\\
&+\delta^{(k-1)(1-\varepsilon_{0})}s^{-1/2}\delta^{l_{0}}\|T\bar{Z}^{m_{0}}\varphi\|_{s,u}+\delta^{2k(1-\varepsilon_{0})-\varepsilon_{0}}s^{-2}\ln s\delta^{l_{1}}\|\not{\mathcal{L}}_{\bar{Z}}^{m_{1}}\not{g}\|_{s,u}\\
\lesssim&\delta^{k(1-\varepsilon_{0})-1/2}s^{-1/2}+\delta^{k(1-\varepsilon_{0})-1}\delta^{l_{1}}\|\not{\mathcal{L}}_{\bar{Z}}^{m_{1}}\check{\chi}\|_{s,u}+\delta^{k(1-\varepsilon_{0})}s^{-2}\ln s\delta^{l_{0}}\|\bar{Z}^{m_{0}}\mu\|_{s,u}\\
&+\delta^{(k-1)(1-\varepsilon_{0})}s^{-1/2}\Big\{s^{-\iota}\sqrt{E_{1,\leq m+2}(s,u)}+\sqrt{E_{2,\leq m+2}(s,u)}\Big\}\end{split}

and (
3.142
), one has

\begin{split}\delta^{l}\|\bar{Z}^{m+1}\mu\|_{s,u}\lesssim&\delta^{k(1-\varepsilon_{0})-1/2}s^{1/2}\ln s+\delta^{(k-1)(1-\varepsilon_{0})}s^{1/2}\Big\{\sqrt{\tilde{E}_{1,\leq m+2}}+\ln s\sqrt{\tilde{E}_{2,\leq m+2}}\Big\}\\
&+\delta^{(2k-1)(1-\varepsilon_{0})-1}s^{1/2}\sqrt{\int_{0}^{u}\tilde{F}_{1,\leq m+2}(s,u^{\prime})du^{\prime}}.\end{split}

(3.143)

If
Z^{m+1}\mu=Z^{m_{1}}(\varrho\mathring{L})\bar{Z}^{m_{2}}\mu
with
m_{1}+m_{2}=m
, with the help of (
2.36
),
then it holds that

\begin{split}&\delta^{l}\|Z^{m+1}\mu\|_{s,u}=\delta^{l}\|Z^{m_{1}}[\varrho\mathring{L},\bar{Z}^{m_{2}}]\mu+Z^{m_{1}}\bar{Z}^{m_{2}}(\varrho\mathring{L})\mu\|_{s,u}\\
\lesssim&\delta^{k(1-\varepsilon_{0})-1/2}s^{1/2}+\delta^{k(1-\varepsilon_{0})-1}s\delta^{l_{1}}\|\not{\mathcal{L}}_{Z}^{m_{1}}\check{\chi}\|_{s,u}+\delta^{k(1-\varepsilon_{0})}s^{-1}\ln s\delta^{l_{0}}\|Z^{m_{0}}\mu\|_{s,u}\\
&+\delta^{(k-1)(1-\varepsilon_{0})}\Big\{s^{-\iota}\sqrt{E_{1,\leq m+2}(s,u)}+\sqrt{E_{2,\leq m+2}(s,u)}\Big\}\\
\lesssim&\delta^{k(1-\varepsilon_{0})-1/2}s^{1/2}+\delta^{k(1-\varepsilon_{0})}s^{-1}\ln s\delta^{l_{0}}\|Z^{m_{0}}\mu\|_{s,u}\\
&+\delta^{(k-1)(1-\varepsilon_{0})}\Big\{s^{-\iota}\sqrt{E_{1,\leq m+2}(s,u)}+\sqrt{E_{2,\leq m+2}(s,u)}\Big\},\end{split}

(3.144)

where (
3.142
) is used in the last inequality.

Thus, for any
Z\in\{\varrho\mathring{L},T,R\}
, it follows from (
3.143
) and (
3.144
) that

\begin{split}\delta^{l}\|Z^{m+1}\mu\|_{s,u}\lesssim&\delta^{k(1-\varepsilon_{0})-1/2}s^{1/2}\ln s+\delta^{(k-1)(1-\varepsilon_{0})}s^{1/2}\Big\{\sqrt{\tilde{E}_{1,\leq m+2}}+\ln s\sqrt{\tilde{E}_{2,\leq m+2}}\Big\}\\
&+\delta^{(2k-1)(1-\varepsilon_{0})-1}s^{1/2}\sqrt{\int_{0}^{u}\tilde{F}_{1,\leq m+2}(s,u^{\prime})du^{\prime}},\end{split}

(3.145)

which, together with (
3.142
), implies that

\begin{split}\delta^{l}\|\not{\mathcal{L}}_{Z}^{m}\check{\chi}\|_{s,u}\lesssim&\delta^{k(1-\varepsilon_{0})+1/2}s^{-3/2}\ln s+s^{-1}\mathscr{E}_{m+2}(s,u).\end{split}

(3.146)

Substituting (
3.145
)-(
3.146
) into (
3.116
)-(
3.126
)
yields the proof of Proposition
3.1
.
∎

It follows from the
L^{\infty}
estimates in Subsection
2.2
that
|Z^{m+1}\mu|\lesssim\delta^{k(1-\varepsilon_{0})-\varepsilon_{0}-l}
holds
when
m\leq N-3
, which implies
\delta^{l}\|Z^{m+1}\mu\|_{s,u}\lesssim\delta^{k(1-\varepsilon_{0})-\varepsilon_{0}+1/2}s^{1/2}

(m\leq N-3)
due to
\|1\|_{s,u}\lesssim\delta^{1/2}s^{1/2}
.
Note that the upper bound
\delta^{k(1-\varepsilon_{0})-1/2-l}s^{1/2}\ln s
of the
L^{2}
norm of
Z^{m+1}\mu
for
m\leq 2N-6

(see Proposition
3.1
)
is much worse than
\delta^{k(1-\varepsilon_{0})-\varepsilon_{0}+1/2-l}s^{1/2}
.
This leads to some essential difficulties for obtaining the global uniform estimates of
\partial\phi
in
A_{2\delta}
when
\varepsilon_{0}
is near
\varepsilon_{k}^{*}
.
Therefore, one needs to improve the
L^{2}
norm of
Z^{m+1}\mu
for
N-2\leq m\leq 2N-6
,
which will be achieved by studying the transport equation (
2.36
) carefully with special attention to the term
\frac{1}{2}G_{\mathring{L}\mathring{L}}^{\gamma}T\varphi_{\gamma}
in (
2.36
). According to (
2.60
) and (
2.61
), one may estimate
\|Z^{m+1}\mathring{L}\phi\|_{s,u}
and
\|\not{\mathcal{L}}_{Z}^{m+1}\not{d}\phi\|_{s,u}
, and even further obtain better estimates on the
L^{2}
norm
of
Z^{m+1}\mathring{L}\mu
. It is emphasized here that one cannot treat
\mathring{L}\phi
simply as
\mathring{L}^{\alpha}\varphi_{\alpha}
,
otherwise, the better smallness and time-decay rate of
\mathring{L}\phi
will be lost. The same caution applies to
\not{d}\phi
.

We now apply (
3.105
) to establish the estimates of
\|Z^{m+1}\mathring{L}\phi\|_{s,u}
and
\|\not{\mathcal{L}}_{Z}^{m+1}\not{d}\phi\|_{s,u}
.

Lemma 3.3
.

Under the assumption
(\star)
, when
\delta>0
is small, it holds that for
m\leq 2N-6
,

\begin{split}\delta^{l}\|\bar{Z}^{m+1}\mathring{L}\phi\|_{s,u}&\lesssim\delta^{5/2-\varepsilon_{0}}s^{-1}+\delta s^{-\iota}\sqrt{\tilde{E}_{1,\leq m+2}(s,u)}+\delta^{2}s^{-1}\sqrt{\tilde{E}_{2,\leq m+2}(s,u)}\\
&+(\delta^{3-\varepsilon_{0}}+\delta^{(k+1)(1-\varepsilon_{0})+1})s^{-1/2}\|R^{m}\not{\triangle}\mu\|_{s,u}+\delta^{2-\varepsilon_{0}}s^{-3/2}\delta^{l^{\prime}}\|\bar{Z}^{m^{\prime}+1}\mu\|_{s,u}\\
&+\delta^{k(1-\varepsilon_{0})}(\delta+\delta^{k(1-\varepsilon_{0})})s^{-3/2}\sqrt{\int_{0}^{u}\tilde{F}_{1}(s,u^{\prime})du^{\prime}},\end{split}

(3.147)

\begin{split}\delta^{l}\|\not{\mathcal{L}}_{\bar{Z}}^{m+1}\not{d}\phi\|_{s,u}\lesssim&\delta^{5/2-\varepsilon_{0}}s^{-1}+\delta s^{-\iota}\sqrt{\tilde{E}_{1,\leq m+2}(s,u)}+\delta^{2}s^{-1}\sqrt{\tilde{E}_{2,\leq m+2}(s,u)}\\
&+\delta^{2-\varepsilon_{0}}s^{1/2}\|R^{m}\not{\triangle}\mu\|_{s,u}+\delta^{2-\varepsilon_{0}}s^{-3/2}\delta^{l^{\prime}}\|\bar{Z}^{m^{\prime}+1}\mu\|_{s,u},\qquad\qquad\qquad\qquad\\
&+\delta^{k(1-\varepsilon_{0})+1}s^{-3/2}\sqrt{\int_{0}^{u}\tilde{F}_{1}(s,u^{\prime})du^{\prime}}\end{split}

(3.148)

\begin{split}\delta^{l}\|\bar{Z}^{m}\mathring{L}^{2}\phi\|_{s,u}&\lesssim\delta^{5/2-\varepsilon_{0}}s^{-2}+\delta s^{-1-\iota}\sqrt{\tilde{E}_{1,\leq m+2}(s,u)}+\delta^{2}s^{-2}\sqrt{\tilde{E}_{2,\leq m+2}(s,u)}\\
+&\delta^{(k+1)(1-\varepsilon_{0})+1}(\delta+\delta^{k(1-\varepsilon_{0})})s^{-5/2}\|R^{m}\not{\triangle}\mu\|_{s,u}+\delta^{2-\varepsilon_{0}}s^{-5/2}\delta^{l^{\prime}}\|\bar{Z}^{m^{\prime}+1}\mu\|_{s,u}\\
&+\delta^{k(1-\varepsilon_{0})}(\delta+\delta^{k(1-\varepsilon_{0})})s^{-5/2}\sqrt{\int_{0}^{u}\tilde{F}_{1}(s,u^{\prime})du^{\prime}},\end{split}

(3.149)

where
\bar{Z}\in\{T,R\}
, the numbers of
T
in
\bar{Z}^{m+1}
and
\bar{Z}^{m^{\prime}+1}
are
l
and
l^{\prime}
respectively,
m^{\prime}\leq m
.

Proof.

For
\bar{Z}^{m+1}=R^{m+1}
, due to (
3.105
), one has

\begin{split}&\|R^{m+1}\mathring{L}\phi\|_{s,u}\lesssim\delta\|TR^{m+1}\mathring{L}\phi\|_{s,u}\\
\lesssim&\delta\|R^{m+1}T(\mathring{L}^{\alpha}\varphi_{\alpha})\|_{s,u}+\delta^{3-\varepsilon_{0}}s^{-5/2}\|\not{\mathcal{L}}_{R}^{m_{1}}{\vphantom{\not{\pi}}}{}^{(R)}\not{\pi}_{T}\|_{s,u}+\delta s^{-1}\|R^{m_{0}}\mathring{L}\phi\|_{s,u},\end{split}

(3.150)

where
T(\mathring{L}^{\alpha}\varphi_{\alpha})=(T\mathring{L}^{i})\varphi_{i}+\mu\tilde{T}^{i}(\mathring{L}\varphi_{i})
.
Recall that
T\mathring{L}^{i}
satisfies (
2.43
). Then

T(\mathring{L}^{\alpha}\varphi_{\alpha})=\mu\tilde{T}^{i}(\mathring{L}\varphi_{i})+(\not{d}_{X}\mu)\not{d}^{X}\phi-(G_{X\mathring{L}}^{\gamma}T\varphi_{\gamma})\not{d}^{X}\phi+\frac{1}{2}(G_{\mathring{L}\mathring{L}}^{\gamma}T\varphi_{\gamma})\tilde{T}^{i}\varphi_{i}+\dots.

(3.151)

With the help of (
2.60
) and (
2.61
), after substituting (
3.151
) into (
3.150
) and absorbing all
\|R^{m_{0}}\mathring{L}\phi\|_{s,u}
in the right hand side by
\sum_{m\leq 2N-6}\|R^{m+1}\mathring{L}\phi\|_{s,u}
in (
3.150
), one obtains

\displaystyle\|R^{m+1}\mathring{L}\phi\|_{s,u}

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle(\delta^{2-\varepsilon_{0}}+\delta^{(k+1)(1-\varepsilon_{0})})s^{-3/2}\big(\|R^{m_{0}}\check{L}^{1}\|_{s,u}+\|R^{m_{0}}\check{L}^{2}\|_{s,u}\big)+\delta^{(k+1)(1-\varepsilon_{0})}s^{-3/2}\|R^{m_{0}}\check{\varrho}\|_{s,u}

\displaystyle+\delta^{2-\varepsilon_{0}}s^{-5/2}\|R^{m_{-1}}x\|_{s,u}+\delta^{(k+1)(1-\varepsilon_{0})+1}s^{-3/2}\|TR^{m_{0}}\varphi\|_{s,u}

\displaystyle+\delta^{k(1-\varepsilon_{0})+2}s^{-2}\|\not{d}R^{m_{0}}\varphi\|_{s,u}+\delta^{2-\varepsilon_{0}}s^{-3/2}\|\not{\mathcal{L}}_{R}^{m_{1}}{\vphantom{\not{\pi}}}{}^{(R)}\not{\pi}_{\mathring{L}}\|_{s,u}+\delta s^{-1}\|R^{m_{0}}\varphi\|_{s,u}

(3.152)

\displaystyle+\delta\|\mathring{L}R^{m_{0}}\varphi\|_{s,u}+\delta^{3-\varepsilon_{0}}s^{-5/2}\|\not{\mathcal{L}}_{R}^{m_{1}}{\vphantom{\not{\pi}}}{}^{(R)}\not{\pi}_{T}\|_{s,u}+\delta^{k(1-\varepsilon_{0})}s^{-1}\|\not{d}R^{m_{0}}\phi\|_{s,u}

\displaystyle+\delta^{3-\varepsilon_{0}}s^{-3/2}\|\not{d}R^{m+1}\mu\|_{s,u}+\delta^{2-\varepsilon_{0}}s^{-3/2}\|R^{m^{\prime}+1}\mu\|_{s,u}.

where
1\leq m_{p}\leq m+1-p
with
p=-1,0,1
. Using Proposition
3.1
to estimate the second to fourth lines in (
3.152
)
yields

\begin{split}\|R^{m+1}\mathring{L}&\phi\|_{s,u}\lesssim\delta^{5/2-\varepsilon_{0}}s^{-1}+\delta s^{-\iota}\sqrt{\tilde{E}_{1,\leq m+2}(s,u)}+\delta^{2}s^{-1}\sqrt{\tilde{E}_{2,\leq m+2}(s,u)}\\
&+\delta^{k(1-\varepsilon_{0})}s^{-1}\|\not{d}R^{m_{0}}\phi\|_{s,u}+\delta^{3-\varepsilon_{0}}s^{-3/2}\|\not{d}R^{m+1}\mu\|_{s,u}\\
&+\delta^{2-\varepsilon_{0}}s^{-3/2}\|R^{m^{\prime}+1}\mu\|_{s,u}+\delta^{k(1-\varepsilon_{0})}(\delta+\delta^{k(1-\varepsilon_{0})})s^{-3/2}\sqrt{\int_{0}^{u}\tilde{F}_{1}(s,u^{\prime})du^{\prime}}.\end{split}

(3.153)

If
\bar{Z}^{m+1}=\bar{Z}^{p_{1}}TR^{p_{2}}
with
p_{1}+p_{2}=m
, then
\bar{Z}^{m+1}\mathring{L}\phi=\bar{Z}^{p_{1}}R^{p_{2}}T(\mathring{L}^{\alpha}\varphi_{\alpha})+\bar{Z}^{p_{1}}[T,R^{p_{2}}]\mathring{L}\phi
. Using (
3.151
) again, one can get similarly that

\begin{split}\delta^{l}\|\bar{Z}^{m+1}\mathring{L}\phi\|_{s,u}\lesssim&\delta^{5/2-\varepsilon_{0}}s^{-1}+\delta s^{-\iota}\sqrt{\tilde{E}_{1,\leq m+2}(s,u)}+\delta^{2}s^{-1}\sqrt{\tilde{E}_{2,\leq m+2}(s,u)}\\
+\delta^{k(1-\varepsilon_{0})}&s^{-1}\delta^{l_{0}}\|\not{d}\bar{Z}^{m_{0}}\phi\|_{s,u}+\delta^{2-\varepsilon_{0}}s^{-3/2}\delta^{l^{\prime}}\|\bar{Z}^{m^{\prime}+1}\mu\|_{s,u}\\
&+\delta^{k(1-\varepsilon_{0})}(\delta+\delta^{k(1-\varepsilon_{0})})s^{-3/2}\sqrt{\int_{0}^{u}\tilde{F}_{1}(s,u^{\prime})du^{\prime}}.\end{split}

(3.154)

Thus, for any
\bar{Z}^{m+1}
which contains
l
vector fields
T
, it follows from (
3.153
) and (
3.154
) that

\begin{split}\delta^{l}\|\bar{Z}&{}^{m+1}\mathring{L}\phi\|_{s,u}\lesssim\delta^{5/2-\varepsilon_{0}}s^{-1}+\delta s^{-\iota}\sqrt{\tilde{E}_{1,\leq m+2}(s,u)}+\delta^{2}s^{-1}\sqrt{\tilde{E}_{2,\leq m+2}(s,u)}\\
&+\delta^{k(1-\varepsilon_{0})}s^{-1}\delta^{l_{0}}\|\not{d}\bar{Z}^{m_{0}}\phi\|_{s,u}+\delta^{3-\varepsilon_{0}}s^{-1/2}\|R^{m}\not{\triangle}\mu\|_{s,u}+\delta^{2-\varepsilon_{0}}s^{-3/2}\delta^{l^{\prime}}\|\bar{Z}^{m^{\prime}+1}\mu\|_{s,u}\\
&+\delta^{k(1-\varepsilon_{0})}(\delta+\delta^{k(1-\varepsilon_{0})})s^{-3/2}\sqrt{\int_{0}^{u}\tilde{F}_{1}(s,u^{\prime})du^{\prime}}.\end{split}

(3.155)

Due to
|\not{\mathcal{L}}_{\bar{Z}}^{m+1}\not{d}\phi|\lesssim s^{-1}|R\bar{Z}^{m+1}\phi|
,
one needs to estimate the
L^{2}
norm of
R\bar{Z}^{m+1}\phi
. As before, for
\bar{Z}^{m+1}=R^{m+1}
, there holds

\begin{split}&\|R^{m+2}\phi\|_{s,u}\lesssim\delta\|R^{m+1}TR\phi\|_{s,u}+\delta^{3-\varepsilon_{0}}s^{-3/2}\|\not{\mathcal{L}}_{R}^{m_{1}}{\vphantom{\not{\pi}}}{}^{(R)}\not{\pi}_{T}\|_{s,u}+\delta s^{-1}\|R^{m_{0}}R\phi\|_{s,u}.\end{split}

(3.156)

Note that
TR\phi={\vphantom{\not{\pi}}}^{(R)}\not{\pi}_{TX}\not{d}^{X}\phi+(R\mu)\tilde{T}^{i}\varphi_{i}-\mu(Rg^{0i})\varphi_{i}-\mu(R\mathring{L}^{i})\varphi_{i}+\mu\tilde{T}^{i}R\varphi_{i}
. In addition, it follows from (
A.4
) in Appendix A and (
2.41
) that

\begin{split}&{\vphantom{\not{\pi}}}{}^{(R)}\not{\pi}_{TX}\not{d}^{X}\phi-\mu(R\mathring{L}^{i})\varphi_{i}\\
=&\upsilon\not{d}^{X}\mu\not{d}_{X}\phi+\mu(G_{X\mathring{L}}^{\gamma}R\varphi_{\gamma})\not{d}^{X}\phi+\mu(G_{X\tilde{T}}^{\gamma}R\varphi_{\gamma})\not{d}^{X}\phi+\mu\epsilon_{i}^{a}\check{T}^{i}g_{ab}(\not{d}_{X}x^{b}\not{d}^{X}\phi)-\frac{\mu}{\varrho}R\phi\\
&+\upsilon(G_{X\tilde{T}}^{\gamma}T\varphi_{\gamma})\not{d}^{X}\phi-\frac{1}{2}\upsilon\mu(G_{\tilde{T}\tilde{T}}^{\gamma}\not{d}^{X}\varphi_{\gamma})\not{d}_{X}\phi+\mu(G_{\mathring{L}\tilde{T}}^{\gamma}R\varphi_{\gamma})\tilde{T}^{i}\varphi_{i}+\frac{1}{2}\mu(G_{\tilde{T}\tilde{T}}^{\gamma}R\varphi_{\gamma})\tilde{T}^{i}\varphi_{i}.\end{split}

Thus,

\displaystyle\|R^{m+2}\phi\|_{s,u}\lesssim

\displaystyle\delta^{2-\varepsilon_{0}}s^{-1/2}\big\{s\|\not{d}R^{m+1}\mu\|_{s,u}+\|R^{m^{\prime}+1}\mu\|_{s,u}\big\}+\delta^{3-\varepsilon_{0}}s^{-3/2}\|\not{\mathcal{L}}_{R}^{m_{1}}{\vphantom{\not{\pi}}}{}^{(R)}\not{\pi}_{T}\|_{s,u}

\displaystyle+\delta\|Z^{m_{0}}\varphi\|_{s,u}+\delta s\|\not{d}R^{m+1}\varphi\|_{s,u}+\delta^{2k(1-\varepsilon_{0})+2}s^{-2}\ln s\|TR^{m+1}\varphi\|_{s,u}

\displaystyle+\delta^{(k+1)(1-\varepsilon_{0})+1}s^{-5/2}\|R^{m_{0}}\upsilon\|_{s,u}+\delta^{(k+1)(1-\varepsilon_{0})+1}s^{-3/2}\ln s\|\not{\mathcal{L}}_{R}^{m_{0}}\not{g}\|_{s,u}

\displaystyle+\delta^{2-\varepsilon_{0}}s^{-1/2}(\|R^{m_{0}}\check{L}^{1}\|_{s,u}+\|R^{m_{0}}\check{L}^{2}\|_{s,u})+\delta^{2-\varepsilon_{0}}s^{-3/2}\|R^{m_{-1}}x\|_{s,u}

\displaystyle\lesssim\delta^{5/2-\varepsilon_{0}}

\displaystyle+\delta s^{1-\iota}\sqrt{\tilde{E}_{1,\leq m+2}(s,u)}+\delta^{2}\sqrt{\tilde{E}_{2,\leq m+2}(s,u)}+\delta^{2-\varepsilon_{0}}s^{1/2}\|\not{d}R^{m+1}\mu\|_{s,u}

(3.157)

\displaystyle+\delta^{2-\varepsilon_{0}}

\displaystyle s^{-1/2}\|R^{m^{\prime}+1}\mu\|_{s,u}+\delta^{k(1-\varepsilon_{0})+1}s^{-1/2}\sqrt{\int_{0}^{u}\tilde{F}_{1}(s,u^{\prime})du^{\prime}}.

For
\bar{Z}^{m+1}=\bar{Z}^{p_{1}}TR^{p_{2}}
with
p_{1}+p_{2}=m
and the number of
T
in
\bar{Z}^{p_{1}}
being
q_{1}
,
due to
R\bar{Z}^{m+1}\mathring{\phi}=R\bar{Z}^{p_{1}}R^{p_{2}}(\mu\tilde{T}^{i}\varphi_{i})+R\bar{Z}^{p_{1}}[T,R^{p_{2}}]\phi
, then it holds that

\begin{split}\delta^{l}\|R\bar{Z}^{m+1}\phi\|_{s,u}\lesssim&\delta^{5/2-\varepsilon_{0}}+\delta^{2}s^{-\iota}\sqrt{\tilde{E}_{1,\leq m+2}(s,u)}+\delta^{2}\sqrt{\tilde{E}_{2,\leq m+2}(s,u)}+\delta^{2-\varepsilon_{0}}s^{-1/2}\delta^{l^{\prime}}\|\bar{Z}^{m^{\prime}+1}\mu\|_{s,u}\\
&+\delta s^{-2}\ln s\delta^{l_{1}}\|R\bar{Z}^{m_{1}}\phi\|_{s,u}+\delta^{k(1-\varepsilon_{0})+1}s^{-1/2}\sqrt{\int_{0}^{u}\tilde{F}_{1}(s,u^{\prime})du^{\prime}},\end{split}

where
1\leq m_{1}\leq m
and the number of
T
in
\bar{Z}^{m_{1}}
is at most
q_{1}
. Then it follows from induction that

\begin{split}\delta^{l}\|R\bar{Z}^{m+1}\phi\|_{s,u}\lesssim&\delta^{5/2-\varepsilon_{0}}+\delta^{2}s^{-\iota}\sqrt{\tilde{E}_{1,\leq m+2}(s,u)}+\delta^{2}\sqrt{\tilde{E}_{2,\leq m+2}(s,u)}\\
&+\delta^{2-\varepsilon_{0}}s^{-1/2}\delta^{l^{\prime}}\|\bar{Z}^{m^{\prime}+1}\mu\|_{s,u}+\delta s^{-2}\ln s\|R^{m_{1}+1}\phi\|_{s,u}\\
&+\delta^{k(1-\varepsilon_{0})+1}s^{-1/2}\sqrt{\int_{0}^{u}\tilde{F}_{1}(s,u^{\prime})du^{\prime}}\\
\lesssim&\delta^{5/2-\varepsilon_{0}}+\delta^{2}s^{-\iota}\sqrt{\tilde{E}_{1,\leq m+2}(s,u)}+\delta^{2}\sqrt{\tilde{E}_{2,\leq m+2}(s,u)}\\
&+\delta^{2-\varepsilon_{0}}s^{-1/2}\delta^{l^{\prime}}\|\bar{Z}^{m^{\prime}+1}\mu\|_{s,u}+\delta^{k(1-\varepsilon_{0})+1}s^{-1/2}\sqrt{\int_{0}^{u}\tilde{F}_{1}(s,u^{\prime})du^{\prime}}.\end{split}

(3.158)

Note that the last inequality in (
3.158
) holds when the number
m+2
is replaced by
m_{1}+1
in (
3.157
).
Therefore, (
3.148
) follows from (
3.157
) and (
3.158
), and furthermore, (
3.147
) is obtained
by inserting (
3.148
) into (
3.155
).

Analogously, due to

\|R^{m}\mathring{L}^{2}\phi\|_{s,u}\lesssim\delta\|[T,R^{m}\mathring{L}]\mathring{L}\phi\|_{s,u}+\delta\|R^{m}\mathring{L}T(\mathring{L}^{\alpha}\varphi_{\alpha})\|_{s,u},

then it follows from (
3.151
) that for
\tilde{Z}\in\{\varrho\mathring{L},R\}
,

\begin{split}&\|R^{m}\mathring{L}^{2}\phi\|_{s,u}\\
\lesssim&(\delta^{2-\varepsilon_{0}}+\delta^{(k+1)(1-\varepsilon_{0})})s^{-5/2}\big(\|\tilde{Z}^{m_{0}}\check{L}^{1}\|_{s,u}+\|\tilde{Z}^{m_{0}}\check{L}^{2}\|_{s,u}\big)+\delta^{(k+1)(1-\varepsilon_{0})}s^{-5/2}\|\tilde{Z}^{m_{0}}\check{\varrho}\|_{s,u}\\
&+\delta^{2-\varepsilon_{0}}s^{-7/2}\|\tilde{Z}^{m_{-1}}x\|_{s,u}+\delta^{3-\varepsilon_{0}}s^{-5/2}\|\not{\mathcal{L}}_{R}^{m_{1}}{\vphantom{\not{\pi}}}{}^{(T)}\not{\pi}_{\mathring{L}}\|_{s,u}+\delta^{3-\varepsilon_{0}}s^{-7/2}\|\not{\mathcal{L}}_{R}^{m_{1}}{\vphantom{\not{\pi}}}{}^{(R)}\not{\pi}_{T}\|_{s,u}\\
&+\delta^{k(1-\varepsilon_{0})}s^{-2}\|R^{m_{0}}\mathring{L}\phi\|_{s,u}+\delta^{k(1-\varepsilon_{0})}s^{-3}\|R^{m_{0}}\phi\|_{s,u}+\delta^{3-\varepsilon_{0}}s^{-3/2}\|\not{d}R^{m}\mathring{L}\mu\|_{s,u}\\
&+\delta^{2-\varepsilon_{0}}s^{-3/2}\|R^{m_{0}}\mathring{L}\mu\|_{s,u}+\delta^{2-\varepsilon_{0}}s^{-5/2}\|R^{m_{0}}\mu\|_{s,u}+\delta s^{-1-\iota}\sqrt{E_{1,\leq m+2}}+\delta^{2}s^{-2}\sqrt{E_{2,\leq m+2}}.\end{split}

Utilizing (
2.36
), (
2.60
) and (
2.61
) leads to

\begin{split}&\|R^{m}\mathring{L}^{2}\phi\|_{s,u}\\
\lesssim&(\delta^{2-\varepsilon_{0}}+\delta^{(k+1)(1-\varepsilon_{0})})s^{-5/2}\big(\|\tilde{Z}^{m_{0}}\check{L}^{1}\|_{s,u}+\|\tilde{Z}^{m_{0}}\check{L}^{2}\|_{s,u}\big)+\delta^{(k+1)(1-\varepsilon_{0})}s^{-5/2}\|\tilde{Z}^{m_{0}}\check{\varrho}\|_{s,u}\\
&+\delta^{2-\varepsilon_{0}}s^{-7/2}\|\tilde{Z}^{m_{-1}}x\|_{s,u}+\delta^{3-\varepsilon_{0}}s^{-5/2}\|\not{\mathcal{L}}_{R}^{m_{1}}{\vphantom{\not{\pi}}}{}^{(T)}\not{\pi}_{\mathring{L}}\|_{s,u}+\delta^{3-\varepsilon_{0}}s^{-7/2}\|\not{\mathcal{L}}_{R}^{m_{1}}{\vphantom{\not{\pi}}}{}^{(R)}\not{\pi}_{T}\|_{s,u}\\
&+\delta^{k(1-\varepsilon_{0})}s^{-2}\|R^{m_{0}}\mathring{L}\phi\|_{s,u}+\delta^{k(1-\varepsilon_{0})+1}s^{-3}\|\not{d}R^{m+1}\phi\|_{s,u}+\delta^{k(1-\varepsilon_{0})}s^{-3}\|R^{m_{0}}\phi\|_{s,u}\\
&+\delta^{2-\varepsilon_{0}}s^{-5/2}\|R^{m_{0}}\mu\|_{s,u}+\delta s^{-1-\iota}\sqrt{E_{1,\leq m+2}}+\delta^{2}s^{-2}\sqrt{E_{2,\leq m+2}}\\
\lesssim&\delta^{5/2-\varepsilon_{0}}s^{-2}+\delta s^{-1-\iota}\sqrt{\tilde{E}_{1,\leq m+2}(s,u)}+\delta^{2}s^{-2}\sqrt{\tilde{E}_{2,\leq m+2}(s,u)}\\
&+\delta^{(k+1)(1-\varepsilon_{0})+1}(\delta+\delta^{k(1-\varepsilon_{0})})s^{-5/2}\|R^{m}\not{\triangle}\mu\|_{s,u}+\delta^{2-\varepsilon_{0}}s^{-5/2}\delta^{l^{\prime}}\|R^{m^{\prime}+1}\mu\|_{s,u}\\
&+\delta^{k(1-\varepsilon_{0})}(\delta+\delta^{k(1-\varepsilon_{0})})s^{-5/2}\sqrt{\int_{0}^{u}\tilde{F}_{1}(s,u^{\prime})du^{\prime}},\end{split}

where (
3.147
) and (
3.148
) have been used. For the case of
\bar{Z}^{p_{1}}TR^{p_{2}}\mathring{L}^{2}\phi
with
p_{1}+p_{2}=m-1
, the desired result can be obtained by using
\bar{Z}^{p_{1}}TR^{p_{2}}\mathring{L}^{2}\phi=\bar{Z}^{p_{1}}R^{p_{2}}[T,\mathring{L}]\mathring{L}\phi+\bar{Z}^{p_{1}}[T,R^{p_{2}}]\mathring{L}^{2}\phi+\bar{Z}^{p_{1}}R^{p_{2}}\mathring{L}T(\mathring{L}^{\alpha}\varphi_{\alpha})
.
∎

(
3.158
) implies that when there is at least one
T
in
\bar{Z}^{m+1}
, it then holds that

\begin{split}\delta^{l}\|\not{\mathcal{L}}_{\bar{Z}}^{m+1}\not{d}\phi\|_{s,u}\lesssim&\delta^{5/2-\varepsilon_{0}}s^{-1}+\delta^{2}s^{-1-\iota}\sqrt{\tilde{E}_{1,\leq m+2}(s,u)}+\delta^{2}s^{-1}\sqrt{\tilde{E}_{2,\leq m+2}(s,u)}\\
&+\delta^{2-\varepsilon_{0}}s^{-3/2}\delta^{l^{\prime}}\|\bar{Z}^{m^{\prime}+1}\mu\|_{s,u}+\delta^{k(1-\varepsilon_{0})+1}s^{-3/2}\sqrt{\int_{0}^{u}\tilde{F}_{1}(s,u^{\prime})du^{\prime}}.\end{split}

(3.159)

In addition, it follows from (
3.127
) that

\begin{split}\delta^{l}\|Z^{m+1}\phi\|_{s,u}\lesssim&\delta^{5/2-\varepsilon_{0}}+\delta^{2}s^{-\iota}\sqrt{\tilde{E}_{1,\leq m+2}(s,u)}+\delta^{2}\sqrt{\tilde{E}_{2,\leq m+2}(s,u)}\\
&+\delta^{2-\varepsilon_{0}}s^{-1/2}\delta^{l^{\prime}}\|\bar{Z}^{m^{\prime}+1}\mu\|_{s,u}+\delta^{k(1-\varepsilon_{0})+1}s^{-1/2}\sqrt{\int_{0}^{u}\tilde{F}_{1}(s,u^{\prime})du^{\prime}}.\end{split}

(3.160)

One can check easily from (
3.147
) and (
3.148
) that
\|\bar{Z}^{m+1}\mathring{L}\phi\|_{s,u}

has admitted the better smallness order and time decay rate than
\|\not{\mathcal{L}}_{\bar{Z}}^{m+1}\not{d}\phi\|_{s,u}
(see the coefficients of
\|R^{m}\not{\triangle}\mu\|_{s,u}
).
In addition, it should be pointed out that
\delta^{2-\varepsilon_{0}}s^{1/2}\|R^{m}\not{\triangle}\mu\|_{s,u}
in (
3.148
) is not enough to
close the energy estimates derived in Section
5
below when
\varepsilon_{0}
is near
\varepsilon_{k}^{*}
.
To improve
L^{2}
norm estimate of
\bar{Z}^{m+1}\mu
by the transport equation for
\mathring{L}\bar{Z}^{m+1}\mu

derived from (
2.36
), our main strategy is to explore the possibility that the related term
\frac{1}{2}G_{\mathring{L}\mathring{L}}^{\gamma}T\varphi_{\gamma}
in (
2.36
) may admit
more precise smallness and better time decay rate information by some suitable combinations of
\not{d}\phi
.
This will be the next main task.

It follows from (
2.48
) that
\varphi_{\gamma_{j}}=\delta_{\gamma_{j}}^{0}\mathring{L}\phi-\mathring{L}_{\gamma_{j}}\tilde{T}^{l_{j}}\varphi_{l_{j}}+g_{\gamma_{j}a}\not{d}^{X}x^{a}\not{d}_{X}\phi
. Then

\displaystyle g^{\alpha\beta,\gamma\gamma_{2}\cdots\gamma_{k}}\varphi_{\gamma_{2}}\cdots\varphi_{\gamma_{k}}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle(-1)^{k-1}g^{\alpha\beta,\gamma\gamma_{2}\cdots\gamma_{k}}\mathring{L}_{\gamma_{2}}\cdots\mathring{L}_{\gamma_{k}}(\tilde{T}^{l_{2}}\cdots\tilde{T}^{l_{k}})(\varphi_{l_{2}}\cdots\varphi_{l_{k}})

\displaystyle+(-1)^{k}(k-1)g^{\alpha\beta,\gamma\gamma_{2}\cdots\gamma_{k}}(g_{\gamma_{2}a}\not{d}^{X}x^{a}\not{d}_{X}\phi)\underbrace{\mathring{L}_{\gamma_{3}}\cdots\mathring{L}_{\gamma_{k}}(\tilde{T}^{l_{3}}\cdots\tilde{T}^{l_{k}})(\varphi_{l_{3}}\cdots\varphi_{l_{k}})}_{\text{vanish when }k=2}

\displaystyle+f(\varphi,\check{L}^{1},\check{L}^{2},\frac{x}{\varrho})\left(\begin{array}[]{ccc}(\mathring{L}\phi)\varphi^{k-2}\\
(\not{d}^{X}x)(\not{d}_{X}\phi)(\not{d}^{X}x)(\not{d}_{X}\phi)\varphi^{k-3}\\
\end{array}\right)

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle(-1)^{k-1}g^{\alpha\beta,\gamma\gamma_{2}\cdots\gamma_{k}}\omega_{\gamma_{2}}\cdots\omega_{\gamma_{k}}(\tilde{T}^{l_{2}}\cdots\tilde{T}^{l_{k}})(\varphi_{l_{2}}\cdots\varphi_{l_{k}})

\displaystyle+(-1)^{k}(k-1)g^{\alpha\beta,\gamma a\gamma_{3}\cdots\gamma_{k}}(m_{ab}\not{d}^{X}x^{b}\not{d}_{X}\phi)\underbrace{\omega_{\gamma_{3}}\cdots\omega_{\gamma_{k}}(\tilde{T}^{l_{3}}\cdots\tilde{T}^{l_{k}})(\varphi_{l_{3}}\cdots\varphi_{l_{k}})}_{\text{vanish when }k=2}

(3.163)

\displaystyle+f(\varphi,\check{L}^{1},\check{L}^{2},\frac{x}{\varrho},\check{\varrho})\left(\begin{array}[]{ccc}\check{L}^{1}\varphi^{k-1}\\
\check{L}^{2}\varphi^{k-1}\\
\check{\varrho}\varphi^{k-1}\\
(\mathring{L}\phi)\varphi^{k-2}\\
(\not{d}^{X}x)(\not{d}_{X}\phi)\varphi^{2k-2}\\
(\not{d}^{X}x)(\not{d}_{X}\phi)(\not{d}^{X}x)(\not{d}_{X}\phi)\varphi^{k-3}\\
\end{array}\right).

Substituting (
3.163
) into the third line of (
2.61
) and using the null condition (
1.9
) yield

\displaystyle(\partial_{\varphi_{\gamma}}g^{\alpha\beta})\mathring{L}_{\alpha}\mathring{L}_{\beta}\mathring{L}_{\gamma}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle(-1)^{k}k(k-1)g^{\alpha\beta,\gamma a\gamma_{3}\cdots\gamma_{k}}\omega_{\alpha}\omega_{\beta}\omega_{\gamma}(m_{ab}\not{d}^{X}x^{b}\not{d}_{X}\phi)\underbrace{\omega_{\gamma_{3}}\cdots\omega_{\gamma_{k}}(\tilde{T}^{l_{3}}\cdots\tilde{T}^{l_{k}})(\varphi_{l_{3}}\cdots\varphi_{l_{k}})}_{\text{vanish when }k=2}

\displaystyle+f(\varphi,\check{L}^{1},\check{L}^{2},\frac{x}{\varrho},\check{\varrho})\left(\begin{array}[]{ccc}\varphi^{k}\\
\check{L}^{1}\varphi^{k-1}\\
\check{L}^{2}\varphi^{k-1}\\
\check{\varrho}\varphi^{k-1}\\
(\mathring{L}\phi)\varphi^{k-2}\\
(\not{d}^{X}x)(\not{d}_{X}\phi)\varphi^{2k-2}\\
(\not{d}^{X}x)(\not{d}_{X}\phi)(\not{d}^{X}x)(\not{d}_{X}\phi)\varphi^{k-3}\\
\end{array}\right).

Based on (
3
), we can introduce a good unknown as

\mathscr{A}=g^{\alpha\beta,\gamma a\gamma_{3}\cdots\gamma_{k}}\omega_{\alpha}\omega_{\beta}\omega_{\gamma}(m_{ab}\not{d}^{X}x^{b}\not{d}_{X}\phi)\omega_{\gamma_{3}}\cdots\omega_{\gamma_{k}},

(3.177)

which is a combination of
\not{d}\phi
.

Lemma 3.4
.

Under the assumption
(\star)
, when
\delta>0
is small, it holds that for
m\leq 2N-6
,

\begin{split}\delta^{l}\|&\bar{Z}^{m+1}\mathscr{A}\|_{s,u}\lesssim\delta^{3/2-\varepsilon_{0}}(\delta+\delta^{k(1-\varepsilon_{0})}\ln s)s^{-1}+\delta s^{-\iota}\sqrt{\tilde{E}_{1,\leq m+2}(s,u)}\\
&+\big(\delta^{2}+\delta^{k(1-\varepsilon_{0})}(\delta+\delta^{k(1-\varepsilon_{0})})\ln^{2}s\big)s^{-1}\sqrt{\tilde{E}_{2,\leq m+2}(s,u)}+\delta^{2-\varepsilon_{0}}s^{-3/2}\delta^{l^{\prime}}\|\bar{Z}^{m^{\prime}+1}\mu\|_{s,u}\\
&+(\delta^{3-\varepsilon_{0}}+\delta^{(k+1)(1-\varepsilon_{0})+1})s^{-1/2}\|R^{m}\not{\triangle}\mu\|_{s,u}+\delta^{k(1-\varepsilon_{0})}s^{-1/2}\sqrt{\int_{0}^{u}\tilde{F}_{1}(s,u^{\prime})du^{\prime}},\end{split}

(3.178)

where
\bar{Z}\in\{T,R\}
, the numbers of
T
in
\bar{Z}^{m+1}
and
\bar{Z}^{m^{\prime}+1}
are
l
and
l^{\prime}
respectively,
m^{\prime}\leq m
.

Remark 3.1
.

Note that the coefficients of
\|R^{m}\not{\triangle}\mu\|_{s,u}

in (
3.147
) and (
3.178
) are the same.
We will use (
3.178
) instead of (
3.147
) to estimate
\|L\bar{Z}^{m+1}\mu\|_{s,u}
later.

Proof.

Due to
\not{d}^{X}x^{b}\not{d}_{X}\phi=(g^{bj}-\mathring{L}^{b}g^{0j}-\mathring{L}^{j}g^{0b}-\mathring{L}^{b}\mathring{L}^{j})\varphi_{j}
by (
2.47
) and
m_{ab}\mathring{L}^{b}=\omega_{a}+m_{ab}\check{L}^{b}+\check{\varrho}\omega_{a}
, then it holds that

\begin{split}\mathscr{A}=&g^{\alpha\beta,\gamma a\gamma_{3}\cdots\gamma_{k}}\omega_{\alpha}\omega_{\beta}\omega_{\gamma}m_{ab}(g^{bj}-\mathring{L}^{b}g^{0j}-\mathring{L}^{j}g^{0b})\varphi_{j}\omega_{\gamma_{3}}\cdots\omega_{\gamma_{k}}\\
&-g^{\alpha\beta,\gamma a\gamma_{3}\cdots\gamma_{k}}\omega_{\alpha}\omega_{\beta}\omega_{\gamma}\omega_{a}\omega_{\gamma_{3}}\cdots\omega_{\gamma_{k}}(\mathring{L}^{j}\varphi_{j})+f(\omega)\left(\begin{array}[]{ccc}\check{L}^{1}\\
\check{L}^{2}\\
\check{\varrho}\\
\end{array}\right)(\mathring{L}^{j}\varphi_{j})\\
=&g^{\alpha\beta,\gamma a\gamma_{3}\cdots\gamma_{k}}\omega_{\alpha}\omega_{\beta}\omega_{\gamma}m_{ab}(g^{bj}-\mathring{L}^{b}g^{0j}-\mathring{L}^{j}g^{0b})\varphi_{j}\omega_{\gamma_{3}}\cdots\omega_{\gamma_{k}}\\
&-g^{\alpha\beta,\gamma 0\gamma_{3}\cdots\gamma_{k}}\omega_{\alpha}\omega_{\beta}\omega_{\gamma}\omega_{\gamma_{3}}\cdots\omega_{\gamma_{k}}(\mathring{L}^{j}\varphi_{j})+f(\omega)\left(\begin{array}[]{ccc}\check{L}^{1}\\
\check{L}^{2}\\
\check{\varrho}\\
\end{array}\right)(\mathring{L}^{j}\varphi_{j}),\end{split}

where the last identity comes from (
1.9
). This, together with (
3.105
), yields

\begin{split}&\|R^{m+1}\mathscr{A}\|_{s,u}\\
\lesssim&\|R^{m+1}\Big\{f(\omega)\left(\begin{array}[]{ccc}\check{L}^{1}\\
\check{L}^{2}\\
\check{\varrho}\\
\end{array}\right)(\mathring{L}^{j}\varphi_{j})\Big\}\|_{s,u}\\
&+\delta\|[R^{m+1},T]\big\{g^{\alpha\beta,\gamma a\gamma_{3}\cdots\gamma_{k}}\omega_{\alpha}\omega_{\beta}\omega_{\gamma}m_{ab}(g^{bj}-\mathring{L}^{b}g^{0j}-\mathring{L}^{j}g^{0b})\varphi_{j}\omega_{\gamma_{3}}\cdots\omega_{\gamma_{k}}\\
&\qquad\qquad\qquad\quad-g^{\alpha\beta,\gamma 0\gamma_{3}\cdots\gamma_{k}}\omega_{\alpha}\omega_{\beta}\omega_{\gamma}\omega_{\gamma_{3}}\cdots\omega_{\gamma_{k}}(\mathring{L}^{j}\varphi_{j})\big\}\|_{s,u}\\
&+\delta\|R^{m+1}\big\{T\big(g^{\alpha\beta,\gamma a\gamma_{3}\cdots\gamma_{k}}\omega_{\alpha}\omega_{\beta}\omega_{\gamma}\omega_{\gamma_{3}}\cdots\omega_{\gamma_{k}}m_{ab}(g^{bj}-\mathring{L}^{b}g^{0j}-\mathring{L}^{j}g^{0b})\big)\varphi_{j}\big\}\|_{s,u}\\
&+\delta\|R^{m+1}\big\{T\big(g^{\alpha\beta,\gamma 0\gamma_{3}\cdots\gamma_{k}}\omega_{\alpha}\omega_{\beta}\omega_{\gamma}\omega_{\gamma_{3}}\cdots\omega_{\gamma_{k}}\big)\mathring{L}^{j}\varphi_{j}\big\}\|_{s,u}\\
&+\delta\|R^{m+1}\big\{g^{\alpha\beta,\gamma a\gamma_{3}\cdots\gamma_{k}}\omega_{\alpha}\omega_{\beta}\omega_{\gamma}\omega_{\gamma_{3}}\cdots\omega_{\gamma_{k}}m_{ab}(g^{bj}-\mathring{L}^{b}g^{0j}-\mathring{L}^{j}g^{0b})T\varphi_{j}\\
&\qquad\qquad\quad-g^{\alpha\beta,\gamma 0\gamma_{3}\cdots\gamma_{k}}\omega_{\alpha}\omega_{\beta}\omega_{\gamma}\omega_{\gamma_{3}}\cdots\omega_{\gamma_{k}}(\mathring{L}^{j}T\varphi_{j})\big\}\|_{s,u}\\
&+\delta\|R^{m+1}\big\{g^{\alpha\beta,\gamma 0\gamma_{3}\cdots\gamma_{k}}\omega_{\alpha}\omega_{\beta}\omega_{\gamma}\omega_{\gamma_{3}}\cdots\omega_{\gamma_{k}}(T\mathring{L}^{j})\varphi_{j}\big\}\|_{s,u}.\end{split}

(3.179)

Denote the first five lines in the right hand side of (
3.179
) as
\mathcal{I}
, the sixth-seventh
lines as
\mathcal{II}
, and the last line as
\mathcal{III}
, which will be estimated separately as follows.

1.

Since
\omega^{i}=\frac{1}{\check{\varrho}+1}\cdot\frac{x^{i}}{\varrho}
,
T\omega^{i}=\frac{\mu\tilde{T}^{i}}{\varrho(\check{\varrho}+1)}-\sum_{j=1}^{3}\frac{\mu x^{i}x^{j}\tilde{T}^{j}}{\varrho^{3}(\check{\varrho}+1)^{3}}
and
T\mathring{L}^{i}
satisfies (
2.43
), then

\begin{split}\mathcal{I}\lesssim&\delta^{1-\varepsilon_{0}}s^{-1/2}\big(\sum_{i=1}^{2}\|R^{m_{0}}\check{L}^{i}\|_{s,u}+\|R^{m_{0}}\check{\varrho}\|_{s,u}\big)+\delta^{k(1-\varepsilon_{0})}s^{-1}\ln s\|R^{m_{0}}\varphi\|_{s,u}\\
&+(\delta^{(k+1)(1-\varepsilon_{0})}\ln s+\delta^{2-\varepsilon_{0}})s^{-5/2}\|R^{m_{-1}}x\|_{s,u}+\delta^{2-\varepsilon_{0}}s^{-3/2}\|\not{\mathcal{L}}_{R}^{m_{1}}{\vphantom{\not{\pi}}}{}^{(R)}\not{\pi}_{T}\|_{s,u}\\
&+\delta^{2-\varepsilon_{0}}s^{-5/2}\ln s(\delta^{k(1-\varepsilon_{0})}+\delta^{2k(1-\varepsilon_{0})-1})\|\not{\mathcal{L}}_{R}^{m_{1}}\not{g}\|_{s,u}+\boxed{\delta^{2-\varepsilon_{0}}s^{-3/2}\|R^{m^{\prime}+1}\mu\|_{s,u}}\\
&+\boxed{\delta^{(k+1)(1-\varepsilon_{0})+1}s^{-3/2}\|\not{d}R^{m+1}\mu\|_{s,u}}+\delta^{k(1-\varepsilon_{0})+1}s^{-1}\|TR^{m^{\prime}+1}\varphi\|_{s,u}\\
&+\delta^{2k(1-\varepsilon_{0})+1}s^{-2}\|\not{d}R^{m+1}\varphi\|_{s,u}+\delta^{2k(1-\varepsilon_{0})+1}s^{-2}\|R^{m^{\prime}+1}\mathring{L}\varphi\|_{s,u},\end{split}

(3.180)

where the second boxed term above arises from
\delta\|R^{m+1}\big(g^{0j}T\mathring{L}^{b}+g^{0b}T\mathring{L}^{j}\big)\varphi_{j}\|_{s,u}
.

2.

Making use of (
1.9
) again, that is,
g^{\alpha\beta,\gamma 0\gamma_{3}\cdots\gamma_{k}}\omega_{\alpha}\omega_{\beta}\omega_{\gamma}\omega_{\gamma_{3}}\cdots\omega_{\gamma_{k}}=g^{\alpha\beta,\gamma a\gamma_{3}\cdots\gamma_{k}}\omega_{\alpha}\omega_{\beta}\omega_{\gamma}\omega_{a}\omega_{\gamma_{3}}\cdots\omega_{\gamma_{k}}
, then one can rewrite
\omega_{a}=m_{ab}\mathring{L}^{b}+(\frac{1}{\check{\varrho}+1}-1)m_{ab}\mathring{L}^{b}-\frac{1}{\check{\varrho}+1}m_{ab}\check{L}^{b}
to get

\begin{split}\mathcal{II}=&\delta\|R^{m+1}\big\{g^{\alpha\beta,\gamma a\gamma_{3}\cdots\gamma_{k}}\omega_{\alpha}\omega_{\beta}\omega_{\gamma}\omega_{\gamma_{3}}\cdots\omega_{\gamma_{k}}(m_{ab}\not{d}^{X}x^{b}\not{d}_{X}\varphi_{j})\mu\tilde{T}^{j}\big\}\|_{s,u}\\
&+\delta\|R^{m+1}\Big\{f(\omega,\mathring{L}^{1},\mathring{L}^{2},\check{\varrho})\left(\begin{array}[]{ccc}\check{L}^{1}\\
\check{L}^{2}\\
\check{\varrho}\\
\end{array}\right)(\mathring{L}^{j}T\varphi_{j})\Big\}\|_{s,u},\end{split}

which implies

\begin{split}\mathcal{II}\lesssim&\delta\|\not{d}R^{m+1}\varphi\|_{s,u}+\delta^{2-\varepsilon_{0}}s^{-5/2}\|R^{m_{-1}}x\|_{s,u}+\delta^{1-\varepsilon_{0}}s^{-1/2}\|R^{m_{0}}\check{\varrho}\|_{s,u}\\
&+\delta^{1-\varepsilon_{0}}s^{-1/2}\big(\|R^{m_{0}}\check{L}^{1}\|_{s,u}+\|R^{m_{0}}\check{L}^{2}\|_{s,u}\big)+\boxed{\delta^{2-\varepsilon_{0}}s^{-3/2}\|R^{m^{\prime}+1}\mu\|_{s,u}}\\
&+\delta s^{-1}\|R^{m_{0}}\varphi\|_{s,u}+\delta^{k(1-\varepsilon_{0})+1}s^{-1}\ln s\|TR^{m^{\prime}+1}\varphi\|_{s,u}\\
&+\delta^{(k+1)(1-\varepsilon_{0})+1}s^{-5/2}\ln s\|\not{\mathcal{L}}_{R}^{m_{1}}{\vphantom{\not{\pi}}}{}^{(R)}\not{\pi}_{T}\|_{s,u}+\delta^{2-\varepsilon_{0}}s^{-3/2}\|\not{\mathcal{L}}_{R}^{m_{1}}\not{g}\|_{s,u}.\end{split}

(3.181)

3.

It follows from (
2.43
) and (
2.61
) that

\begin{split}\mathcal{III}\lesssim&\boxed{\delta^{3-\varepsilon_{0}}s^{-3/2}\|\not{d}R^{m+1}\mu\|_{s,u}}+\boxed{(\delta^{(k+1)(1-\varepsilon_{0})+1}+\delta^{3-\varepsilon_{0}})s^{-5/2}\|R^{m^{\prime}+1}\mu\|_{s,u}}\\
&+\delta^{(k+1)(1-\varepsilon_{0})+1}s^{-5/2}\|\not{\mathcal{L}}_{R}^{m_{1}}\not{g}\|_{s,u}+\delta^{k(1-\varepsilon_{0})}s^{-1}\|\not{d}R^{m^{\prime}+1}\phi\|_{s,u}\\
&+\delta^{k(1-\varepsilon_{0})}s^{-1}\|R^{m^{\prime}+1}\mathring{L}\phi\|_{s,u}+\delta^{(k+2)(1-\varepsilon_{0})}s^{-5/2}\|R^{m_{0}+1}x\|_{s,u}\\
&+\delta^{(k+1)(1-\varepsilon_{0})}s^{-3/2}\big(\|R^{m_{0}}\check{L}^{1}\|_{s,u}+\|R^{m_{0}}\check{L}^{2}\|_{s,u}+\|R^{m_{0}}\check{\varrho}\|_{s,u}\big)\\
&+\delta^{(k+1)(1-\varepsilon_{0})+1}s^{-3/2}\|TR^{m^{\prime}+1}\varphi\|_{s,u}+\delta^{(k+2)(1-\varepsilon_{0})+1}s^{-3}\|\not{\mathcal{L}}_{R}^{m_{1}}{\vphantom{\not{\pi}}}{}^{(R)}\not{\pi}_{T}\|_{s,u}\\
&+\delta^{k(1-\varepsilon_{0})+1}s^{-1}\|R^{m^{\prime}+1}\mathring{L}\varphi\|_{s,u}+\delta^{k(1-\varepsilon_{0})+1}s^{-1}\|\not{d}R^{m+1}\varphi\|_{s,u}\\
&+\delta^{(k+1)(1-\varepsilon_{0})}s^{-3/2}\|R^{m_{0}}\varphi\|_{s,u}.\end{split}

(3.182)

Substituting (
3.180
)-(
3.182
) to (
3.179
), using Proposition
3.1
and Lemma
3.3
to estimate all terms except the boxed ones, and adopting (
2.76
) and (
A.7
) in Appendix A to find the relationship between
\not{d}R^{m+1}\mu
and
R^{m}\not{\triangle}\mu
,
then one can get eventually that

\begin{split}\|R^{m+1}&\mathscr{A}\|_{s,u}\lesssim\delta^{3/2-\varepsilon_{0}}(\delta+\delta^{k(1-\varepsilon_{0})}\ln s)s^{-1}+\delta s^{-\iota}\sqrt{\tilde{E}_{1,\leq m+2}(s,u)}\\
&+\big(\delta^{2}+\delta^{k(1-\varepsilon_{0})}(\delta+\delta^{k(1-\varepsilon_{0})})\ln^{2}s\big)s^{-1}\sqrt{\tilde{E}_{2,\leq m+2}(s,u)}+\delta^{2-\varepsilon_{0}}s^{-3/2}\|R^{m^{\prime}+1}\mu\|_{s,u}\\
&+(\delta^{3-\varepsilon_{0}}+\delta^{(k+1)(1-\varepsilon_{0})+1})s^{-1/2}\|R^{m}\not{\triangle}\mu\|_{s,u}+\delta^{k(1-\varepsilon_{0})}s^{-1/2}\sqrt{\int_{0}^{u}\tilde{F}_{1}(s,u^{\prime})du^{\prime}}.\end{split}

(3.183)

For
\bar{Z}^{m+1}=\bar{Z}^{p_{1}}TR^{p_{2}}
with
p_{1}+p_{2}=m
, it follows from the expression of
\mathscr{A}
, Proposition
3.1
, (
3.159
) and (

) that

\begin{split}&\delta^{l}\|\bar{Z}^{m+1}\mathscr{A}\|_{s,u}\\
\lesssim&\delta^{2-\varepsilon_{0}}s^{-3/2}\big\{\delta^{l_{0}}\|\bar{Z}^{m_{0}}\check{\varrho}\|_{s,u}+s^{-1}\delta^{l_{-1}}\|\bar{Z}^{m_{-1}}x\|_{s,u}\big\}+\delta^{l}\|\not{\mathcal{L}}_{\bar{Z}}^{m+1}\not{d}\phi\|_{s,u}+s^{-1}\delta^{l_{0}}\|\bar{Z}^{m_{0}}\phi\|_{s,u}\\
\lesssim&\delta^{5/2-\varepsilon_{0}}s^{-1}+\delta^{2}s^{-1-\iota}\sqrt{\tilde{E}_{1,\leq m+2}}+\delta^{2}s^{-1}\sqrt{\tilde{E}_{2,\leq m+2}}+\delta^{2-\varepsilon_{0}}s^{-3/2}\delta^{l^{\prime}}\|\bar{Z}^{m^{\prime}+1}\mu\|_{s,u}\\
&+\delta^{k(1-\varepsilon_{0})+1}s^{-3/2}\sqrt{\int_{0}^{u}\tilde{F}_{1}(s,u^{\prime})du^{\prime}}.\end{split}

(3.184)

Therefore, (
3.178
) follows from (
3.183
) and (
3.184
).
∎

Next we can refine the
L^{2}
norm of
Z^{m+1}\mu
despite the appearance of
R^{m}\not{\triangle}\mu
which will be dealt with later.

Proposition 3.2
.

Under the assumption
(\star)
, when
\delta>0
is small, it holds that for
m\leq 2N-6
,

\begin{split}&\delta^{l}\|Z^{m+1}\mu\|_{s,u}\lesssim\delta^{k(1-\varepsilon_{0})-\varepsilon_{0}+1/2}s^{1/2}+\delta^{(k-1)(1-\varepsilon_{0})}s^{1/2}\sqrt{\tilde{E}_{1,\leq m+2}(s,u)}\\
&\qquad+\delta^{k(1-\varepsilon_{0})}s^{1/2}\sqrt{\tilde{E}_{2,\leq m+2}(s,u)}+\delta^{k(1-\varepsilon_{0})}(\delta+\delta^{k(1-\varepsilon_{0})})s^{1/2}\int_{t_{0}}^{s}\tau^{-3/2}\|R^{m}\not{\triangle}\mu\|_{\tau,u}d\tau\\
&\qquad+\delta^{(2k-1)(1-\varepsilon_{0})-1}s^{1/2}\sqrt{\int_{0}^{u}\tilde{F}_{1}(s,u^{\prime})du^{\prime}}.\end{split}

(3.185)

Proof.

For any
\bar{Z}\in\{T,R\}
, (
2.36
) together with (
2.60
) and (
3
) gives

\displaystyle\delta^{l}\|\mathring{L}\bar{Z}^{m+1}\mu\|_{s,u}

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle\delta^{k(1-\varepsilon_{0})}s^{-1}\delta^{l}\|T\bar{Z}^{m+1}\varphi\|_{s,u}+\delta^{(k+1)(1-\varepsilon_{0})}s^{-5/2}\delta^{l_{1}}\|\not{\mathcal{L}}_{\bar{Z}}^{m_{1}}{\vphantom{\not{\pi}}}{}^{(R)}\not{\pi}_{T}\|_{s,u}

\displaystyle+\delta^{k(1-\varepsilon_{0})-1}s^{-1}\delta^{l_{0}}\big(\|\bar{Z}^{m_{0}}\check{\varrho}\|_{s,u}+\|\bar{Z}^{m_{0}}\check{L}^{1}\|_{s,u}+\|\bar{Z}^{m_{0}}\check{L}^{2}\|_{s,u}\big)

\displaystyle+\delta^{(k-1)(1-\varepsilon_{0})-1}s^{-1/2}\delta^{l_{0}}\|\bar{Z}^{m_{0}}\mathring{L}\phi\|_{s,u}+\delta^{k(1-\varepsilon_{0})-1}s^{-1}\delta^{l_{0}}\|\bar{Z}^{m_{0}}\varphi\|_{s,u}

\displaystyle+\delta^{(k-1)(1-\varepsilon_{0})-1}(\delta+\delta^{k(1-\varepsilon_{0})})s^{-3/2}\delta^{l_{0}}\|\not{\mathcal{L}}_{\bar{Z}}^{m_{0}}\not{d}\phi\|_{s,u}+\delta^{k(1-\varepsilon_{0})-\varepsilon_{0}}s^{-1}\delta^{l_{1}}\|\not{\mathcal{L}}_{\bar{Z}}^{m_{1}}{\vphantom{\not{\pi}}}{}^{(R)}\not{\pi}_{\mathring{L}}\|_{s,u}

\displaystyle+\delta^{k(1-\varepsilon_{0})-\varepsilon_{0}}s^{-5/2}\delta^{l_{-1}}\|\bar{Z}^{m_{-1}}x\|_{s,u}+\delta^{(k-1)(1-\varepsilon_{0})}s^{-1/2}\delta^{l}\|\not{d}\bar{Z}^{m+1}\varphi\|_{s,u}

\displaystyle+\delta^{(k-1)(1-\varepsilon_{0})-1}s^{-1/2}\delta^{l_{0}}\|\bar{Z}^{m_{0}}\mathscr{A}\|_{s,u}+\delta^{(k-1)(1-\varepsilon_{0})}s^{-1/2}\delta^{l_{0}}\|\mathring{L}\bar{Z}^{m_{0}}\varphi\|_{s,u}\qquad\qquad

\displaystyle+\delta^{(k+1)(1-\varepsilon_{0})}s^{-1}\delta^{l_{1}}\|\not{\mathcal{L}}_{\bar{Z}}^{m_{1}}{\vphantom{\not{\pi}}}{}^{(T)}\not{\pi}_{\mathring{L}}\|_{s,u}+\delta^{2k(1-\varepsilon_{0})-\varepsilon_{0}}s^{-2}\ln s\delta^{l_{1}}\|\not{\mathcal{L}}_{\bar{Z}}^{m_{1}}\not{g}\|_{s,u}

\displaystyle+\delta^{k(1-\varepsilon_{0})}s^{-2}\delta^{l_{0}}\|\bar{Z}^{m_{0}}\mu\|_{s,u},

where
l_{p}
(
p=-1,0,1
) are the numbers of
T
in
Z^{m_{p}}
and
1\leq m_{p}\leq m+1-p
.
It follows from Proposition
3.1
, Lemma
3.3
and (
3.178
) that

\begin{split}\delta^{l}\|\mathring{L}\bar{Z}^{m+1}\mu\|_{s,u}\lesssim&\delta^{k(1-\varepsilon_{0})}(\delta+\delta^{k(1-\varepsilon_{0})})s^{-1}\|R^{m}\not{\triangle}\mu\|_{s,u}+\delta^{k(1-\varepsilon_{0})}s^{-2}\delta^{l_{0}}\|\bar{Z}^{m_{0}}\mu\|_{s,u}\\
&+\delta^{k(1-\varepsilon_{0})-\varepsilon_{0}+1/2}s^{-1}+\delta^{(k-1)(1-\varepsilon_{0})}s^{-1/2-\iota}\sqrt{\tilde{E}_{1,\leq m+2}(s,u)}\\
&+\delta^{k(1-\varepsilon_{0})}s^{-1}\sqrt{\tilde{E}_{2,\leq m+2}(s,u)}+\delta^{(2k-1)(1-\varepsilon_{0})-1}s^{-1}\sqrt{\int_{0}^{u}\tilde{F}_{1}(s,u^{\prime})du^{\prime}}.\end{split}

As in (
3.135
), by taking
F(s,u,\vartheta)=\delta^{l}\big(\bar{Z}^{m+1}\mu(s,u,\vartheta)-\bar{Z}^{m+1}\mu(t_{0},u,\vartheta)\big)
in (
3.108
), one gets

\begin{split}&\delta^{l}\|\bar{Z}^{m+1}\mu\|_{s,u}\lesssim\delta^{k(1-\varepsilon_{0})-\varepsilon_{0}+1/2}s^{1/2}+\delta^{(k-1)(1-\varepsilon_{0})}s^{1/2}\sqrt{\tilde{E}_{1,\leq m+2}(s,u)}\\
&\qquad+\delta^{k(1-\varepsilon_{0})}s^{1/2}\sqrt{\tilde{E}_{2,\leq m+2}(s,u)}+\delta^{k(1-\varepsilon_{0})}(\delta+\delta^{k(1-\varepsilon_{0})})s^{1/2}\int_{t_{0}}^{s}\tau^{-3/2}\|R^{m}\not{\triangle}\mu\|_{\tau,u}d\tau\\
&\qquad+\delta^{(2k-1)(1-\varepsilon_{0})-1}s^{1/2}\sqrt{\int_{0}^{u}\tilde{F}_{1}(s,u^{\prime})du^{\prime}}.\end{split}

(3.186)

For
Z^{m+1}=Z^{p_{1}}(\varrho\mathring{L})\bar{Z}^{p_{2}}
with
p_{1}+p_{2}=m
, due to (
2.36
), (
2.60
) and (
2.61
), one can get by Proposition
3.1
and (
3.160
) that

\begin{split}&\delta^{l}\|Z^{p_{1}}(\varrho\mathring{L})\bar{Z}^{p_{2}}\mu\|_{s,u}\lesssim\delta^{l}\|Z^{p_{1}}(\varrho\bar{Z}^{p_{2}}\mathring{L}\mu)\|_{s,u}+\delta^{l}\|Z^{p_{1}}(\varrho[\mathring{L},\bar{Z}^{p_{2}}]\mu)\|_{s,u}\\
\lesssim&\delta^{k(1-\varepsilon_{0})-\varepsilon_{0}}\delta^{l_{1}}\|\not{\mathcal{L}}_{Z}^{m_{1}}{\vphantom{\not{\pi}}}{}^{(R)}\not{\pi}_{\mathring{L}}\|_{s,u}+\delta^{(k+1)(1-\varepsilon_{0})}\delta^{l_{1}}\|\not{\mathcal{L}}_{Z}^{m_{1}}{\vphantom{\not{\pi}}}{}^{(T)}\not{\pi}_{\mathring{L}}\|_{s,u}+\delta^{k(1-\varepsilon_{0})}s^{-1}\ln s\delta^{l_{1}}\|Z^{m_{1}}\mu\|_{s,u}\\
&+\delta^{2k(1-\varepsilon_{0})-\varepsilon_{0}}s^{-1}\ln s\delta^{l_{1}}\|\not{\mathcal{L}}_{Z}^{m_{1}}\not{g}\|_{s,u}+\delta^{k(1-\varepsilon_{0})-1}\delta^{l_{1}}\big(\|Z^{m_{1}}\check{L}^{1}\|_{s,u}+\|Z^{m_{1}}\check{L}^{2}\|_{s,u}+\|Z^{m_{1}}\check{\varrho}\|_{s,u}\big)\\
&+\delta^{k(1-\varepsilon_{0})-\varepsilon_{0}}s^{-3/2}\delta^{l_{0}}\|Z^{m_{0}}x\|_{s,u}+\delta^{k(1-\varepsilon_{0})-1}\delta^{l_{0}}\|Z^{m_{0}}\varphi\|_{s,u}+\delta^{(k-1)(1-\varepsilon_{0})-1}s^{-1/2}\delta^{l_{0}}\|Z^{m_{0}}\phi\|_{s,u}\\
\lesssim&\delta^{k(1-\varepsilon_{0})-\varepsilon_{0}+1/2}+\delta^{k(1-\varepsilon_{0})}(1+\delta^{k(1-\varepsilon_{0})-1})s^{-\iota}\sqrt{\tilde{E}_{1,\leq m+2}(s,u)}+\delta^{k(1-\varepsilon_{0})}\sqrt{\tilde{E}_{2,\leq m+2}(s,u)}\\
&+\delta^{k(1-\varepsilon_{0})}s^{-1}\ln s\delta^{l_{1}}\|Z^{m_{1}}\mu\|_{s,u}+\delta^{(2k-1)(1-\varepsilon_{0})-1}\sqrt{\int_{0}^{u}\tilde{F}_{1}(s,u^{\prime})du^{\prime}}.\end{split}

(3.187)

Thus, (
3.185
) follows from (
3.186
) and (
3.187
).
∎

It follows from (
3.185
), Lemma
3.3
, (
3.160
) and (

) that

Corollary 3.1
.

Under the assumption

\delta>0
, it holds that for

,

\displaystyle\delta^{l}\|\bar{Z}^{m+1}\mathring{L}\phi

\displaystyle\|_{s,u}\lesssim\delta^{5/2-\varepsilon_{0}}s^{-1}+\delta s^{-\iota}\sqrt{\tilde{E}_{1,\leq m+2}(s,u)}+\delta^{2}s^{-1}\sqrt{\tilde{E}_{2,\leq m+2}(s,u)}

\displaystyle+

\displaystyle\delta^{2-\varepsilon_{0}}(\delta+\delta^{k(1-\varepsilon_{0})})\big\{s^{-1/2}\|R^{m}\not{\triangle}\mu\|_{s,u}+\delta^{k(1-\varepsilon_{0})}s^{-1}\int_{t_{0}}^{s}\tau^{-3/2}\|R^{m}\not{\triangle}\mu\|_{\tau,u}d\tau\big\}

(3.188)

\displaystyle+

\displaystyle\delta^{k(1-\varepsilon_{0})}(\delta s^{-3/2}+\delta^{k(1-\varepsilon_{0})}s^{-1})\sqrt{\int_{0}^{u}\tilde{F}_{1}(s,u^{\prime})du^{\prime}},

\displaystyle\|_{s,u}\lesssim\delta^{5/2-\varepsilon_{0}}s^{-1}+\delta s^{-\iota}\sqrt{\tilde{E}_{1,\leq m+2}(s,u)}+\delta^{2}s^{-1}\sqrt{\tilde{E}_{2,\leq m+2}(s,u)}

\displaystyle+\delta^{2-\varepsilon_{0}}

\displaystyle s^{1/2}\|R^{m}\not{\triangle}\mu\|_{s,u}+\delta^{(k+1)(1-\varepsilon_{0})+1}(\delta+\delta^{k(1-\varepsilon_{0})})s^{-1}\int_{t_{0}}^{s}\tau^{-3/2}\|R^{m}\not{\triangle}\mu\|_{\tau,u}d\tau

(3.189)

\displaystyle+\delta^{k(1-\varepsilon_{0})}

\displaystyle(\delta s^{-3/2}+\delta^{k(1-\varepsilon_{0})}s^{-1})\sqrt{\int_{0}^{u}\tilde{F}_{1}(s,u^{\prime})du^{\prime}},

\displaystyle\|_{s,u}\lesssim\delta^{5/2-\varepsilon_{0}}s^{-2}+\delta s^{-1-\iota}\sqrt{\tilde{E}_{1,\leq m+2}(s,u)}+\delta^{2}s^{-2}\sqrt{\tilde{E}_{2,\leq m+2}(s,u)}

\displaystyle+

\displaystyle\delta^{(k+1)(1-\varepsilon_{0})+1}(\delta+\delta^{k(1-\varepsilon_{0})})s^{-2}\big\{s^{-1/2}\|R^{m}\not{\triangle}\mu\|_{s,u}+\int_{t_{0}}^{s}\tau^{-\frac{3}{2}}\|R^{m}\not{\triangle}\mu\|_{\tau,u}d\tau\big\}

(3.190)

\displaystyle\|_{s,u}\lesssim\delta^{5/2-\varepsilon_{0}}+\delta(\delta s^{-\iota}+\delta^{k(1-\varepsilon_{0})})\sqrt{\tilde{E}_{1,\leq m+2}(s,u)}+\delta^{2}\sqrt{\tilde{E}_{2,\leq m+2}(s,u)}

\displaystyle\qquad\ \ +\delta^{(k+1)(1-\varepsilon_{0})+1}(\delta+\delta^{k(1-\varepsilon_{0})})\int_{t_{0}}^{s}\tau^{-3/2}\|R^{m}\not{\triangle}\mu\|_{\tau,u}d\tau

and

\begin{split}\delta^{l}\|\bar{Z}^{m+1}&\mathscr{A}\|_{s,u}\lesssim\delta^{3/2-\varepsilon_{0}}(\delta+\delta^{k(1-\varepsilon_{0})}\ln s)s^{-1}+\delta s^{-\iota}\sqrt{\tilde{E}_{1,\leq m+2}(s,u)}\\

&+\delta^{k(1-\varepsilon_{0})}s^{-1/2}\sqrt{\int_{0}^{u}\tilde{F}_{1}(s,u^{\prime})du^{\prime}}.\end{split}

(3.192)

4
Top order
L^{2}
estimates on the derivatives of
\chi
and
\mu

It follows from Proposition
3.2
that to complete the estimate of
\|Z^{m+1}\mu\|_{s,u}
, it remains to bound
\|R^{m}\not{\triangle}\mu\|_{s,u}
. Furthermore, by the energy estimate (
3.89
) and (
3.90
), one checks easily that the top order derivatives of
\varphi

in the energy estimate for (
3.90
) are of
2N-4
. On the other hand, by the expression of
{\vphantom{D}}{}^{(Z)}D_{\gamma,2}^{m}

in (
3.98
), the top order derivatives of
\chi
and
\mu
in (
3.89
) are of
2N-5
and
2N-4
respectively as in
[
10
]
. However,
it follows from Proposition
3.1
that the
L^{2}
estimates on the
2N-5
top order derivatives of
{\chi}

and the
2N-4
top order derivatives of

\mu
should be controlled by the energies of
\varphi
with the orders
2N-3
, which
is beyond the range of energies containing up to
2N-4
order derivatives of
\varphi
.
To overcome this difficulty, as in
[
6
,
23
]
, also motivated by
[
10
,
11
]
,
we will
treat the smallness orders and the time decay rates of
\textrm{tr}{\chi}
and
\not{\triangle}\mu

with the corresponding top order derivatives. It should be pointed out that although the main ideas here are similar to

[
10
, Section 9]
, yet it seems hard to adopt the analysis in
[
10
]
to close the energies in
Section
6
when
\varepsilon_{0}
is near
\varepsilon_{k}^{*}
in this paper. For instance, the quantity
e
in equation (
3.89
) and the factor
(\mathring{L}G_{\alpha\beta}^{\gamma})\mathring{L}^{\alpha}\mathring{L}^{\beta}\underline{\mathring{L}}\varphi_{\gamma}
in equation (
4.204
) below should be handled more carefully by the null structure (
1.9
) and a new identity (
4.201
). In this case, the resulting new transport
equation (
4.224
) is much more suitable for estimating the
L^{2}
norm of the top derivative of
\textrm{tr}{\chi}
in the paper.

4.1
Estimates on the derivatives of
\not{d}
tr
\chi
and
\not{\nabla}\chi

1.
If there is at least one vector field
\varrho\mathring{L}
in
Z^{m}
, that is,
Z^{m}=Z^{p_{1}}(\varrho\mathring{L})\bar{Z}^{p_{2}}
and
p_{1}+p_{2}=m-1
with
\bar{Z}\in\{T,R\}
, then
it follows from (
2.68
) and Proposition
3.1
that

\displaystyle\delta^{l}\|\not{d}Z^{m}(\textrm{tr}\check{\chi})\|_{s,u}=\delta^{l}\|\not{d}Z^{p_{1}}\bar{Z}^{p_{2}}(\varrho\mathring{L})(\textrm{tr}\check{\chi})+\not{d}Z^{p_{1}}[\varrho\mathring{L},\bar{Z}^{p_{2}}](\textrm{tr}\check{\chi})\|_{s,u}

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle s^{-1}\delta^{l_{1}}\|\not{\mathcal{L}}_{Z}^{m_{1}}\check{\chi}\|_{s,u}+\delta^{k(1-\varepsilon_{0})}s^{-3}\ln s\delta^{l_{1}}(\|\not{\mathcal{L}}_{Z}^{m_{1}}{\vphantom{{(R)}}}{}^{(R)}\not{\pi}_{\mathring{L}}\|_{s,u}+\delta\|\not{\mathcal{L}}_{Z}^{m_{1}}{\vphantom{{(T)}}}{}^{(T)}\not{\pi}_{\mathring{L}}\|_{s,u})

\displaystyle+\delta^{k(1-\varepsilon_{0})}s^{-3}\big\{\delta^{l_{1}}\|Z^{m_{1}}\check{L}^{1}\|_{s,u}+\delta^{l_{1}}\|Z^{m_{1}}\check{L}^{2}\|_{s,u}+s^{-1}\delta^{l_{0}}\|Z^{k_{0}}x\|_{s,u}+\ln s\delta^{l_{1}}\|\not{\mathcal{L}}_{Z}^{m_{1}}\not{g}\|_{s,u}\big\}

\displaystyle+\delta^{(k-1)(1-\varepsilon_{0})}s^{-5/2}\delta^{l_{0}}\|Z^{m_{0}}\varphi\|_{s,u}+\delta^{(k-1)(1-\varepsilon_{0})}s^{-3/2}\delta^{l_{0}}\|\not{d}Z^{m_{0}}\varphi\|_{s,u}

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle\delta^{k(1-\varepsilon_{0})+1/2}s^{-5/2}\ln s+\delta^{(k-1)(1-\varepsilon_{0})}s^{-3/2-\iota}\sqrt{\tilde{E}_{1,\leq m+2}}

\displaystyle+\delta^{k(1-\varepsilon_{0})+\varepsilon_{0}}(1+\delta^{k(1-\varepsilon_{0})-1})s^{-5/2}\ln^{2}s\sqrt{\tilde{E}_{2,\leq m+2}}+\delta^{(k-1)(1-\varepsilon_{0})}s^{-2}\sqrt{\int_{0}^{u}\tilde{F}_{1}(s,u^{\prime})du^{\prime}}.

(4.193)

Thus,

\begin{split}\delta^{l}\|\not{\nabla}\not{\mathcal{L}}_{Z}^{m}\check{\chi}\|_{s,u}\lesssim&\delta^{k(1-\varepsilon_{0})+1/2}s^{-5/2}\ln s+\delta^{(k-1)(1-\varepsilon_{0})}s^{-3/2-\iota}\sqrt{\tilde{E}_{1,\leq m+2}}\\
&+\delta^{k(1-\varepsilon_{0})+\varepsilon_{0}}(1+\delta^{k(1-\varepsilon_{0})-1})s^{-5/2}\ln^{2}s\sqrt{\tilde{E}_{2,\leq m+2}}\\
&+\delta^{(k-1)(1-\varepsilon_{0})}s^{-2}\sqrt{\int_{0}^{u}\tilde{F}_{1}(s,u^{\prime})du^{\prime}}.\end{split}

(4.194)

2.
When all vectorfields
Z^{\prime}
s are in
\{T,R\}
, then (
2.66
) implies

\begin{split}\mathring{L}(\textrm{tr}&{\chi})=-|{\check{\chi}}|^{2}-\frac{2}{\varrho}\text{tr}\chi+\frac{1}{\varrho^{2}}-G_{X\mathring{L}}^{\gamma}\not{d}^{X}\mathring{L}\varphi_{\gamma}+\frac{1}{2}G_{\mathring{L}\mathring{L}}^{\gamma}\not{\triangle}\varphi_{\gamma}+\frac{1}{2}\not{g}^{XX}G_{XX}^{\gamma}\mathring{L}^{2}\varphi_{\gamma}\\
&-\big\{\frac{1}{2}G_{\mathring{L}\mathring{L}}^{\gamma}(\mathring{L}\varphi_{\gamma})+G_{\tilde{T}\mathring{L}}^{\gamma}(\mathring{L}\varphi_{\gamma})-G_{X\mathring{L}}^{\gamma}(\not{d}^{X}\varphi_{\gamma})\big\}\textrm{tr}\chi+f(\varphi,\not{d}x,\mathring{L}^{1},\mathring{L}^{2})\varphi^{k-2}\left(\begin{array}[]{ccc}\mathring{L}\varphi\\
\not{d}\varphi\\
\end{array}\right)^{2},\end{split}

(4.195)

where

\begin{split}&\not{\triangle}\varphi_{\gamma}=\mu^{-1}\big(\mathring{L}\mathring{\underline{L}}\varphi_{\gamma}+\frac{1}{2\varrho}\mathring{\underline{L}}\varphi_{\gamma}-{H}_{\gamma}\big)\end{split}

(4.196)

by (
2.64
).
Note that

\begin{split}G_{X\mathring{L}}^{\gamma}\not{d}^{X}\mathring{L}\varphi_{\gamma}=&\mathring{L}(G_{X\mathring{L}}^{\gamma}\not{d}^{X}\varphi_{\gamma})-(\mathring{L}G_{\alpha\beta}^{\gamma})\mathring{L}^{\beta}\not{d}_{X}x^{\alpha}\not{d}^{X}\varphi-G_{\alpha\beta}(\not{d}_{X}\mathring{L}^{\alpha})\mathring{L}^{\beta}\not{d}^{X}\varphi_{\gamma}\\
&-G_{\alpha\beta}\not{d}_{X}x^{\alpha}(\mathring{L}\mathring{L}^{\beta})\not{d}^{X}\varphi_{\gamma}+2\textrm{tr}\chi(G_{X\mathring{L}}^{\gamma}\not{d}^{X}\varphi_{\gamma}),\end{split}

and the term

can be moved to the left hand of (

).
Analogous treatments can be carried out for

and

\begin{split}\mathring{L}\big(\textrm{tr}\chi-E)=\big(-\frac{2}{\varrho}+\mathcal{E}\big)\textrm{tr}\chi+\frac{1}{\varrho^{2}}-|\check{\chi}|^{2}-\frac{1}{2}\mu^{-1}\mathring{L}(G_{\alpha\beta}^{\gamma})\mathring{L}^{\alpha}\mathring{L}^{\beta}\underline{\mathring{L}}\varphi_{\gamma}+e,\end{split}

where

\begin{split}E=&-G_{X\mathring{L}}^{\gamma}\not{d}^{X}\varphi_{\gamma}+\frac{1}{2}\mu^{-1}G_{\mathring{L}\mathring{L}}^{\gamma}\mathring{\underline{L}}\varphi_{\gamma}+\frac{1}{2}\not{g}^{XX}G_{XX}^{\gamma}\mathring{L}\varphi_{\gamma},\\

\begin{split}e=&\frac{1}{2}\mu^{-2}(G_{\mathring{L}\mathring{L}}^{\gamma}T\varphi_{\gamma})^{2}+f_{1}(\varphi,\not{d}x^{i},\mathring{L}^{i},\not{g})\varphi^{k-2}\left(\begin{array}[]{ccc}\mathring{L}\varphi\\
\not{d}\varphi\\
\end{array}\right)^{2}+f_{2}(\varphi,\not{d}x^{i},\mathring{L}^{i},\not{g})\mu^{-1}\varphi^{2k-2}\mathring{L}\varphi T\varphi\\
&+f_{3}(\varphi,\not{d}x^{i},\mathring{L}^{i},\not{g})\mu^{-1}\varphi^{k-1}(G_{\mathring{L}\mathring{L}}^{\gamma}\not{d}_{X}\varphi_{\gamma})T\varphi+f_{4}(\varphi,\not{d}x^{i},\mathring{L}^{i},\not{g})\mu^{-1}\varphi^{k-1}(G_{\mathring{L}\mathring{L}}^{\gamma}T\varphi_{\gamma})\not{d}\varphi.\end{split}

(4.199)

Here note that for the global estimate on the solution

to (
1.1
) with (

(4.200)

In addition, inserting (

\not{d}^{X}x^{i}\not{d}_{X}\varphi_{i}=\mu^{-1}\underline{\mathring{L}}^{\alpha}\mathring{L}\varphi_{\alpha}=-(\mathring{L}^{\alpha}+2g^{0\alpha})\mathring{L}\varphi_{\alpha},

which, together with (

\begin{split}&G_{\mathring{L}\mathring{L}}^{\gamma}\not{d}_{X}\varphi_{\gamma}=G_{\mathring{L}\mathring{L}}^{\gamma}\not{d}_{X}x^{i}(\partial_{\gamma}\varphi_{i})\\
=&\delta_{\gamma}^{0}G_{\mathring{L}\mathring{L}}^{\gamma}(\not{d}_{X}x^{i}\mathring{L}\varphi_{i})-(G_{\mathring{L}\mathring{L}}^{\gamma}\mathring{L}_{\gamma})\tilde{T}^{j}\not{d}_{X}\varphi_{j}+g_{\gamma j}G_{\mathring{L}\mathring{L}}^{\gamma}\not{d}_{X}x^{j}(\not{d}^{X}x^{i}\not{d}_{X}\varphi_{i})\\
=&\delta_{\gamma}^{0}G_{\mathring{L}\mathring{L}}^{\gamma}(\not{d}_{X}x^{i}\mathring{L}\varphi_{i})-(G_{\mathring{L}\mathring{L}}^{\gamma}\mathring{L}_{\gamma})\tilde{T}^{j}\not{d}_{X}\varphi_{j}-g_{\gamma j}G_{\mathring{L}\mathring{L}}^{\gamma}\not{d}_{X}x^{j}(\mathring{L}^{\alpha}+2g^{0\alpha})\mathring{L}\varphi_{\alpha}.\end{split}

(4.202)

Substituting (

\begin{split}e=&\frac{1}{2}\mu^{-2}(G_{\mathring{L}\mathring{L}}^{\gamma}T\varphi_{\gamma})^{2}+f_{1}(\varphi,\not{d}x,\mathring{L}^{1},\mathring{L}^{2},\not{g})\varphi^{k-2}\left(\begin{array}[]{ccc}\mathring{L}\varphi\\
\not{d}\varphi\\
\end{array}\right)^{2}\\
&+f_{2}(\varphi,\not{d}x,\mathring{L}^{1},\mathring{L}^{2},\not{g})\mu^{-1}\varphi^{2k-2}\mathring{L}\varphi T\varphi+f_{3}(\varphi,\not{d}x,\mathring{L}^{1},\mathring{L}^{2},\not{g})\mu^{-1}\varphi^{k-1}(G_{\mathring{L}\mathring{L}}^{\gamma}\mathring{L}_{\gamma})T\varphi\not{d}\varphi.\end{split}

(4.203)

Let
F^{m}=\not{d}\bar{Z}^{m}\textrm{tr}\chi-\not{d}\bar{Z}^{m}E
with

. Then by an induction

&-\frac{1}{2}\not{d}\bar{Z}^{m}\big(\mu^{-1}\mathring{L}(G_{\alpha\beta}^{\gamma})\mathring{L}^{\alpha}\mathring{L}^{\beta}\underline{\mathring{L}}\varphi_{\gamma}\big)+e^{m},\end{split}

(4.204)

\begin{split}e^{m}=&\not{\mathcal{L}}_{\bar{Z}}^{m}e^{0}+\underbrace{\sum_{p_{1}+p_{2}=m-1}{\tiny{\tiny{\tiny{\tiny}}}}\not{\mathcal{L}}_{\bar{Z}}^{p_{1}}\not{\mathcal{L}}_{[\mathring{L},\bar{Z}]}F^{p_{2}}+\sum_{\tiny\begin{array}[]{c}p_{1}+p_{2}=m\\
p_{1}\geq 1\end{array}}\bar{Z}^{p_{1}}\big(-\frac{2}{\varrho}+\mathcal{E})\not{d}\bar{Z}^{p_{2}}\textrm{tr}\chi}_{\text{vanish when $m=1$}}\end{split}

(4.205)

\begin{split}e^{0}=&\not{d}e+(\not{d}\mathcal{E})\textrm{tr}\chi.\end{split}

(4.206)

Similarly,

G_{\alpha\beta}^{\gamma}=-kg_{\alpha\alpha^{\prime}}g_{\beta\beta^{\prime}}g^{\alpha^{\prime}\beta^{\prime},\gamma\gamma_{2}\cdots\gamma_{k}}\varphi_{\gamma_{2}}\cdots\varphi_{\gamma_{k}}+f(\varphi)\varphi^{k},

then by (

\mathring{L}\mathring{L}^{i}=-\frac{1}{2}(G_{\mathring{L}\mathring{L}}^{\gamma}\mathring{L}_{\gamma})\tilde{T}^{j}\not{d}^{X}x^{i}\not{d}_{X}\varphi_{j}+O(\varphi^{k-1}\mathring{L}\varphi)
from (

\begin{split}\mathring{L}G_{\alpha\beta}^{\gamma}=&-k(k-1)g_{\alpha\alpha^{\prime}}g_{\beta\beta^{\prime}}g^{\alpha^{\prime}\beta^{\prime},\gamma\gamma_{2}\cdots\gamma_{k}}(\mathring{L}\partial_{\gamma_{2}}\phi)\varphi_{\gamma_{3}}\cdots\varphi_{\gamma_{k}}+f(\varphi)\varphi^{k-1}\mathring{L}\varphi\\
=&k(k-1)g_{\alpha\alpha^{\prime}}g_{\beta\beta^{\prime}}g^{\alpha^{\prime}\beta^{\prime},\gamma\gamma_{2}\cdots\gamma_{k}}\mathring{L}_{\gamma_{2}}\tilde{T}^{i_{2}}(\mathring{L}\varphi_{i_{2}})\varphi_{\gamma_{3}}\cdots\varphi_{\gamma_{k}}\\
&-k(k-1)g_{\alpha\alpha^{\prime}}g_{\beta\beta^{\prime}}g^{\alpha^{\prime}\beta^{\prime},\gamma\gamma_{2}\cdots\gamma_{k}}\mathring{L}(\delta_{\gamma_{2}}^{0}\mathring{L}\phi+g_{\gamma_{2}i}\not{d}^{X}x^{i}\not{d}_{X}\phi)\varphi_{\gamma_{3}}\cdots\varphi_{\gamma_{k}}\\
&+f(\varphi,\mathring{L}^{1},\mathring{L}^{2})\varphi^{k-1}\left(\begin{array}[]{ccc}\mathring{L}\varphi\\
(G_{\mathring{L}\mathring{L}}^{\gamma}\mathring{L}_{\gamma})\not{d}^{X}x\not{d}_{X}\varphi\end{array}\right).\end{split}

Applying (
2.48
) to
\varphi_{\gamma_{p}}=\partial_{\gamma_{p}}\phi

(3\leq p\leq k)
again leads to

\begin{split}LG_{\alpha\beta}^{\gamma}=&(-1)^{k}k(k-1)g_{\alpha\alpha^{\prime}}g_{\beta\beta^{\prime}}g^{\alpha^{\prime}\beta^{\prime},\gamma\gamma_{2}\cdots\gamma_{k}}\mathring{L}_{\gamma_{2}}\cdots\mathring{L}_{\gamma_{k}}\tilde{T}^{i_{2}}\cdots\tilde{T}^{i_{k}}(\mathring{L}\varphi_{i_{2}})\varphi_{i_{3}}\cdots\varphi_{i_{k}}\\
&-(-1)^{k}k(k-1)(k-2)g_{\alpha\alpha^{\prime}}g_{\beta\beta^{\prime}}g^{\alpha^{\prime}\beta^{\prime},\gamma\gamma_{2}\cdots\gamma_{k}}\mathring{L}_{\gamma_{2}}\tilde{T}^{i}(\mathring{L}\varphi_{i})(g_{\gamma_{3}j}\not{d}^{X}x^{j}\not{d}_{X}\phi)\\
&\qquad\qquad\qquad\qquad\qquad\cdot\mathring{L}_{\gamma_{4}}\cdots\mathring{L}_{\gamma_{k}}\tilde{T}^{i_{4}}\cdots\tilde{T}^{i_{k}}\varphi_{i_{4}}\cdots\varphi_{i_{k}}\\
&-k(k-1)g_{\alpha\alpha^{\prime}}g_{\beta\beta^{\prime}}g^{\alpha^{\prime}\beta^{\prime},\gamma\gamma_{2}\cdots\gamma_{k}}\mathring{L}(\delta_{\gamma_{2}}^{0}\mathring{L}\phi+g_{\gamma_{2}i}\not{d}^{X}x^{i}\not{d}_{X}\phi)\varphi_{\gamma_{3}}\cdots\varphi_{\gamma_{k}}\\
&+f(\varphi,\mathring{L}^{1},\mathring{L}^{2})\left(\begin{array}[]{ccc}\varphi^{k-1}\mathring{L}\varphi\\
\varphi^{k-1}(G_{\mathring{L}\mathring{L}}^{\gamma}\mathring{L}_{\gamma})\not{d}^{X}x\not{d}_{X}\varphi\\

\begin{split}&(\mathring{L}G_{\alpha\beta}^{\gamma})\mathring{L}^{\alpha}\mathring{L}^{\beta}\underline{\mathring{L}}\varphi_{\gamma}=(\mathring{L}G_{\alpha\beta}^{\gamma})\mathring{L}^{\alpha}\mathring{L}^{\beta}\underline{\mathring{L}}^{\nu}\partial_{\gamma}\varphi_{\nu}\\
