\displaystyle\delta^{k(1-\varepsilon_{0})-\varepsilon_{0}}s^{-5/2}\delta^{l_{0}}\|\not{\mathcal{L}}_{\bar{Z}}^{m_{1}}\check{\chi}\|_{s,u}+s^{-1}\delta^{l_{1}}\|\not{d}\bar{Z}^{m_{1}}\mathcal{E}\|_{s,u}+\delta^{2k(1-\varepsilon_{0})-\varepsilon_{0}}s^{-9/2}\ln s\delta^{l_{1}}\|\not{\mathcal{L}}_{\bar{Z}}^{m_{1}}\not{g}\|_{s,u}

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle\delta^{k(1-\varepsilon_{0})-\varepsilon_{0}+1/2}s^{-3}+\delta^{(k-1)(1-\varepsilon_{0})}s^{-5/2-\iota}\sqrt{\tilde{E}_{1,\leq m+2}}+\delta^{k(1-\varepsilon_{0})}s^{-3}\sqrt{\tilde{E}_{2,\leq m+2}}

\displaystyle+\delta^{k(1-\varepsilon_{0})}(\delta+\delta^{k(1-\varepsilon_{0})})s^{-3}\big\{\|R^{m}\not{\triangle}\mu\|_{s,u}+\delta^{k(1-\varepsilon_{0})-\varepsilon_{0}}\int_{t_{0}}^{s}\tau^{-3/2}\|R^{m}\not{\triangle}\mu\|_{\tau,u}d\tau\big\}

(4.243)

\displaystyle+\delta^{(2k-1)(1-\varepsilon_{0})-1}s^{-3}\sqrt{\int_{0}^{u}\tilde{F}_{1}(s,u^{\prime})du^{\prime}}.

Combining the estimates (
4.233
)-(
4.243
) with (

) yields

\displaystyle\delta^{l}\|\hat{e}^{m}\|_{s,u}

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle\delta^{k(1-\varepsilon_{0})+1/2-\varepsilon_{0}}s^{-3}+\delta^{(k-1)(1-\varepsilon_{0})}s^{-5/2-\iota}\sqrt{\tilde{E}_{1,\leq m+2}}+\delta^{k(1-\varepsilon_{0})}s^{-3}\sqrt{\tilde{E}_{2,\leq m+2}}

\displaystyle+\delta^{k(1-\varepsilon_{0})-1}s^{-2}\delta^{l}\|\mathring{L}\bar{Z}^{m+1}\varphi\|_{s,u}+\delta^{k(1-\varepsilon_{0})}(\delta+\delta^{k(1-\varepsilon_{0})}\ln s)s^{-3}\|R^{m}\not{\triangle}\mu\|_{s,u}

(4.244)

\displaystyle+\delta^{2k(1-\varepsilon_{0})-\varepsilon_{0}}(\delta+\delta^{k(1-\varepsilon_{0})})s^{-3}\int_{t_{0}}^{s}\tau^{-3/2}\|R^{m}\not{\triangle}\mu\|_{\tau,u}d\tau+\delta^{k(1-\varepsilon_{0})}s^{-2}\ln s\delta^{l}\|\hat{F}^{m}\|_{s,u}

\displaystyle+\delta^{(k-1)(1-\varepsilon_{0})}(s^{-1/2}+\delta^{k(1-\varepsilon_{0})-1})s^{-3}\sqrt{\int_{0}^{u}\tilde{F}_{1}(s,u^{\prime})du^{\prime}}.

&+\delta^{k(1-\varepsilon_{0})}s^{-2}\sqrt{\tilde{E}_{2,\leq m+2}}+\delta^{k(1-\varepsilon_{0})}s^{-2}\int_{t_{0}}^{s}(\delta+\delta^{k(1-\varepsilon_{0})}\ln\tau)\tau^{-1/2}\|R^{m}\not{\triangle}\mu\|_{\tau,u}d\tau\\
&+\delta^{k(1-\varepsilon_{0})-1}s^{-3/2-\iota}\sqrt{\int_{0}^{u}\tilde{F}_{1,m+2}(s,u^{\prime})du^{\prime}}.\end{split}

(4.245)

In addition,

holds due to the definition of
\hat{F}^{m}
. Hence, one has by inequalities (

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle\delta^{k(1-\varepsilon_{0})+1/2-\varepsilon_{0}}s^{-2}+\delta^{(k-1)(1-\varepsilon_{0})}s^{-3/2-\iota}\sqrt{\tilde{E}_{1,\leq m+2}}+\delta^{k(1-\varepsilon_{0})}s^{-2}\sqrt{\tilde{E}_{2,\leq m+2}}

\displaystyle+\delta^{k(1-\varepsilon_{0})-1}s^{-3/2-\iota}\sqrt{\int_{0}^{u}\tilde{F}_{1,m+2}(s,u^{\prime})du^{\prime}}+\delta^{k(1-\varepsilon_{0})}(\delta+\delta^{k(1-\varepsilon_{0})}\ln s)s^{-2}\|R^{m}\not{\triangle}\mu\|_{(s,u)}

\displaystyle+\delta^{k(1-\varepsilon_{0})}s^{-2}\int_{t_{0}}^{s}(\delta+\delta^{k(1-\varepsilon_{0})}\ln\tau)\tau^{-1/2}\|R^{m}\not{\triangle}\mu\|_{\tau,u}d\tau.

\displaystyle\delta^{k(1-\varepsilon_{0})+1/2-\varepsilon_{0}}s^{-2}+\delta^{(k-1)(1-\varepsilon_{0})}s^{-3/2-\iota}\sqrt{\tilde{E}_{1,\leq m+2}}+\delta^{k(1-\varepsilon_{0})}s^{-2}\sqrt{\tilde{E}_{2,\leq m+2}}

\displaystyle+\delta^{k(1-\varepsilon_{0})-1}s^{-3/2-\iota}\sqrt{\int_{0}^{u}\tilde{F}_{1,m+2}(s,u^{\prime})du^{\prime}}+\delta^{k(1-\varepsilon_{0})}(\delta+\delta^{k(1-\varepsilon_{0})}\ln s)s^{-2}\|R^{m}\not{\triangle}\mu\|_{(s,u)}

4.2
Estimates on the derivatives of
\not{\triangle}\mu

Although the main idea in this subsection parallels that in
[
10
, Section 9.2]
, in order to
close the energy estimate at the beginning of Section
6
as
\varepsilon_{0}
approaches
\varepsilon_{k}^{*}
,
we need to obtain more refined estimates for each term in the transport equation under
the general higher null condition (for instance, see (
4.285
) below).

Similarly to the analysis in
[
10
, Section 9.2]
, one can set

\begin{split}\tilde{E}=&-\frac{1}{2}\mu(G_{\mathring{L}\mathring{L}}^{\gamma}+2G_{\tilde{T}\mathring{L}}^{\gamma})\not{\triangle}\varphi_{\gamma}+\frac{1}{2}\mu^{-1}G_{\mathring{L}\mathring{L}}^{\gamma}T\underline{\mathring{L}}\varphi_{\gamma},\\
\tilde{\mathcal{E}}=&-\frac{1}{2}G^{\gamma}_{\mathring{L}\mathring{L}}\mathring{L}\varphi_{\gamma}-G^{\gamma}_{\tilde{T}\mathring{L}}\mathring{L}\varphi_{\gamma},\\
\tilde{F}=&\not{\triangle}\mu-\tilde{E}.\end{split}

(4.248)

Then it holds that

\begin{split}\mathring{L}\tilde{F}=&-2\textrm{tr}\check{\chi}\tilde{F}+(-\frac{2}{\varrho}+\tilde{\mathcal{E}})\tilde{F}+(\not{d}^{X}\textrm{tr}\chi)\mathcal{Q}_{X}+\tilde{e}\end{split}

with

\displaystyle\mathcal{Q}_{X}=

\displaystyle-\not{d}_{X}\mu-\mu G^{\gamma}_{X\tilde{T}}\mathring{L}\varphi_{\gamma}-\mu G^{\gamma}_{\tilde{T}\mathring{L}}\not{d}_{X}\varphi_{\gamma}-\mu G^{\gamma}_{\mathring{L}\mathring{L}}\not{d}_{X}\varphi_{\gamma}+G^{\gamma}_{X\mathring{L}}T\varphi_{\gamma}

and

\displaystyle\tilde{e}=

\displaystyle 2(\not{d}^{X}\mu)\not{d}_{X}\tilde{\mathcal{E}}+(-2\textrm{tr}\check{\chi}-\frac{2}{\varrho}+\tilde{\mathcal{E}})\tilde{E}-\frac{1}{2}\mathring{L}(\mu^{-1}G_{\mathring{L}\mathring{L}}^{\gamma})T\underline{\mathring{L}}\varphi_{\gamma}

\displaystyle-\frac{1}{2}\mu^{-1}G_{\mathring{L}\mathring{L}}^{\gamma}\big\{T\mu(\not{\triangle}\varphi_{\gamma})+{\vphantom{{(T)}}}^{(T)}\not{\pi}_{\mathring{L}}^{X}\not{d}_{X}\underline{\mathring{L}}\varphi_{\gamma}-\frac{1}{2\varrho^{2}}\underline{\mathring{L}}\varphi_{\gamma}-\frac{1}{2\varrho}T\underline{\mathring{L}}\varphi_{\gamma}+TH_{\gamma}\big\}

\displaystyle+f_{1}(\varphi,\mathring{L}^{1},\mathring{L}^{2},\not{d}x,\varrho\not{\triangle}x)\left(\begin{array}[]{ccc}\mu\mathring{L}\varphi\\
\mu\not{d}\varphi\\
T\varphi\\
\mu\varphi\textrm{tr}\chi\end{array}\right)\left(\begin{array}[]{ccc}\varphi^{k-2}\not{\triangle}\varphi\\
\varphi^{k-1}\varrho^{-1}\not{\triangle}x\\
\varphi^{k-1}\textrm{tr}\check{\chi}\not{\triangle}x\end{array}\right)

\displaystyle+f_{2}(\varphi,\mathring{L}^{1},\mathring{L}^{2},\varrho\not{d}\mathring{L}^{1},\varrho\not{d}\mathring{L}^{2},\varrho\not{\triangle}x)\varphi^{k-3}\left(\begin{array}[]{ccc}\varrho^{-1}\varphi^{k+1}\\
\not{d}\varphi\\
\varphi\not{d}\mathring{L}^{1}\\
\varphi\not{d}\mathring{L}^{2}\end{array}\right)\left(\begin{array}[]{ccc}\not{d}\varphi\\
\mathring{L}\varphi\\
\varphi\not{d}\mathring{L}^{1}\\
\varphi\not{d}\mathring{L}^{2}\end{array}\right)\left(\begin{array}[]{ccc}\mu\mathring{L}\varphi\\
\mu\not{d}\varphi\\
T\varphi\end{array}\right)

\displaystyle+f_{3}(\varphi,\mathring{L}^{1},\mathring{L}^{2},\not{d}x)\not{d}^{X}\mu\not{d}_{X}\varphi\left(\begin{array}[]{ccc}\varphi^{2(k-1)}\not{d}\varphi\\
\varphi^{2(k-1)}\mathring{L}\varphi\\
\varphi^{k-1}\textrm{tr}\chi\end{array}\right)+f_{4}(\varphi,\mathring{L}^{1},\mathring{L}^{2},\not{d}x)\left(\begin{array}[]{ccc}\not{d}T\varphi\\
\mu\not{d}\mathring{L}\varphi\end{array}\right)\left(\begin{array}[]{ccc}\varphi^{k-2}\not{d}\varphi\\
\varphi^{k-1}\not{d}\mathring{L}^{1}\\
\varphi^{k-1}\not{d}\mathring{L}^{2}\end{array}\right).

Analogously to the introduction of
F^{k}
in Subsection

, one can set

with

\begin{split}\mathring{L}\tilde{F}^{m}=&(-2\textrm{tr}\check{\chi}-\frac{2}{\varrho}+\tilde{\mathcal{E}})\tilde{F}^{m}+(\not{d}^{X}{\bar{Z}}^{k}\textrm{tr}\chi)\mathcal{Q}_{X}+{\vphantom{{(\bar{Z})}}}^{(\bar{Z})}\not{\pi}_{\mathring{L}}^{X}(\not{d}_{X}\tilde{F}^{m-1})+\tilde{e}^{m},\end{split}

(4.275)

where

\begin{split}\tilde{e}^{m}=&\underbrace{\sum_{{\mbox{\tiny$\begin{array}[]{c}m_{1}+m_{2}=m-1\\
m_{1}\geq 1\end{array}$}}}(\not{\mathcal{L}}_{\bar{Z}}^{m_{1}}{\vphantom{{(\bar{Z})}}}{}^{(\bar{Z})}\not{\pi}_{\mathring{L}}^{X})(\not{d}_{X}\tilde{F}^{m_{2}})}_{\textrm{vanish when}\ m=1}+\bar{Z}^{m}\tilde{e}\\
&+\sum_{{\mbox{\tiny$\begin{array}[]{c}m_{1}+m_{2}=m\\

(4.276)

As (

T^{2}\varphi_{\gamma}=T(\mu\delta_{\gamma}^{0}\tilde{T}^{i}\mathring{L}\varphi_{i}-\mathring{L}_{\gamma}\tilde{T}^{i}T\varphi_{i}+\mu\tilde{T}^{i}g_{\gamma j}\not{d}^{X}x^{j}\not{d}_{X}\varphi_{i})

\displaystyle+\delta^{2k(1-\varepsilon_{0})-1-\varepsilon_{0}}(\delta+\delta^{k(1-\varepsilon_{0})})s^{-1}\int_{t_{0}}^{s}\tau^{-3/2}\|R^{m}\not{\triangle}\mu\|_{\tau,u}d\tau

(4.278)

\displaystyle+\delta^{(2k-1)(1-\varepsilon_{0})-2}s^{-1}\sqrt{\int_{0}^{u}\tilde{F}_{1,m+2}(s,u^{\prime})du^{\prime}}.

(4.280)

\displaystyle+\delta^{(2k-1)(1-\varepsilon_{0})-2}s^{-1}\sqrt{\int_{0}^{u}\tilde{F}_{1,m+2}(s,u^{\prime})du^{\prime}}.

\displaystyle+

It follows from

\displaystyle\delta^{l}\|\bar{Z}^{m}\big(\mathring{L}(\mu^{-1}G_{\mathring{L}\mathring{L}}^{\gamma})T^{2}\varphi_{\gamma}\big)\|_{s,u}

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle\delta^{k(1-\varepsilon_{0})-\varepsilon_{0}}s^{-3/2}\delta^{l_{1}}\|\bar{Z}^{m_{1}}(\mu^{-1}G_{\mathring{L}\mathring{L}}^{\gamma}T^{2}\varphi_{\gamma})\|_{s,u}+\delta^{k(1-\varepsilon_{0})-1-\varepsilon_{0}}s^{-5/2}\delta^{l_{0}}\|\bar{Z}^{m_{0}}\mu\|_{s,u}

\displaystyle+\delta^{k(1-\varepsilon_{0})-1}s^{-2}\delta^{l}\|TZ^{m+1}\varphi\|_{s,u}+\delta^{k(1-\varepsilon_{0})-\varepsilon_{0}}s^{-7/2}\delta^{l_{1}}\|\not{\mathcal{L}}_{\bar{Z}}^{m_{1}}{\vphantom{\not{\pi}}}{}^{(R)}\not{\pi}_{T}\|_{s,u}

\displaystyle+\delta^{k(1-\varepsilon_{0})-2}s^{-2}\delta^{l_{0}}\big(\|\bar{Z}^{m_{0}}\check{L}^{1}\|_{s,u}+\|\bar{Z}^{m_{0}}\check{L}^{2}\|_{s,u}+\|\bar{Z}^{m_{0}}\check{\varrho}\|_{s,u}+\|Z^{m_{0}}\varphi\|_{s,u}\big)

\displaystyle+\delta^{(k-1)(1-\varepsilon_{0})-2}s^{-5/2}\delta^{l_{0}}\|Z^{m_{0}}\phi\|_{s,u}+\delta^{(k+1)(1-\varepsilon_{0})-2}s^{-7/2}\delta^{l_{-1}}\|\bar{Z}^{m_{-1}}x\|_{s,u}

\displaystyle+\delta^{(k-1)(1-\varepsilon_{0})-2}s^{-1/2}\delta^{l_{1}}\big(\|\bar{Z}^{m_{1}}\mathring{L}^{2}\phi\|_{s,u}+\|\not{d}\bar{Z}^{m_{1}}\mathring{L}\phi\|_{s,u}\big)

\displaystyle+\delta^{k(1-\varepsilon_{0})-1}s^{-3}\delta^{l_{0}}\|\not{\mathcal{L}}_{\bar{Z}}^{m_{0}}\not{g}\|_{s,u}+\delta^{k(1-\varepsilon_{0})-1}s^{-2}\delta^{l_{1}}\|\not{\mathcal{L}}_{\bar{Z}}^{m_{1}}\check{\chi}\|_{s,u}

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle\delta^{k(1-\varepsilon_{0})-1/2-\varepsilon_{0}}s^{-2}+\delta^{(k-1)(1-\varepsilon_{0})-1}s^{-3/2-\iota}\sqrt{\tilde{E}_{1,\leq m+2}}+\delta^{k(1-\varepsilon_{0})-1}s^{-2}\sqrt{\tilde{E}_{2,\leq m+2}}

(4.285)

\displaystyle+\delta^{k(1-\varepsilon_{0})-1}(\delta+\delta^{k(1-\varepsilon_{0})})s^{-2}\delta^{l}\|\tilde{F}^{m}\|_{s,u}+\delta^{(2k-1)(1-\varepsilon_{0})-2}s^{-2}\sqrt{\int_{0}^{u}\tilde{F}_{1,m+2}(s,u^{\prime})du^{\prime}}

\displaystyle+\delta^{2k(1-\varepsilon_{0})-1-\varepsilon_{0}}(\delta+\delta^{k(1-\varepsilon_{0})})s^{-2}\int_{t_{0}}^{s}\tau^{-3/2}\delta^{l}\|\tilde{F}^{m}\|_{\tau,u}d\tau.

Note that (
4.2
) and (
2.65
) imply that
\bar{Z}^{m}\tilde{e}
contains the term

\frac{1}{2}\mu^{-1}(G_{\mathring{L}\mathring{L}}^{\gamma}T\varphi_{\gamma})\bar{Z}^{m}T\text{tr}\check{\chi}
, which equals
\frac{1}{2}\mu^{-1}(G_{\mathring{L}\mathring{L}}^{\gamma}T\varphi_{\gamma})\bar{Z}^{m}\not{\triangle}\mu+\cdots
by (
2.69
).
Thus,
it follows from Propositions
3.1
,
3.2
, (
4.278
), (
4.280
) and (
4.285
) that

\displaystyle\delta^{l}\|\bar{Z}^{m}\tilde{e}\|_{s,u}

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle\delta^{k(1-\varepsilon_{0})-1-\varepsilon_{0}}\big(s^{-3}\delta^{l_{0}}\|\bar{Z}^{m_{0}}\mu\|_{s,u}+s^{-3/2}\delta^{l_{1}}\|\not{\mathcal{L}}_{\bar{Z}}^{m_{1}}\check{\chi}\|_{s,u}\big)+s^{-1}\delta^{l_{1}}\|\bar{Z}^{m_{1}}\tilde{E}\|_{s,u}

\displaystyle+\delta^{l_{1}}\|\bar{Z}^{m_{1}}\big(\mathring{L}(\mu^{-1}G_{\mathring{L}\mathring{L}}^{\gamma})T^{2}\varphi_{\gamma}\big)\|_{s,u}+s^{-1}\delta^{l_{1}}\|\bar{Z}^{m_{1}}(\mu^{-1}G_{\mathring{L}\mathring{L}}^{\gamma}T^{2}\varphi_{\gamma})\|_{s,u}

\displaystyle+\delta^{k(1-\varepsilon_{0})-1}s^{-3}\delta^{l_{1}}\|\not{\mathcal{L}}_{\bar{Z}}^{m_{1}}\not{g}\|_{s,u}+\delta^{k(1-\varepsilon_{0})-1}s^{-2}\delta^{l_{1}}\|\not{\mathcal{L}}_{\bar{Z}}^{m_{1}}{\vphantom{\not{\pi}}}{}^{(T)}\not{\pi}_{\mathring{L}}\|_{s,u}

\displaystyle+\delta^{k(1-\varepsilon_{0})-1}(\delta^{k(1-\varepsilon_{0})-1}+1)s^{-3}(\delta^{l_{0}}\|\bar{Z}^{m_{0}}\check{L}^{1}\|_{s,u}+\delta^{l_{0}}\|\bar{Z}^{m_{0}}\check{L}^{2}\|_{s,u}+s^{-1}\delta^{l_{-1}}\|\bar{Z}^{m_{-1}}x\|_{s,u})

\displaystyle+\delta^{2k(1-\varepsilon_{0})-\varepsilon_{0}}s^{-7/2}\ln s\delta^{l_{1}}\|\not{\mathcal{L}}_{\bar{Z}}^{m_{1}}{\vphantom{\not{\pi}}}{}^{(T)}\not{\pi}\|_{s,u}+\delta^{k(1-\varepsilon_{0})-\varepsilon_{0}}s^{-3/2}\delta^{l_{1}}\|\bar{Z}^{m_{1}}\not{\triangle}\mu\|_{s,u}

\displaystyle+\delta^{(k-1)(1-\varepsilon_{0})-1}s^{-3/2-\iota}\sqrt{\tilde{E}_{1,\leq m+2}}+\delta^{k(1-\varepsilon_{0})-1}s^{-2}\sqrt{\tilde{E}_{2,\leq m+2}}

\displaystyle+\delta^{k(1-\varepsilon_{0})}s^{-5}(\delta^{k(1-\varepsilon_{0})-1}\ln s+1)\delta^{l_{1}}\|\not{\mathcal{L}}_{\bar{Z}}^{m_{1}}{\vphantom{\not{\pi}}}{}^{(R)}\not{\pi}_{T}\|_{s,u}

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle\delta^{k(1-\varepsilon_{0})-1/2-\varepsilon_{0}}s^{-2}+\delta^{(k-1)(1-\varepsilon_{0})-1}s^{-3/2-\iota}\sqrt{\tilde{E}_{1,\leq m+2}}+\delta^{k(1-\varepsilon_{0})-1}s^{-2}\sqrt{\tilde{E}_{2,\leq m+2}}

\displaystyle+\delta^{k(1-\varepsilon_{0})-\varepsilon_{0}}s^{-3/2}\delta^{l}\|\tilde{F}^{m}\|_{s,u}+\delta^{2k(1-\varepsilon_{0})-1-\varepsilon_{0}}(\delta+\delta^{k(1-\varepsilon_{0})})s^{-2}\int_{t_{0}}^{s}\tau^{-3/2}\delta^{l}\|\tilde{F}^{m}\|_{\tau,u}d\tau

\displaystyle+\delta^{(2k-1)(1-\varepsilon_{0})-2}s^{-2}\sqrt{\int_{0}^{u}\tilde{F}_{1,m+2}(s,u^{\prime})du^{\prime}}.

Therefore, it holds that

\displaystyle\delta^{l}\|\tilde{e}^{m}\|_{s,u}

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle\delta^{k(1-\varepsilon_{0})-1/2-\varepsilon_{0}}s^{-2}+\delta^{(k-1)(1-\varepsilon_{0})-1}s^{-3/2-\iota}\sqrt{\tilde{E}_{1,\leq m+2}}+\delta^{k(1-\varepsilon_{0})-1}s^{-2}\sqrt{\tilde{E}_{2,\leq m+2}}

\displaystyle+\delta^{k(1-\varepsilon_{0})-\varepsilon_{0}}s^{-\frac{3}{2}}\delta^{l}\|\tilde{F}^{m}\|_{s,u}+\delta^{2k(1-\varepsilon_{0})-1-\varepsilon_{0}}(\delta+\delta^{k(1-\varepsilon_{0})})s^{-2}\int_{t_{0}}^{s}\tau^{-3/2}\delta^{l}\|\tilde{F}^{m}\|_{\tau,u}d\tau

\displaystyle+\delta^{(2k-1)(1-\varepsilon_{0})-2}s^{-2}\sqrt{\int_{0}^{u}\tilde{F}_{1,m+2}(s,u^{\prime})du^{\prime}}.

(4.286)

Substituting (
4.282
) and (
4.286
) into (
4.281
) and then applying Gronwall’s inequality yield

\begin{split}\delta^{l}\|\tilde{F}^{m}\|_{s,u}\lesssim&\delta^{k(1-\varepsilon_{0})-1/2-\varepsilon_{0}}s^{-1}+\delta^{(k-1)(1-\varepsilon_{0})-1}s^{-1/2-\iota}\sqrt{\tilde{E}_{1,\leq m+2}}\\
+\delta^{k(1-\varepsilon_{0})-1}&s^{-1}\sqrt{\tilde{E}_{2,\leq m+2}}+\delta^{(2k-1)(1-\varepsilon_{0})-2}s^{-1}\sqrt{\int_{0}^{u}\tilde{F}_{1,m+2}(s,u^{\prime})du^{\prime}}\end{split}

and further

\begin{split}\delta^{l}\|\bar{Z}^{m}\not{\triangle}\mu\|_{s,u}\lesssim&\delta^{k(1-\varepsilon_{0})-1/2-\varepsilon_{0}}s^{-1}+\delta^{(k-1)(1-\varepsilon_{0})-1}s^{-1/2-\iota}\sqrt{\tilde{E}_{1,\leq m+2}}\\
+\delta^{k(1-\varepsilon_{0})-1}&s^{-1}\sqrt{\tilde{E}_{2,\leq m+2}}+\delta^{(2k-1)(1-\varepsilon_{0})-2}s^{-1}\sqrt{\int_{0}^{u}\tilde{F}_{1,m+2}(s,u^{\prime})du^{\prime}}\end{split}

with the help of (
4.280
).

For the other cases containing at least one
\varrho\mathring{L}
in
Z^{m}
, one can make use of the
commutators
[\varrho\mathring{L},\bar{Z}]
and
[\varrho\mathring{L},\not{\triangle}]
and subsequently
utilize the transport equation (
2.36
)
to obtain the related
L^{2}
norm estimates.
Therefore, we eventually arrive at

\begin{split}\delta^{l}\|Z^{m}\not{\triangle}\mu\|_{s,u}\lesssim&\delta^{k(1-\varepsilon_{0})-1/2-\varepsilon_{0}}s^{-1}+\delta^{(k-1)(1-\varepsilon_{0})-1}s^{-1/2-\iota}\sqrt{\tilde{E}_{1,\leq m+2}}\\
&+\delta^{k(1-\varepsilon_{0})-1}s^{-1}\sqrt{\tilde{E}_{2,\leq m+2}}+\delta^{(2k-1)(1-\varepsilon_{0})-2}s^{-1}\sqrt{\int_{0}^{u}\tilde{F}_{1,m+2}(s,u^{\prime})du^{\prime}}.\end{split}

(4.287)

And hence, it follows from (
4.247
) and (
4.287
) that

\begin{split}&\delta^{l}\|\not{d}Z^{m}\textrm{tr}\chi\|_{s,u}+\delta^{l}\|\not{\nabla}{\not{\mathcal{L}}}_{Z}^{m}\check{\chi}\|_{s,u}\\
\lesssim&\delta^{k(1-\varepsilon_{0})+1/2-\varepsilon_{0}}s^{-2}+\delta^{(k-1)(1-\varepsilon_{0})}s^{-3/2-\iota}\sqrt{\tilde{E}_{1,\leq m+2}}+\delta^{k(1-\varepsilon_{0})}s^{-2}\sqrt{\tilde{E}_{2,\leq m+2}}\\
&+\delta^{k(1-\varepsilon_{0})-1}s^{-3/2-\iota}\sqrt{\int_{0}^{u}\tilde{F}_{1,m+2}(s,u^{\prime})du^{\prime}}.\end{split}

(4.288)

5
Estimates for the error terms

After all the preparations for the optimal
L^{2}
estimates on the related quantities in Sections
3
and
4
, we are ready to
handle the error terms
\delta\int_{D^{s,u}}|\Phi\cdot\mathring{\underline{L}}\Psi|
and

\int_{D^{s,u}}\varrho^{2\iota}|\Phi\cdot\mathring{L}\Psi|
in (
3.89
), and then complete the final energy estimates for
\varphi
.
Although the main strategy is analogous to that in
[
10
]
, due to the slow time decay of the
solutions to the 2D wave equations
and the requirement on the optimal smallness exponent of short pulse data, we still give all the details since many precise estimates
in Sections
3
-
4
derived from the higher order null condition (
1.9
) will be applied (for instance,
one can see (
5.302
), (
5.303
) and Remark
5.1
below for details).

For
\Psi=Z^{m+1}\varphi_{\gamma}
in (
3.81
), then the corresponding
\Phi
is just
\Phi_{\gamma}^{m+1}
which has been explicitly
given in (
3.92
).
One can now deal with each term in (
3.92
) as follows.

5.1
Treatment on
J_{1}^{m+1}

This subsection deals with the term
J_{1}^{m+1}
in (
3.92
).
First, expand
Z^{m+1}
as
Z_{m+1}Z_{m}\cdots Z_{2}Z_{1}
with
Z_{i}\in\{\varrho\mathring{L},T,R\}
and set
\varphi_{\gamma}^{n}=\begin{cases}Z_{n}\cdots Z_{1}\varphi_{\gamma},\ &n\geq 1,\\
\varphi_{\gamma},\ &n=0.\end{cases}

(1)

Due to form of
\Phi_{\gamma}^{m+1}
, it is necessary to estimate the derivatives of
\mu\mathscr{D}^{\alpha}{{\vphantom{C}}{}^{(Z)}C_{\gamma}^{n}}_{,\alpha}

(0\leq n\leq m)
. To this end, one treats first
{\vphantom{D}}{}^{(Z)}D_{\gamma,1}^{n}
and
{\vphantom{D}}{}^{(Z)}D_{\gamma,3}^{n}
in (
3.96
), which do not contain the top order derivatives of
\varphi_{\gamma}
.
In fact, substituting (
A.2
)-(
A.4
) in Appendix A into (
3.97
) and (
3.99
) yields directly

{\vphantom{D}}{}^{(T)}D_{\gamma,1}^{n}=

\displaystyle(T\mu)\mathring{L}^{2}\varphi_{\gamma}^{n}+\mu(\not{d}_{X}\mu+2\mu\zeta_{X})\not{d}^{X}\mathring{L}\varphi_{\gamma}^{n}+\frac{1}{2}\textrm{tr}{\vphantom{\not{\pi}}}^{(T)}\not{\pi}(\mathring{L}\mathring{\underline{L}}\varphi_{\gamma}^{n}+\frac{1}{2}\textrm{tr}\chi\mathring{\underline{L}}\varphi_{\gamma}^{n})

\displaystyle+(\not{d}_{X}\mu+2\mu\zeta_{X})\not{d}^{X}\mathring{\underline{L}}\varphi_{\gamma}^{n}-(T\mu)\not{\triangle}\varphi_{\gamma}^{n}+\frac{1}{2}\mu\text{tr}{\vphantom{\not{\pi}}}^{(T)}\not{\pi}\not{\triangle}\varphi_{\gamma}^{n},

(5.289)

{\vphantom{D}}{}^{(T)}D_{\gamma,3}^{n}=

\displaystyle\big\{\textrm{tr}\chi T\mu+\frac{1}{4}(\mu\textrm{tr}\chi+\textrm{tr}{\vphantom{\not{\pi}}}^{(T)}\not{\pi})\textrm{tr}{\vphantom{\not{\pi}}}^{(T)}\not{\pi}-\frac{1}{2}|\not{d}\mu|^{2}-\mu\zeta_{X}(\not{d}^{X}\mu)\big\}\mathring{L}\varphi_{\gamma}^{n}

\displaystyle+(\frac{1}{2}\mathring{L}\mu-\mu\textrm{tr}\chi)(\not{d}_{X}\mu+2\mu\zeta_{X})\not{d}^{X}\varphi_{\gamma}^{n},

(5.290)

{\vphantom{D}}{}^{(\varrho\mathring{L})}D_{\gamma,1}^{n}=

\displaystyle(2-\mu+\varrho\mathring{L}\mu)\mathring{L}^{2}\varphi_{\gamma}^{n}-2\varrho(\not{d}_{X}\mu+2\mu\zeta_{X})\not{d}^{X}\mathring{L}\varphi_{\gamma}^{n}+\varrho\textrm{tr}\chi(\mathring{L}\mathring{\underline{L}}\varphi_{\gamma}^{n}+\frac{1}{2}\textrm{tr}\chi\mathring{\underline{L}}\varphi_{\gamma}^{n})

\displaystyle-(\varrho\mathring{L}\mu-\varrho\mu\textrm{tr}\check{\chi})\not{\triangle}\varphi_{\gamma}^{n},

(5.291)

{\vphantom{D}}{}^{(\varrho\mathring{L})}D_{\gamma,3}^{n}=

\displaystyle\textrm{tr}\chi\big\{2-\mu+\varrho\mathring{L}\mu+\frac{1}{2}\varrho\text{tr}{\vphantom{\not{\pi}}}^{(T)}\not{\pi}+\frac{1}{2}\varrho\mu\textrm{tr}\chi\big\}\mathring{L}\varphi_{\gamma}^{n}+2\varrho\textrm{tr}\chi(2\mu\zeta^{X}+\not{d}^{X}\mu)\not{d}_{X}\varphi_{\gamma}^{n},

(5.292)

{\vphantom{D}}{}^{(R)}D_{\gamma,1}^{n}=

\displaystyle(R\mu)\mathring{L}^{2}\varphi_{\gamma}^{n}-{\vphantom{\not{\pi}}}^{(R)}\not{\pi}_{\mathring{\underline{L}}X}\not{d}^{X}\mathring{L}\varphi_{\gamma}^{n}+\frac{1}{2}\textrm{tr}{\vphantom{\not{\pi}}}^{(R)}\not{\pi}(\mathring{L}\mathring{\underline{L}}\varphi_{\gamma}^{n}+\frac{1}{2}\textrm{tr}\chi\mathring{\underline{L}}\varphi_{\gamma}^{n})-{\vphantom{\not{\pi}}}^{(R)}\not{\pi}_{\mathring{L}X}\not{d}^{X}\mathring{\underline{L}}\varphi_{\gamma}^{n}

\displaystyle-(R\mu)\not{\triangle}\varphi_{\gamma}^{n}+\frac{1}{2}\mu\text{tr}{\vphantom{\not{\pi}}}^{(R)}\not{\pi}\not{\triangle}\varphi_{\gamma}^{n},

(5.293)

{\vphantom{D}}{}^{(R)}D_{\gamma,3}^{n}=

\displaystyle\big\{\textrm{tr}\chi R\mu+\frac{1}{4}(\textrm{tr}{\vphantom{\not{\pi}}}^{(T)}\not{\pi}+\mu\textrm{tr}\chi)\textrm{tr}{\vphantom{\not{\pi}}}^{(R)}\not{\pi}+\frac{1}{2}\not{d}^{X}\mu{\vphantom{\not{\pi}}}^{(R)}\not{\pi}_{\mathring{L}X}\big\}\mathring{L}\varphi_{\gamma}^{n}+\big\{\textrm{tr}\chi{\vphantom{\not{\pi}}}^{(R)}\not{\pi}_{TX}

\displaystyle+\textrm{tr}{\vphantom{\not{\pi}}}^{(R)}\not{\pi}(\mu\zeta_{X}+\frac{1}{2}\not{d}_{X}\mu)+(-\frac{1}{2}\mathring{L}\mu+\mu\text{tr}\chi+\frac{1}{2}\text{tr}{\vphantom{\not{\pi}}}^{(T)}\not{\pi}){\vphantom{\not{\pi}}}^{(R)}\not{\pi}_{\mathring{L}X}\big\}\not{d}^{X}\varphi_{\gamma}^{n}.

(5.294)

Note that it follows from (
5.289
), (
5.291
), and (
5.293
) that each of
{\vphantom{D}}{}^{(Z)}D_{\gamma,1}^{n}
contains the factor
\mathring{L}\mathring{\underline{L}}\varphi_{\gamma}^{n}+\frac{1}{2}\textrm{tr}\chi\mathring{\underline{L}}\varphi_{\gamma}^{n}
which can be estimated by using (
2.64
) as explained after (
3.99
).
It follows easily from the expression of
J_{1}^{m+1}
that at most

(m-n)-
order derivatives appear in
{\vphantom{D}}{}^{(Z)}D_{\gamma,i}^{n}

(i=1,3)
.
Thus, the
L^{2}
norms of all terms in
J_{1}^{m+1}
can be estimated by
the corresponding
L^{\infty}
estimates in Subsection
2.2

and the related
L^{2}
estimates in Proposition
3.1
, which do not depend on the estimates of top order derivatives
in Section
4
.
Therefore,

\displaystyle\delta^{2l+1}|\int_{D^{s,u}}\sum_{p=1}^{m}\big(Z_{m+1}+{\vphantom{\lambda}}^{(Z_{m+1})}\lambda\big)\dots\big(Z_{m+2-p}+{\vphantom{\lambda}}^{(Z_{m+2-p})}\lambda\big){\vphantom{D}}^{(Z_{m+1-p})}D_{\gamma,1}^{m-p}\cdot\mathring{\underline{L}}\varphi_{\gamma}^{m+1}|

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle\delta^{2l+1}\int_{t_{0}}^{s}\sum_{p=1}^{m}\|\big(Z_{m+1}+{\vphantom{\lambda}}^{(Z_{m+1})}\lambda\big)\dots\big(Z_{m+2-p}+{\vphantom{\lambda}}^{(Z_{m+2-p})}\lambda\big){\vphantom{D}}^{(Z_{m+1-p})}D_{\gamma,1}^{m-p}\|_{\tau,u}

\displaystyle\qquad\qquad\cdot\|\mathring{\underline{L}}Z^{m+1}\varphi_{\gamma}\|_{\tau,u}d\tau

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle\delta^{6-6\varepsilon_{0}}+\int_{t_{0}}^{s}\tau^{-1-\iota}\tilde{E}_{1,\leq m+2}(\tau,u)d\tau+\delta\int_{t_{0}}^{s}\tau^{-1-\iota}\tilde{E}_{2,\leq m+2}(\tau,u)d\tau+\delta^{-1}{\int_{0}^{u}\tilde{F}_{1,m+2}(s,u^{\prime})du^{\prime}}.

(5.295)

Similarly,

\displaystyle\delta^{2l+1}|\int_{D^{s,u}}\sum_{p=1}^{m}\big(Z_{m+1}+{\vphantom{\lambda}}^{(Z_{m+1})}\lambda\big)\dots\big(Z_{m+2-p}+{\vphantom{\lambda}}^{(Z_{m+2-p})}\lambda\big){\vphantom{D}}^{(Z_{m+1-p})}D_{\gamma,3}^{m-p}\cdot\mathring{\underline{L}}Z^{m+1}\varphi_{\gamma}|

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle\delta^{4-2\varepsilon_{0}}+\delta^{2(k+1)(1-\varepsilon_{0})}+\int_{t_{0}}^{s}\tau^{-2}\tilde{E}_{1,\leq m+2}(\tau,u)d\tau+\delta\int_{t_{0}}^{s}\tau^{-2}\ln\tau\tilde{E}_{2,\leq m+2}(\tau,u)d\tau

(5.296)

\displaystyle+\delta^{-1}{\int_{0}^{u}\tilde{F}_{1,m+2}(s,u^{\prime})du^{\prime}}.

The terms in
\delta^{2l}\int_{D^{s,u}}\varrho^{2\iota}|\Phi\cdot\mathring{L}\Psi|

related to the integrand factors
{\vphantom{D}}{}^{(Z)}D_{\gamma,i}^{n}

(i=1,3)
can also be estimated as follows

\displaystyle\delta^{2l}\int_{D^{s,u}}|\sum_{p=1}^{m}\varrho^{2\iota}\big(Z_{m+1}+{\vphantom{\lambda}}^{(Z_{m+1})}\lambda\big)\dots\big(Z_{m+2-p}+{\vphantom{\lambda}}^{(Z_{m+2-p})}\lambda\big)\big({\vphantom{D}}^{(Z_{m+1-p})}D_{\gamma,1}^{m-p}

\displaystyle\qquad\qquad\qquad+{\vphantom{D}}^{(Z_{m+1-p})}D_{\gamma,3}^{m-p}\big)\mathring{L}\varphi_{\gamma}^{m+1}|

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle\delta^{2l+1}\int_{D^{s,u}}\varrho^{2\iota}\Big\{\sum_{p=1}^{m}\big(Z_{m+1}+{\vphantom{\lambda}}^{(Z_{m+1})}\lambda\big)\dots\big(Z_{m+2-p}+{\vphantom{\lambda}}^{(Z_{m+2-p})}\lambda\big)\big({\vphantom{D}}^{(Z_{m+1-p})}D_{\gamma,1}^{m-p}

\displaystyle\qquad+{\vphantom{D}}^{(Z_{m+1-p})}D_{\gamma,3}^{m-p}\big)\Big\}^{2}+\delta^{2l-1}\int_{D^{s,u}}\varrho^{2\iota}|\mathring{L}Z^{m+1}\varphi_{\gamma}|^{2}

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle\delta^{6-6\varepsilon_{0}}+\delta^{4-2\varepsilon_{0}}+\int_{t_{0}}^{s}\tau^{-2}\tilde{E}_{1,\leq m+2}(\tau,u)d\tau+\delta\int_{t_{0}}^{s}\tau^{-4+2\iota}\ln^{4}\tau\tilde{E}_{2,\leq m+2}(\tau,u)d\tau

(5.297)

\displaystyle+\delta^{-1}\int_{0}^{u}\tilde{F}_{1,m+2}(s,u^{\prime})du^{\prime}.

(2)

We now estimate the terms involving
{\vphantom{D}}{}^{(Z)}D_{\gamma,2}^{n}

(0\leq n\leq m)
in
J_{1}^{m+1}
. Note that in the special case
n=0
,
j
equals
m
in
J_{1}^{m+1}
, and the order of the top derivatives in
{\vphantom{D}}{}^{(Z)}D_{\gamma,2}^{0}
is
m
, so that
{\vphantom{D}}{}^{(Z)}D_{\gamma,2}^{0}
contains terms involving the
(m+1)^{\text{th}}
order derivatives of the deformation tensor.
This prevents one from using Proposition
3.1
to estimate the
L^{2}
norm of
{\vphantom{D}}{}^{(Z)}D_{\gamma,2}^{0}

directly since the
L^{2}
norm of the
(m+1)^{\text{th}}
order derivatives of the deformation tensor can be controlled only by
\tilde{E}_{1,\leq m+3}(s,u)
and
\tilde{E}_{2,\leq m+3}(s,u)
in the energy estimates
(note that
\tilde{E}_{1,\leq m+3}(s,u)
and
\tilde{E}_{2,\leq m+3}(s,u)
cannot be absorbed directly by the energies

\tilde{E}_{1,\leq m+2}(s,u)
and
\tilde{E}_{2,\leq m+2}(s,u)
on the left hand side of the resulting energy inequality by (
3.89
)).
Thus, we will
examine the expression of
{\vphantom{D}}{}^{(Z)}D_{\gamma,2}^{n}
carefully and apply the estimates in Section
4

to handle the top order derivatives of
\textrm{tr}\chi
and
\mu
.
Indeed, it follows from direct computations that

{\vphantom{D}}{}^{(T)}D_{\gamma,2}^{n}=

\displaystyle\big\{\mathring{L}T\mu+\frac{1}{4}\uwave{\mathring{\underline{L}}(\text{tr}{\vphantom{\not{\pi}}}^{(T)}\not{\pi})}+\boxed{\not{\nabla}_{X}\big(\frac{1}{2}\mu\not{d}^{X}\mu}+\mu^{2}\zeta^{X}\big)\big\}\mathring{L}\varphi_{\gamma}^{n}+\big\{\frac{1}{4}\mathring{L}(\textrm{tr}{\vphantom{\not{\pi}}}^{(T)}\not{\pi})

\displaystyle+\frac{1}{2}\boxed{\not{\nabla}_{X}(\not{d}^{X}\mu}+2\mu\zeta^{X})\big\}\mathring{\underline{L}}\varphi_{\gamma}^{n}+\big\{\frac{1}{2}\not{\mathcal{L}}_{\mathring{L}}(2\mu^{2}\zeta_{X}+\mu\not{d}_{X}\mu)-\uline{\not{d}_{X}T\mu}

\displaystyle+\underline{\frac{1}{2}\not{\mathcal{L}}_{\mathring{\underline{L}}}(\not{d}_{X}\mu}+2\mu\zeta_{X})+\underbrace{\frac{1}{2}\not{d}_{X}(\mu\text{tr}{\vphantom{\not{\pi}}}^{(T)}\not{\pi})}\big\}\not{d}^{X}\varphi_{\gamma}^{n},

(5.298)

{\vphantom{D}}{}^{(\varrho\mathring{L})}D_{\gamma,2}^{n}=

\displaystyle\big\{\mathring{L}\big(\varrho\mathring{L}\mu-\mu\big)+\uwave{\frac{1}{2}\mathring{\underline{L}}\big(\varrho\textrm{tr}\check{\chi}\big)}-\varrho\boxed{\not{\nabla}_{X}\big(\not{d}^{X}\mu}+2\mu\zeta^{X}\big)\big\}\mathring{L}\varphi_{\gamma}^{n}+\frac{1}{2}\mathring{L}\big(\varrho\textrm{tr}\check{\chi}\big)\mathring{\underline{L}}\varphi_{\gamma}^{n}

\displaystyle-\big\{\not{\mathcal{L}}_{\mathring{L}}\big(\varrho\not{d}_{X}\mu+2\varrho\mu\zeta_{X}\big)+\not{d}_{X}\big(\mu+\varrho\mathring{L}\mu\big)-\varrho\underbrace{\not{d}_{X}(\mu\textrm{tr}\chi)}\big\}\not{d}^{X}\varphi_{\gamma}^{n},

(5.299)

{\vphantom{D}}{}^{(R)}D_{\gamma,2}^{n}=

\displaystyle\big\{\mathring{L}R\mu-\frac{1}{2}\underbrace{\not{\nabla}^{X}{\vphantom{\not{\pi}}}{}^{(R)}\not{\pi}_{\mathring{\underline{L}}X}}+\frac{1}{4}\uwave{\mathring{\underline{L}}(\textrm{tr}{\vphantom{\not{\pi}}}^{(R)}\not{\pi})}\big\}\mathring{L}\Psi_{\gamma}^{n}+\big\{\frac{1}{4}\mathring{L}(\textrm{tr}{\vphantom{\not{\pi}}}^{(R)}\not{\pi})

\displaystyle-\frac{1}{2}\underbrace{\not{\nabla}^{X}{\vphantom{\not{\pi}}}{}^{(R)}\not{\pi}_{\mathring{L}X}}\big\}\mathring{\underline{L}}\Psi_{\gamma}^{n}+\big\{-\frac{1}{2}\not{\mathcal{L}}_{\mathring{L}}{\vphantom{\not{\pi}}}^{(R)}\not{\pi}_{\mathring{\underline{L}}X}-\frac{1}{2}\uwave{\not{\mathcal{L}}_{\mathring{\underline{L}}}{\vphantom{\not{\pi}}}^{(R)}\not{\pi}_{\mathring{L}X}}-\boxed{\not{d}_{X}R\mu}

\displaystyle+\frac{1}{2}\underbrace{\not{d}_{X}(\mu\textrm{tr}{\vphantom{\not{\pi}}}^{(R)}\not{\pi})}\big\}\not{d}^{X}\Psi_{\gamma}^{n}.

(5.300)

It is emphasized that special attentions are needed to handle the terms with underlines, wavy lines, boxes, or braces
in the (
5.298
)-(
5.300
). In
{\vphantom{D}}{}^{(T)}D_{\gamma,2}^{n}
,
due to
\frac{1}{2}\not{\mathcal{L}}_{\mathring{\underline{L}}}\not{d}_{X}\mu=\not{d}_{X}T\mu+\frac{1}{2}\mu\not{d}_{X}\mathring{L}\mu
by
\mathring{\underline{L}}=\mu\mathring{L}+2T
, the corresponding underline part becomes

-\not{d}_{X}T\mu+\frac{1}{2}\not{\mathcal{L}}_{\mathring{\underline{L}}}\not{d}_{X}\mu=\frac{1}{2}\mu\not{d}_{X}\mathring{L}\mu,

(5.301)

which can be treated by using (
2.36
). It follows from (
A.4
) and (
2.38
) that all terms with wavy lines in (
5.298
)-(
5.300
) contain
\not{\mathcal{L}}_{\mathring{\underline{L}}}\check{\chi}
, which can be written as
2\not{\nabla}^{2}\mu+\mu\not{\mathcal{L}}_{\mathring{L}}\check{\chi}+\cdots
by (
2.69
). Note that the terms in
\mu\not{\mathcal{L}}_{\mathring{L}}\check{\chi}+\cdots

may be estimated by (
2.68
) and Proposition
3.1
while
L^{2}
norm of the terms corresponding to
\not{\nabla}^{2}\mu

may be treated by (
4.287
). Meanwhile,
one can use (
4.287
) and (
4.288
) to estimate those terms with boxes and braces respectively.

On the other hand, it is noticed that there are some terms whose factors are the derivatives of the deformation tensors
with respect to
\mathring{L}
, for example,
\frac{1}{4}{\mathring{L}}(\text{tr}{\vphantom{\not{\pi}}}^{(T)}\not{\pi})\underline{\mathring{L}}\varphi_{\gamma}^{n}
appears in (
5.298
). In fact, these terms are not “bad” in the sense that the derivatives of
\mathring{L}

for the involved deformation tensors are
actually equipped with the “good” quantities in terms of (
2.36
) and (
2.68
) after examining
each term in (
A.2
) and (
A.4
) in Appendix A.

In summary, using (
4.287
)-(
4.288
), one can eventually arrive at

\displaystyle\delta^{2l+1}|\int_{D^{s,u}}\sum_{p=1}^{m}\big(Z_{m+1}+{\vphantom{\lambda}}^{(Z_{m+1})}\lambda\big)\dots\big(Z_{m+2-p}+{\vphantom{\lambda}}^{(Z_{m+2-p})}\lambda\big){\vphantom{D}}^{(Z_{m+1-p})}D_{\gamma,2}^{m-p}\cdot\mathring{\underline{L}}\varphi_{\gamma}^{m+1}|

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle\delta\int_{t_{0}}^{s}\Big(\delta^{1-\varepsilon_{0}}\tau^{-1/2}\delta^{l}\|Z^{m}\not{\triangle}\mu\|_{\tau,u}+\delta^{-\varepsilon_{0}}\tau^{1/2}\delta^{l}\|\not{d}Z^{m}\textrm{tr}\chi\|_{\tau,u}+\cdots\Big)\sqrt{E_{2,m+2}(\tau,u)}d\tau

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle\delta^{2(k+1)(1-\varepsilon_{0})-2\varepsilon_{0}}+\int_{t_{0}}^{s}\tau^{-1-\iota}\tilde{E}_{1,\leq m+2}(\tau,u)d\tau+\delta\int_{t_{0}}^{s}\tau^{-1-\iota}\tilde{E}_{2,\leq m+2}(\tau,u)d\tau

(5.302)

\displaystyle+\delta^{2k(1-\varepsilon_{0})-2\varepsilon_{0}-1}\int_{0}^{u}\tilde{F}_{1,m+2}(s,u^{\prime})du^{\prime}

and

\displaystyle\delta^{2l}\int_{D^{s,u}}\sum_{p=1}^{m}|\varrho^{2\iota}\big(Z_{m+1}+{\vphantom{\lambda}}^{(Z_{m+1})}\lambda\big)\dots\big(Z_{m+2-p}+{\vphantom{\lambda}}^{(Z_{m+2-p})}\lambda\big){\vphantom{D}}^{(Z_{m+1-p})}D_{\gamma,2}^{m-p}\cdot\mathring{L}\varphi_{\gamma}^{m+1}|

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle\delta^{2(k+1)(1-\varepsilon_{0})-2\varepsilon_{0}}+\int_{t_{0}}^{s}\tau^{-2}\tilde{E}_{1,\leq m+2}(\tau,u)d\tau+\delta\int_{t_{0}}^{s}\tau^{-3+2\iota}\ln^{2}\tau\tilde{E}_{2,\leq m+2}(\tau,u)d\tau

(5.303)

\displaystyle+\delta^{-1}\int_{0}^{u}\tilde{F}_{1,m+2}(s,u^{\prime})du^{\prime}.

Remark 5.1
.

It should be pointed out that the main reason for getting the precise improvements of the
L^{2}
norm of
Z^{m+1}\mu
in Proposition
3.2
is to
obtain the optimal estimates of
Z^{m}\not{\triangle}\mu
and
\not{d}Z^{m}\textrm{tr}\chi
in (
4.287
) and (
4.288
), respectively. These optimal estimates lead to the smallness factor
\delta^{2(k+1)(1-\varepsilon_{0})-2\varepsilon_{0}}
in the right hand sides of (
5.302
) and (
5.303
). Note that
\delta^{2(k+1)(1-\varepsilon_{0})-2\varepsilon_{0}}<\delta^{2-2\varepsilon_{0}}
holds
due to
\varepsilon_{0}<\varepsilon_{k}^{*}
. Based on this, one can close the energy estimates in Section
6
(see (
6.308
) for details).

5.2
Treatments on
J_{2}^{m+1}
and
\Phi_{\gamma}^{1}

This subsection deals with
J_{2}^{m+1}
and
\Phi_{\gamma}^{1}
in (
3.92
).
Note that
J_{2}^{m+1}
and
\Phi_{\gamma}^{1}
do not contain the top order derivatives of
\textrm{tr}{\chi}
and
\not{\triangle}\mu
. Therefore, according to Proposition
3.1
and the expressions
of
{\vphantom{D}}{}^{(Z)}D_{\gamma,j}^{n}
in (
5.289
)-(
5.294
) and (
5.298
)-(
5.300
), one can obtain

\displaystyle\delta^{2l+1}\int_{D^{s,u}}|\sum_{p=1}^{3}{\vphantom{D}}{}^{(Z_{m+1})}D_{\gamma,p}^{m}\cdot\mathring{\underline{L}}{\varphi}_{\gamma}^{m+1}|

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle\delta^{2(k+1)(1-\varepsilon_{0})}+\int_{t_{0}}^{s}\tau^{-1-\iota}\ln^{2}\tau\tilde{E}_{1,\leq m+2}(\tau,u)d\tau+\delta\int_{t_{0}}^{s}\tau^{-1-\iota}\tilde{E}_{2,\leq m+2}(\tau,u)d\tau

(5.304)

\displaystyle+\delta^{-1}\int_{0}^{u}\tilde{F}_{1,m+2}(s,u^{\prime})du^{\prime}.

and

\displaystyle\delta^{2l}\int_{D^{s,u}}|\sum_{p=1}^{3}\varrho^{2\iota}{\vphantom{D}}{}^{(Z_{m+1})}D_{\gamma,p}^{m}\cdot\mathring{L}{\varphi}_{\gamma}^{m+1}|

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle\delta^{2(k+1)(1-\varepsilon_{0})}+\int_{t_{0}}^{s}\tau^{-2}\ln^{2}\tau\tilde{E}_{1,\leq m+2}(\tau,u)d\tau+\delta\int_{t_{0}}^{s}\tau^{-4+2\iota}\ln^{2}\tau\tilde{E}_{2,\leq m+2}(\tau,u)d\tau

(5.305)

\displaystyle+\delta^{-1}\int_{0}^{u}\tilde{F}_{1,m+2}(s,u^{\prime})du^{\prime}.

In addition,
\Phi_{\gamma}^{0}=\mu\Box_{g}\varphi_{\gamma}
is given explicitly in (
2.49
).
Then it follows from this, Proposition
3.1
and (
3.93
) that

\displaystyle\delta^{2l+1}\int_{D^{s,u}}|(Z_{m+1}+{\vphantom{\lambda}}^{(Z_{m+1})}\lambda)\cdots(Z_{1}+{\vphantom{\lambda}}^{(Z_{1})}\lambda)\Phi_{\gamma}^{0}\cdot\mathring{\underline{L}}\varphi_{\gamma}^{m+1}|

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle\delta^{2(k+1)(1-\varepsilon_{0})}+\int_{t_{0}}^{s}\tau^{-1-\iota}\tilde{E}_{1,\leq m+2}(\tau,u)d\tau+\delta\int_{t_{0}}^{s}\tau^{-1-\iota}\tilde{E}_{2,\leq m+2}(\tau,u)d\tau

(5.306)

\displaystyle+\delta^{-1}\int_{0}^{u}\tilde{F}_{1,m+2}(s,u^{\prime})du^{\prime}

and

\begin{split}&\delta^{2l}\int_{D^{s,u}}\varrho^{2\iota}|(Z_{m+1}+{\vphantom{\lambda}}^{(Z_{m+1})}\lambda)\cdots(Z_{1}+{\vphantom{\lambda}}^{(Z_{1})}\lambda)\Phi_{\gamma}^{0}\cdot\mathring{L}\varphi_{\gamma}^{m+1}|\\
\lesssim&\delta^{2(k+1)(1-\varepsilon_{0})}+\int_{t_{0}}^{s}\tau^{-2}\tilde{E}_{1,\leq m+2}(\tau,u)d\tau+\delta\int_{t_{0}}^{s}\tau^{-4+2\iota}\tilde{E}_{2,\leq m+2}(\tau,u)d\tau\\
&+\delta^{-1}\int_{0}^{u}\tilde{F}_{1,m+2}(s,u^{\prime})du^{\prime}.\end{split}

(5.307)

6
Global estimates in
A_{2\delta}

Following the idea of
[
10
]
, we are now ready to prove the global estimates on the smooth solution
\phi
to the equation (
1.1
) with (
1.2
), (
1.14
) and (
1.15
)-(
1.16
) near
C_{0}
when
\varepsilon_{0}<\varepsilon_{k}^{*}
. To this end, one has to estimate the solution in
\tilde{C}_{2\delta}
as in
[
10
]
. Compared with
[
10
, Section 11]
, to obtain the behavior of the solution for
\varepsilon_{0}
approaching
\varepsilon_{k}^{*}
, one needs the more precise estimates on all related quantities.

By substituting (
5.295
)-(
5.297
) and (
5.302
)-(
5.307
) into (
3.89
), one gets from
Gronwall’s inequality and
0<\varepsilon_{0}<\varepsilon_{k}^{*}
that

\delta\tilde{E}_{2,\leq 2N-4}(s,u)+\delta F_{2,\leq 2N-4}(s,u)+\tilde{E}_{1,\leq 2N-4}(s,u)+F_{1,\leq 2N-4}(s,u)\lesssim\delta^{2-2\varepsilon_{0}}.

(6.308)

This, together with (
3.191
) and (
4.287
), yields that for
m\leq 2N-5
,

\delta^{l}\|Z^{m}\phi\|_{s,u}\lesssim\delta^{5/2-\varepsilon_{0}}.

(6.309)

Based on (
6.308
) and (
6.309
), one can close the bootstrap
assumptions
(\star)
in Subsection
2.2
by using the analogous Sobolev-type embedding formula which follows form Proposition 18.10 in
[
23
]
.

Lemma 6.1
.

For any function
f\in H^{2}(S_{s,u})
, under the assumptions
(\star)
, if
\delta>0
is small, then

\|f\|_{L^{\infty}(S_{s,u})}\lesssim\frac{1}{s^{1/2}}\sum_{p\leq 1}\|R^{p}f\|_{L^{2}(S_{s,u})}.

(6.310)

It follows from (
6.308
)-(
6.310
) that for
0<\varepsilon_{0}<\varepsilon_{k}^{*}
and
m\leq 2N-6
,

\delta^{l}|Z^{m}\varphi_{\gamma}|\lesssim\frac{\delta^{l}}{s^{1/2}}\sum_{p\leq 1}\|R^{p}Z^{m}\varphi_{\gamma}\|_{L^{2}(S_{s,u})}\overset{\eqref{SSi}}{\lesssim}\frac{\delta^{1/2}}{s^{1/2}}\big(\sqrt{E_{1,\leq 2N-4}}+\sqrt{E_{2,\leq 2N-4}}\big)\lesssim\delta^{1-\varepsilon_{0}}s^{-1/2};

(6.311)

while for
m\leq 2N-7
,

\delta^{l}|Z^{m}\phi|\lesssim\delta^{1/2}s^{-1/2}\delta^{l}\sum_{p\leq 1}(\|\underline{\mathring{L}}R^{p}Z^{m}\phi\|_{s,u}+\|{\mathring{L}}R^{p}Z^{m}\phi\|_{s,u})\lesssim\delta^{2-\varepsilon_{0}}s^{-1/2}.

(6.312)

Replacing
(\star)
by (
6.311
)-(
6.312
) in Section
2.2
and following all procedures there yield the following
improved estimates over Proposition
2.2
: for
m\leq 2N-9
,

\begin{split}&|\not{\mathcal{L}}_{Z}^{m}\check{\chi}|\lesssim\delta^{k(1-\varepsilon_{0})-l}s^{-2},\quad|\not{\mathcal{L}}_{Z}^{m+1}\not{d}x^{j}|\lesssim\delta^{-l},\quad|\not{\mathcal{L}}_{Z}^{m}{\vphantom{\not{\pi}}}{}^{(T)}\not{\pi}_{\mathring{L}}|\lesssim\delta^{k(1-\varepsilon_{0})-1-l}s^{-1},\\
&|Z^{m+1}\check{L}^{j}|+|\not{\mathcal{L}}_{Z}^{m}{\vphantom{\not{\pi}}}{}^{(R)}\not{\pi}|+|\not{\mathcal{L}}_{Z}^{m}{\vphantom{\not{\pi}}}{}^{(R)}\not{\pi}_{\mathring{L}}|+|Z^{m+1}\check{\varrho}|\lesssim\delta^{k(1-\varepsilon_{0})-l}s^{-1}\ln s,\\
&|\not{\mathcal{L}}_{Z}^{m}R|+|Z^{m+1}\upsilon|\lesssim\delta^{k(1-\varepsilon_{0})-l}\ln s,\quad|Z^{m+1}\mu|\lesssim\delta^{k(1-\varepsilon_{0})-\varepsilon_{0}-l}s^{-(k-1)/2},\\
&|\not{\mathcal{L}}_{Z}^{m}{\vphantom{\not{\pi}}}{}^{(T)}\not{\pi}|\lesssim\delta^{k(1-\varepsilon_{0})-1-l}s^{-1}+s^{-l-1},\quad|\not{\mathcal{L}}_{Z}^{m}{\vphantom{\not{\pi}}}{}^{(R)}\not{\pi}_{T}|\lesssim(\delta^{k(1-\varepsilon_{0})-l}+\delta^{2k(1-\varepsilon_{0})-1-l})s^{-1}\ln s,\end{split}

(6.313)

where
l
is the number of
T
as before.

Since the estimates in (
6.311
) and (
6.312
) are independent of the constant
M
in
(\star)
,
the bootstrap assumptions
(\star)
are proved. Therefore, the global estimates on the solution

\phi
to (
1.1
) with (
1.2
), (
1.9
) and (
1.14
)
in the domain
D^{s,4\delta}
(see Figure
2
in Subsection
2.1
) are established.

In addition, one can further update the estimates on
\check{\varrho}
(see (
2.57
)),
\upsilon
(see (
2.33
)) and
g_{ij}\check{L}^{i}\omega^{j}
, which will be used to obtain the more precise smallness orders and time decay rates for the
L^{\infty}
norms of
\phi
under the actions of
\Gamma\in\{L,\underline{L},\Omega_{ij}\}
in
A_{2\delta}

and further be taken as the boundary values on
\tilde{C}_{2\delta}
to study the global Goursat problem
of (
1.1
) in
B_{2\delta}
.

Lemma 6.2
.

In
D^{s,4\delta}
, the quantities
\check{L}^{i}
and
\check{\varrho}
have the following estimates:

\delta^{l}|Z^{m}\check{\varrho}|+\delta^{l}|Z^{m}(g_{ij}\check{L}^{i}\omega^{j})|\lesssim\delta^{(k+1)(1-\varepsilon_{0})}s^{-1},\quad\delta^{l}|Z^{m}(g_{\alpha\beta}\omega^{\alpha}\omega^{\beta})|\lesssim\delta^{(k+1)(1-\varepsilon_{0})}s^{-3/2},

(6.314)

where
l
is the number of
T
in
Z^{m}
and
m\leq 2N-9
.

Proof.

On the initial time
t_{0}
,

\displaystyle\mu=\frac{1}{\sqrt{(g^{0i}\omega_{i})^{2}+g^{ij}\omega_{i}\omega_{j}}},

(6.315)

\displaystyle\tilde{L}^{i}=-g^{0i}\big(-g^{0j}\omega_{j}+\sqrt{(g^{0j}\omega_{j})^{2}+g^{ab}\omega_{a}\omega_{b}}\big)+g^{ij}\omega_{j}.

(6.316)

Due to
g^{\alpha\beta}=m^{\alpha\beta}+g^{\alpha\beta,\gamma_{1}\cdots\gamma_{k}}\varphi_{\gamma_{1}}\cdots\varphi_{\gamma_{k}}+O(\varphi^{k+1})
, then it
follows from (
2.30
) that

\begin{split}\check{L}^{i}|_{t_{0}}=&\Big\{(-g^{0i,\gamma_{1}\cdots\gamma_{k}}-\frac{1}{2}g^{ab,\gamma_{1}\cdots\gamma_{k}}\omega_{a}\omega_{b}\omega^{i}+g^{ij,\gamma_{1}\cdots\gamma_{k}}\omega_{j})\varphi_{\gamma_{1}}\cdots\varphi_{\gamma_{k}}+f(\omega,\varphi)\varphi^{k+1}\Big\}\Big|_{t_{0}}.\end{split}

(6.317)

In addition, by
\partial_{t}=-\frac{1}{2}\omega_{0}\underline{L}+\frac{1}{2}L
,
\partial_{i}=-\frac{1}{2}\omega_{i}\underline{L}+\frac{1}{2}\omega_{i}L+\frac{1}{r}\omega^{i}_{\perp}\Omega

and (
1.9
), one has

\begin{split}g_{ij}\check{L}^{i}\omega^{j}|_{t_{0}}&=\Big\{(-\frac{\underline{L}\phi}{2})^{k}(g^{0i,\gamma_{1}\cdots\gamma_{k}}\omega_{0}\omega_{i}+\frac{1}{2}g^{ij,\gamma_{1}\cdots\gamma_{k}}\omega_{i}\omega_{j})\omega_{\gamma_{1}}\cdots\omega_{\gamma_{k}}+f(\varphi,\omega)\left(\begin{array}[]{ccc}\varphi^{k+1}\\
\varphi^{k-1}L\phi\\
\varphi^{k-1}\frac{1}{r}\Omega\phi\end{array}\right)\Big\}\Big|_{t_{0}}\\
&=f(\varphi,\omega)\left(\begin{array}[]{ccc}\varphi^{k+1}\\
\varphi^{k-1}L\phi\\
\varphi^{k-1}\frac{1}{r}\Omega\phi\end{array}\right)\Bigg|_{t_{0}}.\end{split}

(6.318)

On the other hand, it follows from (
6.317
) that

\begin{split}\upsilon|_{t_{0}}=&\{-g_{0j}\epsilon_{i}^{j}x^{i}-g_{aj}\check{L}^{a}\epsilon_{i}^{j}x^{i}-(g_{aj}-m_{aj})\omega^{a}\epsilon_{i}^{j}x^{i}\}|_{t_{0}}\\
=&\epsilon_{i}^{j}x^{i}\Big\{(-g^{0j,\gamma_{1},\cdots\gamma_{k}}+g^{aj,\gamma_{1},\cdots\gamma_{k}}\omega_{a})\varphi_{\gamma_{1}}\cdots\varphi_{\gamma_{k}}-\check{L}^{j}+f(\varphi,\omega)\varphi^{k+1}\Big\}\Big|_{t_{0}}\\
=&rf(\varphi,\omega)\varphi^{k+1}\Big|_{t_{0}}.\end{split}

(6.319)

For any
\bar{Z}\in\{T=-\mu(g^{0i}+\mathring{L}^{i})\partial_{i},R=\Omega-\upsilon\tilde{T}\}
, (
6.318
) and (
6.319
) imply

\delta^{l}|\bar{Z}^{m}(g_{ij}\check{L}^{i}\omega^{j})|_{t_{0}}\lesssim\delta^{(k+1)(1-\varepsilon_{0})}.

(6.320)

Under the action of
\mathring{L}
, the term
\varrho g_{ij}\check{L}^{i}\omega^{j}
can be written as

\begin{split}\mathring{L}(\varrho g_{ij}\check{L}^{i}\omega^{j})=\big(G_{ij}^{\gamma}\mathring{L}\varphi_{\gamma}\big)\varrho\check{L}^{i}\omega^{j}+\frac{\varrho}{r}g_{ij}\mathring{L}(\varrho\check{L}^{i})(\mathring{L}^{j}-\check{L}^{j})+\frac{\varrho}{r}g_{ij}\check{L}^{i}(\check{L}^{j}-\omega^{j}\omega_{a}\check{L}^{a}),\end{split}

(6.321)

where

g_{ij}\mathring{L}(\varrho\check{L}^{i})\mathring{L}^{j}=-\frac{1}{2}\varrho G_{\mathring{L}\mathring{L}}^{\gamma}\mathring{L}\varphi_{\gamma}+f(\varphi,\mathring{L}^{1},\mathring{L}^{2})\varrho\varphi^{2k-1}\left(\begin{array}[]{ccc}\mathring{L}\varphi\\
\not{d}_{X}\varphi\not{d}^{X}x\end{array}\right)

(6.322)

follows from (
2.40
), (
2.39
), and
g_{ij}(\not{d}^{X}x^{i})\mathring{L}^{j}=-g_{0j}(\not{d}^{X}x^{j})
.
In addition, (
1.9
) implies

\begin{split}G_{\mathring{L}\mathring{L}}^{\gamma}\mathring{L}\varphi_{\gamma}=&-(\partial_{\varphi_{\gamma}}g^{\kappa\lambda})\mathring{L}_{\kappa}\mathring{L}_{\lambda}\mathring{L}^{\nu}\partial_{\gamma}\varphi_{\nu}\\
=&-(\partial_{\varphi_{0}}g^{\kappa\lambda})\mathring{L}_{\kappa}\mathring{L}_{\lambda}\big(\mathring{L}^{2}\phi-(\mathring{L}\mathring{L}^{i})\varphi_{i}\big)+\big((\partial_{\varphi_{\gamma}}g^{\kappa\lambda})\mathring{L}_{\kappa}\mathring{L}_{\lambda}\mathring{L}_{\gamma}\big)\tilde{T}^{i}\mathring{L}\varphi_{i}\\
&-(\partial_{\varphi_{\gamma}}g^{\kappa\lambda})\mathring{L}_{\kappa}\mathring{L}_{\lambda}g_{\gamma j}(\not{d}^{X}x^{j})\big(\not{d}_{X}\mathring{L}\phi-(\not{d}_{X}\mathring{L}^{i})\varphi_{i}\big),\end{split}

(6.323)

where
(\mathring{L}\mathring{L}^{i})\varphi_{i}=O(|\varphi^{k}\mathring{L}\varphi|+|\varphi^{k}\not{d}\varphi|)
and
(\not{d}_{X}\mathring{L}^{i})\varphi_{i}=\textrm{tr}\chi\not{d}_{X}\phi+O(|\varphi^{k}\mathring{L}\varphi|+|\varphi^{k}\not{d}\varphi|)
due to (
2.39
) and (
2.41
) respectively, and
(\partial_{\varphi_{\gamma}}g^{\kappa\lambda})\mathring{L}_{\kappa}\mathring{L}_{\lambda}\mathring{L}_{\gamma}
satisfies (
2.61
). Substituting (
6.322
)-(
6.323
) into (
6.321
) and using (
6.311
)-(
6.313
) lead to

\delta^{l}|\bar{Z}^{m}\mathring{L}(\varrho g_{ij}\check{L}^{i}\omega^{j})|\lesssim\delta^{(k+1)(1-\varepsilon_{0})}s^{-3/2},\quad m\leq 2N-9.

(6.324)

Therefore, for
m\leq 2N-9
, it follows from (
6.313
) and (
6.324
) that

\begin{split}\delta^{l}|\mathring{L}\bar{Z}^{m}(\varrho g_{ij}\check{L}^{i}\omega^{j})|\lesssim&\delta^{l}\underbrace{\sum_{p_{1}+p_{2}=m-1}|\big(\not{\mathcal{L}}_{\bar{Z}}^{p_{1}}{\vphantom{\not{\pi}}}{}^{(\bar{Z})}\not{\pi}_{\mathring{L}}^{X}\big)\not{d}_{X}\bar{Z}^{p_{2}}(\varrho g_{ij}\check{L}^{i}\omega^{j})|}_{\text{vanish when}\ m=0}+\delta^{l}|\bar{Z}^{m}\mathring{L}(\varrho g_{ij}\check{L}^{i}\omega^{j})|\\
\lesssim&\delta^{(k+1)(1-\varepsilon_{0})}s^{-3/2}.\end{split}

This, together with (
6.320
), yields

\delta^{l}|\bar{Z}^{m}(g_{ij}\check{L}^{i}\omega^{j})|\lesssim\delta^{(k+1)(1-\varepsilon_{0})}s^{-1},\quad m\leq 2N-9.

(6.325)

If there is at least one
\varrho\mathring{L}
in
Z^{m}

(m\leq 2N-8)
, namely,
Z^{m}=Z^{p_{1}}(\varrho\mathring{L})\bar{Z}^{p_{2}}

with
p_{1}+p_{2}=m-1
, then

(\varrho\mathring{L})\bar{Z}^{p_{2}}(g_{ij}\check{L}^{i}\omega^{j})=\underbrace{\sum_{q_{1}+q_{2}=p_{2}-1}\varrho\bar{Z}^{q_{1}}{\vphantom{\not{\pi}}}{}^{(\bar{Z})}\not{\pi}_{\mathring{L}}^{X}\cdot\not{d}_{X}\bar{Z}^{q_{2}}(g_{ij}\check{L}^{i}\omega^{j})}_{\text{vanish when}\ p_{2}=0}+\varrho\bar{Z}^{p_{2}}\big(\varrho^{-1}\mathring{L}(\varrho g_{ij}\check{L}^{i}\omega^{j})-\varrho^{-1}g_{ij}\check{L}^{i}\omega^{j}\big).

With an induction argument on the number of
\varrho\mathring{L}
in
Z^{p_{1}}
, it follows from the expression above, (
6.313
) and (
6.324
) that

|Z^{p_{1}}(\varrho\mathring{L})\bar{Z}^{p_{2}}(g_{ij}\check{L}^{i}\omega^{j})|\\
\lesssim\delta^{(k+1)(1-\varepsilon_{0})-l}s^{-1}.

(6.326)

Therefore, for any vector
Z\in\{\varrho\mathring{L},T,R\}
, when
m\leq 2N-9
, by (
6.325
) and (
6.326
), one can get

\begin{split}\delta^{l}|Z^{m}(g_{ij}\check{L}^{i}\omega^{j})|\lesssim\delta^{(k+1)(1-\varepsilon_{0})}s^{-1}.\end{split}

(6.327)

In order to improve the estimate (
2.58
) on
\check{\varrho}
, one needs to check the numerator in (
2.57
).
Due to
\varphi_{\gamma_{p}}=\delta_{\gamma_{p}}^{0}\mathring{L}\phi-\mathring{L}_{\gamma_{p}}\tilde{T}^{i}\varphi_{i}+g_{\gamma_{p}i}\not{d}_{X}x^{i}\not{d}^{X}\phi

and
g^{00}=-1
, then

\displaystyle 1-g_{ij}\omega^{i}\omega^{j}+2g_{ij}\check{T}^{i}\omega^{j}=1-g_{ij}\omega^{i}\omega^{j}-2g_{0i}\omega^{i}-2g_{ij}\check{L}^{i}\omega^{j}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle g^{ij,\gamma_{1}\cdots\gamma_{k}}\omega_{i}\omega_{j}\varphi_{\gamma_{1}}\cdots\varphi_{\gamma_{k}}+2g^{0i,\gamma_{1}\cdots\gamma_{k}}\omega_{0}\omega_{i}\varphi_{\gamma_{1}}\cdots\varphi_{\gamma_{k}}-2g_{ij}\check{L}^{i}\omega^{j}+f(\varphi,\omega)\varphi^{k+1}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle(-1)^{k}g^{\alpha\beta,\gamma_{1}\cdots\gamma_{k}}\omega_{\alpha}\omega_{\beta}\mathring{L}_{\gamma_{1}}\cdots\mathring{L}_{\gamma_{k}}(\tilde{T}^{i_{1}}\varphi_{i_{1}})\cdots(\tilde{T}^{i_{k}}\varphi_{i_{k}})-2g_{ij}\check{L}^{i}\omega^{j}+f(\varphi,\omega)\left(\begin{array}[]{ccc}\varphi^{k+1}\\
\varphi^{k-1}\mathring{L}\phi\\
\varphi^{k-1}\not{d}^{X}x\not{d}_{X}\phi\end{array}\right)

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle-2g_{ij}\check{L}^{i}\omega^{j}+f(\varphi,\mathring{L}^{1},\mathring{L}^{2},\frac{x}{\varrho},\check{\varrho})\left(\begin{array}[]{ccc}\varphi^{k+1}\\
\varphi^{k-1}\mathring{L}\phi\\
\varphi^{k-1}\not{d}^{X}x\not{d}_{X}\phi\\
\check{L}^{1}\varphi^{k}\\
\check{L}^{2}\varphi^{k}\\
\check{\varrho}\varphi^{k}\end{array}\right).

Substituting (
6
) into (
2.57
) and applying (
6.327
) yield that for
m\leq 2N-9
,

\delta^{l}|Z^{m}\check{\varrho}|\lesssim\delta^{(k+1)(1-\varepsilon_{0})}s^{-1}.

Since
g_{00}=-1+f(\varphi)\varphi^{k+1}
, then
g_{\alpha\beta}\omega^{\alpha}\omega^{\beta}=-1+2g_{0i}\omega^{i}+g_{ij}\omega^{i}\omega^{j}+f(\varphi,\omega)\varphi^{k+1}
.
This, together with the first identity in (
6
), shows that

g_{\alpha\beta}\omega^{\alpha}\omega^{\beta}=f(\varphi,\mathring{L}^{1},\mathring{L}^{2},\frac{x}{\varrho},\check{\varrho})\left(\begin{array}[]{ccc}\varphi^{k+1}\\
\varphi^{k-1}\mathring{L}\phi\\
\varphi^{k-1}\not{d}^{X}x\not{d}_{X}\phi\\
\check{L}^{1}\varphi^{k}\\
\check{L}^{2}\varphi^{k}\\
\check{\varrho}\varphi^{k}\end{array}\right),

which implies
\delta^{l}|Z^{m}(g_{\alpha\beta}\omega^{\alpha}\omega^{\beta})|\lesssim\delta^{(k+1)(1-\varepsilon_{0})}s^{-3/2}
.
∎

Remark 6.1
.

It follows from (
6.314
) that
\check{\varrho}=\frac{r}{\varrho}-1=O(\delta^{(k+1)(1-\varepsilon_{0})}s^{-1})
. This
shows that the distance between
C_{0}
and
C_{4\delta}
on the hypersurface
\Sigma_{t}
is
4\delta+O(\delta^{(k+1)(1-\varepsilon_{0})})
. Thus, the characteristic
surface
C_{u}

(0\leq u\leq 4\delta)
is almost straight with the error
O(\delta^{(k+1)(1-\varepsilon_{0})})
from
the corresponding light conic surface due to
\delta^{(k+1)(1-\varepsilon_{0})}=o(\delta)
for
\varepsilon_{0}<\varepsilon_{k}^{*}
.

Lemma 6.3
.

In
D^{s,3\delta}
, for
m\leq 2N-8
, it holds that

\delta^{l}|Z^{m}\upsilon|\lesssim\delta^{(k+1)(1-\varepsilon_{0})},

(6.337)

where
l
is the number of
T
in
Z^{m}
.

Proof.

Write
g_{\alpha\beta}=m_{\alpha\beta}+g_{\alpha\beta}^{\gamma_{1}\cdots\gamma_{k}}\varphi_{\gamma_{1}}\cdots\varphi_{\gamma_{k}}+h_{\alpha\beta}(\varphi)
in (
1.3
).
Then (
2.33
) implies

\begin{split}\upsilon=&-g_{0j}\epsilon_{i}^{j}x^{i}-g_{aj}\check{L}^{a}\epsilon_{i}^{j}x^{i}-g_{aj}\epsilon_{i}^{j}\frac{x^{a}x^{i}}{\varrho}\\
=&\varrho\big\{-(g_{0j}^{\gamma_{1}\cdots\gamma_{k}}+g_{ja}^{\gamma_{1}\cdots\gamma_{k}}\frac{x^{a}}{\varrho})\epsilon_{i}^{j}\frac{x^{i}}{\varrho}\varphi_{\gamma_{1}}\cdots\varphi_{\gamma_{k}}-m_{ja}\check{L}^{a}\epsilon_{i}^{j}\frac{x^{i}}{\varrho}\big\}+\varrho f(\varphi,\frac{x}{\varrho})\left(\begin{array}[]{ccc}\varphi^{k+1}\\
\check{L}^{1}\varphi^{k}\\
\check{L}^{2}\varphi^{k}\end{array}\right).\end{split}

(6.338)

Let

\mathscr{F}=-(g_{0j}^{\gamma_{1}\cdots\gamma_{k}}+g_{ja}^{\gamma_{1}\cdots\gamma_{k}}\frac{x^{a}}{\varrho})\epsilon_{i}^{j}\frac{x^{i}}{\varrho}\varphi_{\gamma_{1}}\cdots\varphi_{\gamma_{k}}-m_{ja}\check{L}^{a}\epsilon_{i}^{j}\frac{x^{i}}{\varrho}.

(6.339)

Then

\displaystyle T\mathscr{F}=

\displaystyle-k(g_{0j}^{\gamma_{1}\cdots\gamma_{k}}+g_{ja}^{\gamma_{1}\cdots\gamma_{k}}\frac{x^{a}}{\varrho})\epsilon_{i}^{j}\frac{x^{i}}{\varrho}(T\varphi_{\gamma_{1}})\varphi_{\gamma_{2}}\cdots\varphi_{\gamma_{k}}-m_{ja}\epsilon_{i}^{j}\big((T\mathring{L}^{a})\frac{x^{i}}{\varrho}+\check{L}^{a}T(\frac{x^{i}}{\varrho})\big)

\displaystyle-\big(g_{0j}^{\gamma_{1}\cdots\gamma_{k}}T(\frac{x^{i}}{\varrho})+g_{ja}^{\gamma_{1}\cdots\gamma_{k}}T(\frac{x^{a}x^{i}}{\varrho^{2}})\big)\epsilon_{i}^{j}\varphi_{\gamma_{1}}\cdots\varphi_{\gamma_{k}}+m_{ja}T(\frac{x^{a}}{\varrho})\epsilon_{i}^{j}\frac{x^{i}}{\varrho}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle-k(g_{0j}^{\gamma_{1}\cdots\gamma_{k}}+g_{ja}^{\gamma_{1}\cdots\gamma_{k}}\frac{x^{a}}{\varrho})\epsilon_{i}^{j}\frac{x^{i}}{\varrho}(T\varphi_{\gamma_{1}})\varphi_{\gamma_{2}}\cdots\varphi_{\gamma_{k}}-m_{ja}\epsilon_{i}^{j}(T\mathring{L}^{a})\frac{x^{i}}{\varrho}

(6.340)

\displaystyle+\frac{1}{\varrho}f(\varphi,\check{L}^{1},\check{L}^{2},\mu,\frac{x}{\varrho})\left(\begin{array}[]{ccc}\varphi^{k}\\
\check{L}^{1}\\
\check{L}^{2}\\
\mu-1\end{array}\right),

where
T(\frac{x^{a}}{\varrho})=-\frac{\mu}{\varrho}g^{0a}-\frac{\mu}{\varrho}\check{L}^{a}+\frac{(1-\mu)x^{a}}{\varrho^{2}}
has been used.
Note that there is a factor
T\mathring{L}^{a}
in (
6.340
) containing the term
-G_{X\mathring{L}}^{\gamma}(T\varphi_{\gamma})\not{d}^{X}x^{a}

due to (
2.43
). It follows from the definition of
G_{X\mathring{L}}^{\gamma}
in (
2.35
)
and (
2.47
) that

\displaystyle-G_{X\mathring{L}}^{\gamma}(T\varphi_{\gamma})\not{d}^{X}x^{a}=-G_{i\beta}^{\gamma}(\not{d}_{X}x^{i}\cdot\not{d}^{X}x^{a})\mathring{L}^{\beta}(T\varphi_{\gamma})

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle-G_{a\beta}^{\gamma}\mathring{L}^{\beta}(T\varphi_{\gamma})-G_{\mathring{L}\mathring{L}}^{\gamma}(T\varphi_{\gamma})\tilde{T}^{a}+G_{0\beta}^{\gamma}\mathring{L}^{\beta}\tilde{T}^{a}(T\varphi_{\gamma})+f(\varphi,\mathring{L}^{i})\varphi^{2k-1}T\varphi_{\gamma}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle-k(g_{a0}^{\gamma\gamma_{2}\cdots\gamma_{k}}+g_{aj}^{\gamma\gamma_{2}\cdots\gamma_{k}}\frac{x^{j}}{\varrho}+g_{0\beta}^{\gamma\gamma_{2}\cdots\gamma_{k}}\mathring{L}^{\beta}\frac{x^{a}}{\varrho})\varphi_{\gamma_{2}}\cdots\varphi_{\gamma_{k}}(T\varphi_{\gamma})-G_{\mathring{L}\mathring{L}}^{\gamma}(T\varphi_{\gamma})\tilde{T}^{a}

(6.345)

\displaystyle+f(\varphi,\mathring{L}^{1},\mathring{L}^{2})\left(\begin{array}[]{ccc}\varphi^{k}\\
\varphi^{k-1}\check{L}^{1}\\
\varphi^{k-1}\check{L}^{2}\end{array}\right)T\varphi.

Examining (
2.43
) carefully and using (
6.345
), one can get

\begin{split}T\mathring{L}^{a}=&-k(g_{a0}^{\gamma\gamma_{2}\cdots\gamma_{k}}+g_{aj}^{\gamma\gamma_{2}\cdots\gamma_{k}}\frac{x^{j}}{\varrho}+g_{0\beta}^{\gamma\gamma_{2}\cdots\gamma_{k}}\mathring{L}^{\beta}\frac{x^{a}}{\varrho})\varphi_{\gamma_{2}}\cdots\varphi_{\gamma_{k}}(T\varphi_{\gamma})\\
&+f(\varphi,\mathring{L}^{1},\mathring{L}^{1})\left(\begin{array}[]{ccc}\varphi^{k}(T\varphi)\\
\varphi^{k-1}\check{L}^{1}(T\varphi)\\
\varphi^{k-1}\check{L}^{2}(T\varphi)\\
G_{\mathring{L}\mathring{L}}^{\gamma}(T\varphi_{\gamma})\\
\not{d}^{X}\mu\not{d}_{X}x\\
\varphi^{k-1}(\mu\mathring{L}\varphi)\\
\varphi^{k-1}(\mu\not{d}^{X}\varphi\not{d}_{X}x)\end{array}\right).\end{split}

(6.349)

For
m\leq 2N-8
, substitute (
6.349
) into (
6.340
) to yield

\delta^{l}|Z^{m}T\mathscr{F}|\lesssim\delta^{k(1-\varepsilon_{0})-\varepsilon_{0}}s^{-1},

where (
2.60
) and (
2.61
) have been used.
Thus,

\delta^{l}|TZ^{m}\mathscr{F}|\leq\delta^{l}|[T,Z^{m}]\mathscr{F}|+\delta^{l}|Z^{m}T\mathscr{F}|\lesssim\delta^{k(1-\varepsilon_{0})-\varepsilon_{0}}s^{-1},

which, together with (
2.48
), implies

\delta^{l}|(\partial_{t}-\partial_{r})Z^{m}\mathscr{F}|\leq\delta^{l}|TZ^{m}\mathscr{F}|+\delta^{l}|\mathring{L}Z^{m}\mathscr{F}|+\delta^{l}|\not{d}Z^{m}\mathscr{F}|\lesssim\delta^{k(1-\varepsilon_{0})-\varepsilon_{0}}s^{-1}.

(6.350)

Integrating (
6.350
) along integral curves of
\partial_{t}-\partial_{r}
yields

\delta^{l}|Z^{m}\mathscr{F}|\lesssim\delta^{(k+1)(1-\varepsilon_{0})}s^{-1},\qquad m\leq 2N-8.

(6.351)

It follows from (
6.338
), (
6.351
) and (
6.2
) that for
m\leq 2N-8
,

\delta^{l}|Z^{m}\upsilon|\lesssim\delta^{(k+1)(1-\varepsilon_{0})}.

∎

Now we are ready to obtain the more precise orders of smallness of
\delta
and time decay rates for
the
L^{\infty}
norms of
\phi
under the actions of
\Gamma\in\{L,\underline{L},\Omega\}
.
Recall the standard vectors
\{L,\underline{L},\Omega\}
defined in the end of Section
1
.
It follows from (
2.48
) and (
2.32
) that

\begin{split}&L=\mathring{L}-\mu^{-1}\omega^{\alpha}\mathring{L}_{\alpha}T+\omega^{\alpha}g_{\alpha j}(\not{d}^{X}x^{j})X,\\
&\underline{L}=\mathring{L}+\mu^{-1}(\omega^{i}\mathring{L}_{i}-\mathring{L}_{0})T+(g_{0j}\not{d}^{X}x^{j}-\omega^{i}g_{ij}\not{d}^{X}x^{j})X,\\
&\Omega=R+\mu^{-1}\upsilon T.\end{split}

(6.352)

The identity

\omega^{\alpha}\mathring{L}_{\alpha}=g_{\alpha\beta}\omega^{\alpha}\omega^{\beta}+g_{0i}\check{L}^{i}+g_{ij}\omega^{i}\check{L}^{j}+\check{\varrho}(g_{0i}\omega^{i}+g_{ij}\omega^{i}\omega^{j})

and (
6.314
) imply that for
m\leq 2N-9
,

\delta^{l}|Z^{m}(\omega^{\alpha}\mathring{L}_{\alpha})|\lesssim\delta^{(k+1)(1-\varepsilon_{0})}s^{-1}

(6.353)

holds in
D^{s,4\delta}
.
Therefore, collecting (
6.337
), (
6.352
), (
6.353
) with (
6.311
) and (
6.312
) yields that in
D^{s,3\delta}
,

|\Gamma^{m}\varphi_{\gamma}|\lesssim\delta^{1-\varepsilon_{0}-l}s^{-1/2},\qquad|\Gamma^{m}\phi|\lesssim\delta^{2-\varepsilon_{0}-l}s^{-1/2},\quad m\leq 2N-8,

(6.354)

where
\Gamma\in\{(s+r)L,\underline{L},\Omega\}
and
l
is the number of
\underline{L}
in
\Gamma^{m}
.

For any point
P\in\tilde{C}_{2\delta}=\{(t,x):t\geq t_{0},t-|x|=2\delta\}
, there is an integral curve of

L
across this point, and the corresponding initial point is denoted by
P_{0}(t_{0},x_{0})
on
\Sigma_{t_{0}}
with
|x_{0}|=1
.
From (
2.53
),
|\underline{L}^{p}L^{q}\Omega^{c}\phi(P_{0})|\lesssim\delta^{2-\varepsilon_{0}}
holds.
Next we derive an improved estimate on
\underline{L}^{p}L^{q}\Omega^{c}\phi
at the point
P
.
It is emphasized here that the condition of
0<\varepsilon_{0}<\varepsilon_{k}^{*}
plays a key role
in the following proposition.

Proposition 6.1
.

For any
(t,x)\in\tilde{C}_{2\delta}
, when
N
is large enough, it holds that

|\underline{L}^{p}L^{q}\Omega^{c}\phi(t,x)|\lesssim\delta^{2-\varepsilon_{0}}t^{-1/2-q}.

(6.355)

Proof.

According to (
B.13
) in Appendix B and (
6.354
), one has

|L(r^{1/2}\bar{\partial}^{m}\Omega^{c}{\underline{L}}\phi)|\lesssim\delta^{2-\varepsilon_{0}-m}t^{-2}+\delta^{(k+1)(1-\varepsilon_{0})-\varepsilon_{0}-m}t^{-3/2},

(6.356)

where
\bar{\partial}\in\{\partial_{t},\partial_{r}\}
.
Integrating (
6.356
) along integral curves of
L
and using the estimate (
2.53
) on initial data at
t_{0}
,
one then can get
that on
\tilde{C}_{2\delta}
,

|\bar{\partial}^{m}\Omega^{c}{\underline{L}}\phi|\lesssim\delta^{2-\varepsilon_{0}-m}t^{-1/2}+\delta^{(k+1)(1-\varepsilon_{0})-\varepsilon_{0}-m}t^{-1/2},\ m+c\leq 2N-10.

(6.357)

Next we construct a sequence of functions inductively as follows:

\displaystyle a_{0}(\epsilon)

\displaystyle=2-\epsilon,

\displaystyle a_{1}(\epsilon)

\displaystyle=(k+1)(1-\epsilon)-\epsilon,

\displaystyle a_{n+1}(\epsilon)

\displaystyle=(k+2)a_{n}(\epsilon)-1,\qquad n\geq 1.

Then for
n\geq 1
,

a_{n+1}(\epsilon)=(k+2)^{n}a_{1}(\epsilon)-\frac{(k+2)^{n}-1}{k+1}.

Let
\epsilon(n)
and
\bar{\epsilon}(n)
be the solutions to
a_{n}(\epsilon)=a_{0}(\epsilon)
and
ka_{n}(\epsilon)+1-\epsilon=a_{n+1}(\epsilon)
respectively, so that

\displaystyle\epsilon(n)

\displaystyle=\frac{k}{k+1}-\frac{1}{(k+2)^{n}-1},

\displaystyle\bar{\epsilon}(n)

\displaystyle=\frac{k}{k+1}-\frac{k}{(k+1)\big(2(k+2)^{n}-1\big)}.

Thus,

\displaystyle\lim_{n\rightarrow\infty}\epsilon(n)=\lim_{n\rightarrow\infty}\bar{\epsilon}(n)=\frac{k}{k+1},

\displaystyle\epsilon(n)<\bar{\epsilon}(n)<\epsilon(n+1).

For any fixed number
\varepsilon_{0}\in(0,\varepsilon_{k}^{*})
, there exists an
n\in\mathbb{N}
such that
\epsilon(n-1)<\varepsilon_{0}\leq\epsilon(n)

with the convention that
\epsilon(0)=0
.

1.

When
n=1
, i.e.,
0<\varepsilon_{0}\leq\epsilon(1)
,
(k+1)(1-\varepsilon_{0})-\varepsilon_{0}\geq 2-\varepsilon_{0}
holds.
It follows from (
6.357
) that on
\tilde{C}_{2\delta}
,

|\bar{\partial}^{m}\Omega^{c}{\underline{L}}\phi|\lesssim\delta^{2-\varepsilon_{0}-m}t^{-1/2},\ m+c\leq 2N-10.

2.

When
n=2
and
\varepsilon_{0}\leq\bar{\epsilon}(1)
, one has
(k+1)(1-\varepsilon_{0})-\varepsilon_{0}<2-\varepsilon_{0}
and
2-\varepsilon_{0}<1-\varepsilon_{0}+ka_{1}(\varepsilon_{0})\leq a_{2}(\varepsilon_{0})
.
Then (
6.357
) implies that on
\tilde{C}_{2\delta}
,

|\bar{\partial}^{m}\Omega^{c}{\underline{L}}\phi|\lesssim\delta^{a_{1}(\varepsilon_{0})-m}t^{-1/2},\ m+c\leq 2N-10.

(6.358)

Using (
6.358
) and (
B.13
) in Appendix B again, then one has that for
m+c\leq 2N-11
,

|L\big(r^{1/2}\bar{\partial}^{m}\Omega^{c}{\underline{L}}\phi\big)|\lesssim\delta^{2-\varepsilon_{0}-m}t^{-2}+\delta^{1-\varepsilon_{0}+ka_{1}(\varepsilon_{0})-m}t^{-2}+\delta^{a_{2}(\varepsilon_{0})-m}t^{-3/2}\lesssim\delta^{2-\varepsilon_{0}-m}t^{-3/2},

which implies that on
\tilde{C}_{2\delta}
,

|\bar{\partial}^{m}\Omega^{c}{\underline{L}}\phi|\lesssim\delta^{2-\varepsilon_{0}-m}t^{-1/2},\ m+c\leq 2N-11.

When
n=2
and
\bar{\epsilon}(1)<\varepsilon_{0}\leq\epsilon(2)
, one has
(k+1)(1-\varepsilon_{0})-\varepsilon_{0}<2-\varepsilon_{0}
and
2-\varepsilon_{0}\leq a_{2}(\varepsilon_{0})<1-\varepsilon_{0}+ka_{1}(\varepsilon_{0})
. Exactly similar to the case for
n=2
and
\varepsilon_{0}\leq\bar{\epsilon}(1)
, one has
that on
\tilde{C}_{2\delta}
,

|\bar{\partial}^{m}\Omega^{c}{\underline{L}}\phi|\lesssim\delta^{2-\varepsilon_{0}-m}t^{-1/2},\ m+c\leq 2N-11.

3.

For general
n\geq 3
, we now focus on the case of
j\in\mathbb{N}\cap[1,n-1]
and
\varepsilon_{0}>\epsilon(j)
.
First, it follows from
\varepsilon_{0}>\epsilon(1)
and (
6.358
) that on
\tilde{C}_{2\delta}
,

|\bar{\partial}^{m}\Omega^{c}{\underline{L}}\phi|\lesssim\delta^{a_{1}(\varepsilon_{0})-m}t^{-1/2},\ m+c\leq 2N-10.

Then, an induction argument on
j
can be used to show that on
\tilde{C}_{2\delta}
,

|\bar{\partial}^{m}\Omega^{c}{\underline{L}}\phi|\lesssim\delta^{a_{j}(\varepsilon_{0})-m}t^{-1/2}

(6.359)

for
m+c\leq 2N-9-j
(
1\leq j\leq n-1
). In fact, if (
6.359
) holds true for all
j
with
1\leq j\leq j_{0}\leq n-2
, then when
m+c\leq 2N-10-j_{0}
, it holds that

|L(r^{1/2}\bar{\partial}^{m}\Omega^{c}\underline{L}\phi)|\lesssim\delta^{2-\varepsilon_{0}-m}t^{-2}+\delta^{1-\varepsilon_{0}-m+ka_{j_{0}}(\varepsilon_{0})}t^{-2}+\delta^{a_{j_{0}+1}(\varepsilon_{0})-m}t^{-3/2}.

It follows from
\varepsilon_{0}>\epsilon(j_{0}+1)>\bar{\epsilon}(j_{0})
that
a_{j_{0}+1}(\varepsilon_{0})<2-\varepsilon_{0}
and
a_{j_{0}+1}(\varepsilon_{0})<1-\varepsilon_{0}+ka_{j_{0}}(\varepsilon_{0})

hold, and hence
|L(r^{1/2}\bar{\partial}^{m}\Omega^{c}\underline{L}\phi)|\lesssim\delta^{a_{j_{0}+1}(\varepsilon_{0})-m}t^{-3/2}
, which implies (
6.359
).
Thus, on
\tilde{C}_{2\delta}
,

|\bar{\partial}^{m}\Omega^{c}{\underline{L}}\phi|\lesssim\delta^{a_{n-1}(\varepsilon_{0})-m}t^{-1/2},\ m+c\leq 2N-8-n.

(6.360)

By (
6.360
) and (
B.13
) in Appendix B, we obtain that for
m+c\leq 2N-9-n
,

|L(r^{1/2}\bar{\partial}^{m}\Omega^{c}\underline{L}\phi)|\lesssim\delta^{2-\varepsilon_{0}-m}t^{-2}+\delta^{1-\varepsilon_{0}+ka_{n-1}(\varepsilon_{0})-m}t^{-2}+\delta^{a_{n}(\varepsilon_{0})-m}t^{-3/2}.

(6.361)

Since
\epsilon(n-1)<\varepsilon_{0}\leq\epsilon(n)
, then
2-\varepsilon_{0}\leq a_{n}(\varepsilon_{0})
and
2-\varepsilon_{0}<ka_{n-1}(\varepsilon_{0})+1-\varepsilon_{0}

hold. Therefore, integrating (
6.361
) along integral curves of
L
yields that on
\tilde{C}_{2\delta}
,

|\bar{\partial}^{m}\Omega^{c}{\underline{L}}\phi|\lesssim\delta^{2-\varepsilon_{0}-m}t^{-1/2},\ m+c\leq 2N-9-n.

Consequently, for any
n\in\mathbb{N}
and
m+c\leq 2N-9-n
, it holds that

\displaystyle|\bar{\partial}^{m}\Omega^{c}{\underline{L}}\phi|_{\tilde{C}_{2\delta}}\lesssim\delta^{2-\varepsilon_{0}-m}t^{-1/2},

(6.362)

\displaystyle|L\bar{\partial}^{m}\Omega^{c}{\underline{L}}\phi|_{\tilde{C}_{2\delta}}\lesssim\delta^{2-\varepsilon_{0}-m}t^{-3/2}.

(6.363)

With an induction argument on the power of
\underline{L}
, one can show
that for
c+m+2p\leq 2N-7-n
and on
\tilde{C}_{2\delta}
,

\displaystyle|\bar{\partial}^{m}\Omega^{c}\underline{L}^{p}\phi|\lesssim\delta^{2-\varepsilon_{0}-m}t^{-1/2},

(6.364)

\displaystyle|L\bar{\partial}^{m}\Omega^{c}\underline{L}^{p}\phi|\lesssim\delta^{2-\varepsilon_{0}-m}t^{-3/2}.

(6.365)

Based on (
6.365
), we now assume that for some positive integer
q_{0}
, when
1\leq q\leq q_{0}
and
q-1+c+m+2p\leq 2N-7-n
,
there holds that on
\tilde{C}_{2\delta}
,

|L^{q}\bar{\partial}^{m}\Omega^{c}\underline{L}^{p}\phi|\lesssim\delta^{2-\varepsilon_{0}-m}t^{-1/2-q}.

(6.366)

For
q_{0}+c+m+2p\leq 2N-7-n
and
p\geq 1
, by (
6.354
), (
6.364
) and the assumption (
6.366
),
it follows from (
B.13
) in Appendix B that on
\tilde{C}_{2\delta}
,

\begin{split}&\delta^{m}|L^{q_{0}+1}\bar{\partial}^{m}\Omega^{c}\underline{L}^{p}\phi|=\delta^{m}|L^{q_{0}}\bar{\partial}^{m}\Omega^{c}\underline{L}^{p-1}(L\underline{L}\phi)|\\
\lesssim&\delta^{2-\varepsilon_{0}}t^{-3/2-q_{0}}+\sum_{c_{1}+m_{1}\leq c+m+1}\delta^{m_{1}}|L^{q_{0}+1}\bar{\partial}^{m_{1}}\Omega^{c_{1}}\underline{L}^{\leq p-1}\phi|t^{-1}.\end{split}

By reducing the power of
\underline{L}
gradually, one can eventually obtain that on
\tilde{C}_{2\delta}
,

\begin{split}\delta^{m}|L^{q_{0}+1}\bar{\partial}^{m}\Omega^{c}\underline{L}^{p}\phi|\lesssim&\delta^{2-\varepsilon_{0}}t^{-3/2-q_{0}}+\sum_{c_{1}+m_{1}\leq c+m+p}\delta^{m_{1}}|L^{q_{0}+1}\bar{\partial}^{m_{1}}\Omega^{c_{1}}\phi|t^{-1}\\
\lesssim&\delta^{2-\varepsilon_{0}}t^{-3/2-q_{0}}.\end{split}

This shows that (
6.366
) holds for any positive
q_{0}
by induction.

Then (
6.355
) follows by setting
m=0
in (
6.364
) and (
6.366
) together with the second estimate in (
6.354
).
∎

7
Global estimates in
B_{2\delta}
and the proof of Theorem
1.1

In this section, we will derive the global estimates on the solution
\phi
to the equation (
1.1
) in
B_{2\delta}
.
Note that although the general procedure is exactly same as the last section in
[
11
]
, yet
due to the requirements of the precise orders of smallness of
\delta
and the time decay rates together
with the higher order null condition (
1.9
), some details are still given here.

Set

D_{t}:=\{(\bar{t},x):\bar{t}-|x|\geq 2\delta,t_{0}\leq\bar{t}\leq t\},

see Figure
2
below. As explained in
[
11
]
, different from
[
21
]
and
[
25
]
, the solution

\phi
to (
1.1
) in
D_{t}
may be large here due to its initial data on time
t_{0}
(see Theorem
2.1
).
By the way, when
\delta>0
is small, we know that the
L^{\infty}
norms of
\phi
and its derivatives are small on the
boundary
\tilde{C}_{2\delta}
of
D_{t}
(see (
6.355
)).

Figure 2:
The domain
D_{t}

As in
[
11
]
, we use the following modified Klainerman-Sobolev Lemma (see
[
11
, Lemma ll.1]
)
to solve the Goursat problem here.

Lemma 7.1
.

For any function
f(t,x)\in C^{\infty}(\mathbb{R}^{1+2})
,
t\geq 1
,
(t,x)\in D_{T}=\{(t,x):t-|x|\geq 2\delta,t_{0}\leq t\leq T\}
, the following inequalities hold:

\mid f(t,x)\mid\lesssim\sum_{i=0}^{2}t^{-1}\delta^{(i-1)\mathtt{s}}\|\bar{\Gamma}^{i}f(t,\cdot)\|_{L^{2}(r\leq t/2)},\ |x|\leq\frac{1}{4}t,

(7.367)

\mid f(t,x)\mid\lesssim\mid f(t,B_{t}^{x})\mid+\sum_{a\leq 1,|\beta|\leq 1}t^{-1/2}\|\Omega^{a}\partial^{\beta}f(t,\cdot)\|_{L^{2}(t/4\leq r\leq t-2\delta)},\ |x|\geq\frac{1}{4}t,

(7.368)

where
\bar{\Gamma}\in\{S,H_{i},\Omega\}
,
(t,B_{t}^{x})
is the intersection point of
the boundary
\tilde{C}_{2\delta}
and the ray crossing
(t,x)
which emanates from
(t,0)
,
and
\mathtt{s}
is the any nonnegative constant in (
7.367
).

The following inequality will also be needed (see
[
11
, Lemma 11.2]
).

Lemma 7.2
.

For
f(t,x)\in C^{\infty}(\mathbb{R}^{1+2})
and
t\geq 1
, it holds that for
1\leq\bar{t}\leq t-2\delta
,

\|\frac{f(t,\cdot)}{1+t-|\cdot|}\|_{L^{2}(\bar{t}\leq|x|\leq t-2\delta)}\lesssim t^{1/2}\|f(t,B_{t}^{\cdot})\|_{L^{\infty}(\bar{t}\leq|x|\leq t-2\delta)}+\|\partial f(t,\cdot)\|_{L^{2}(\bar{t}\leq|x|\leq t-2\delta)}.

(7.369)

We intend to use the energy method to prove global estimates of the solution
\phi
to (
1.1
) in
B_{2\delta}
.
Set

E_{p,l}(t)=\sum_{\tilde{\Gamma}\in\{\partial,S,H_{1},H_{2}\}}\Big\{\|\partial\tilde{\Gamma}^{p}\Omega^{l}\phi(t,\cdot)\|_{L^{2}(\Sigma_{t}\cap D_{t})}^{2}+\sum_{i=1}^{2}\iint_{D_{t}}\frac{|\tilde{Z}_{i}\tilde{\Gamma}^{p}\Omega^{l}\phi|^{2}(t^{\prime},x)}{1+(t^{\prime}-|x|)^{3/2}}dxdt^{\prime}\Big\},

(7.370)

where
\tilde{Z}_{i}=\omega_{i}\partial_{t}+\partial_{i},i=1,2
and
p+l\leq 4
. As in
[
11
]
,
the introduction of the term
\sum_{i=1}^{2}\iint_{D_{t}}\frac{|\tilde{Z}_{i}\tilde{\Gamma}^{p}\Omega^{l}\phi|^{2}(t^{\prime},x)}{1+(t^{\prime}-|x|)^{3/2}}dxdt^{\prime}

in (
7.370
) is motivated by the works on global existence of small data solutions to the 3D nonlinear wave equations
satisfying the weak null condition in
[
17
]
.

Thanks to
the estimate (
2.52
) on
\Sigma_{t_{0}}
, one makes the following bootstrap assumptions:

For
t\geq t_{0}
, there exists a uniform constant
M_{0}
such that

\begin{split}&E_{0,l}(t)\leq{M_{0}}^{2}\delta^{4-2\varepsilon_{0}},\qquad E_{1,l}(t)\leq{M_{0}}^{2}\delta^{\frac{17}{6}-2\varepsilon_{0}},\qquad E_{2,l}(t)\leq{M_{0}}^{2}\delta^{1-2\varepsilon_{0}},\\
&E_{3,l}(t)\leq{M_{0}}^{2}\delta^{-1-2\varepsilon_{0}},\qquad E_{4,l}(t)\leq{M_{0}}^{2}\delta^{-3-2\varepsilon_{0}}.\end{split}

(7.371)

According to Lemma
7.1
and the assumptions in (
7.371
), similarly to the last section of
[
11
]
, one can establish
the following
L^{\infty}
estimates.

Proposition 7.1
.

Under the assumptions (
7.371
), when
\delta>0
is small, it holds that

\begin{split}&|\partial\Omega^{\leq 2}\phi|\lesssim M_{0}\delta^{5/4-\varepsilon_{0}}t^{-1/2},\qquad\quad|\partial\tilde{\Gamma}\Omega^{\leq 1}\phi|\lesssim M_{0}\delta^{11/24-\varepsilon_{0}}t^{-1/2},\\
&|\partial\tilde{\Gamma}^{2}\phi|\lesssim M_{0}\delta^{-1/2-\varepsilon_{0}}t^{-1/2}\end{split}

(7.372)

and

\begin{split}&|\tilde{Z}\Omega^{\leq 2}\phi|\lesssim M_{0}\delta^{5/4-\varepsilon_{0}}t^{-3/2}(1+t-r),\qquad\quad|\tilde{Z}\tilde{\Gamma}\Omega^{\leq 1}\phi|\lesssim M_{0}\delta^{11/24-\varepsilon_{0}}t^{-3/2}(1+t-r),\\
&|\tilde{Z}\tilde{\Gamma}^{2}\phi|\lesssim M_{0}\delta^{-1/2-\varepsilon_{0}}t^{-3/2}(1+t-r),\end{split}

(7.373)

where
\tilde{Z}\in\{\tilde{Z}_{1},\tilde{Z}_{2}\}
.

Proof.

Following the proof of
[
11
, Proposition 11,1]
, one can choose
s=\frac{3}{4}
for
\tilde{Z}\Omega^{\leq 2}\phi
,
s=\frac{23}{24}

for
\tilde{Z}\tilde{\Gamma}\Omega^{\leq 1}\phi
and
s=1
for
\tilde{Z}\tilde{\Gamma}^{2}\phi
in (11.11) of
[
11
]

to obtain (
7.372
)-(
7.373
), respectively. The details are omitted here.
∎

As in the proof of
[
11
, Corollary 11.1]
, it follows from Proposition
7.1
that

Corollary 7.1
.

Under the same assumptions in Proposition
7.1
, it holds that

\begin{split}&|\partial^{2}\Omega^{\leq 1}\phi|\lesssim M_{0}\delta^{11/24-\varepsilon_{0}}t^{-1/2}(1+t-r)^{-1},\\
&|\partial^{2}\tilde{\Gamma}\phi|\lesssim M_{0}\delta^{-1/2-\varepsilon_{0}}t^{-1/2}(1+t-r)^{-1}\end{split}

(7.374)

and

\begin{split}&|\tilde{Z}\partial\Omega^{\leq 1}\phi|\lesssim M_{0}\delta^{11/24-\varepsilon_{0}}t^{-3/2},\\
&|\tilde{Z}\partial\tilde{\Gamma}\phi|\lesssim M_{0}\delta^{-1/2-\varepsilon_{0}}t^{-3/2}.\end{split}

(7.375)

We now derive the global weighted energy estimates on
\phi
in
B_{2\delta}
. As in
[
11
]
, after choosing
the smooth ghost weight function
W=e^{2(1+t-r)^{-1/2}}
introduced first in
[
3
]
,
then direct computations show

\begin{split}&W(\partial_{t}v)g^{\alpha\beta}\partial_{\alpha\beta}^{2}v\\
=&\frac{1}{2}\partial_{t}\big\{W\big((g^{00}(\partial_{t}v)^{2}-g^{ij}\partial_{i}v\partial_{j}v)\big)\big\}+\partial_{i}\big\{W\big((g^{0i}(\partial_{t}v)^{2}+g^{ij}\partial_{t}v\partial_{j}v)\big)\big\}\\
&+\big(-(\partial_{i}g^{0i})(\partial_{t}v)^{2}-(\partial_{i}g^{ij})\partial_{t}v\partial_{j}v+\frac{1}{2}(\partial_{t}g^{ij})\partial_{i}v\partial_{j}v\big)W\\
&+\frac{W}{(1+t-r)^{3/2}}\big(\frac{1}{2}g^{00}(\partial_{t}v)^{2}-\frac{1}{2}g^{ij}(\partial_{i}v)(\partial_{j}v)-g^{0i}\omega_{i}(\partial_{t}v)^{2}-g^{ij}\omega_{i}(\partial_{t}v)(\partial_{j}v)\big).\end{split}

(7.376)

Due to
\partial_{i}=\tilde{Z}_{i}-\omega_{i}\partial_{t}
, then

\begin{split}&-(\partial_{i}g^{0i})(\partial_{t}v)^{2}-(\partial_{i}g^{ij})\partial_{t}v\partial_{j}v+\frac{1}{2}(\partial_{t}g^{ij})\partial_{i}v\partial_{j}v\\
=&-k\big\{g^{0i,\gamma_{1}\cdots\gamma_{k}}(\partial_{i}\partial_{\gamma_{1}}\phi)(\partial_{t}v)^{2}+g^{ij,\gamma_{1}\cdots\gamma_{k}}(\partial_{i}\partial_{\gamma_{1}}\phi)(\partial_{t}v)(\partial_{j}v)\\
&-\frac{1}{2}g^{ij,\gamma_{1}\cdots\gamma_{k}}(\partial_{t}\partial_{\gamma_{1}}\phi)(\partial_{i}v)(\partial_{j}v)\big\}(\partial_{\gamma_{2}}\phi)\cdots(\partial_{\gamma_{k}}\phi)+f(\partial\phi)(\partial\phi)^{k}\partial^{2}\phi(\partial v)^{2}\\
=&f(\omega,\partial\phi)\left(\begin{array}[]{ccc}\partial^{2}\phi(\partial\phi)^{k}(\partial v)^{2}\\
\tilde{Z}\partial\phi(\partial\phi)^{k-1}(\partial v)^{2}\\
\partial^{2}\phi(\tilde{Z}\phi)(\partial\phi)^{k-2}(\partial v)^{2}\\
\partial^{2}\phi(\partial\phi)^{k-1}(\tilde{Z}v)\partial v\end{array}\right).\end{split}

(7.377)

Note that integrating (
7.376
) over
D_{t}
and using the integration by parts give rise to a term on the lateral
boundary
\tilde{C}_{2\delta}
as

\begin{split}&\Big\{-\frac{1}{2}(g^{00}(\partial_{t}v)^{2}-g^{ij}\partial_{i}v\partial_{j}v)+(g^{0i}(\partial_{t}v)^{2}+g^{ij}\partial_{t}v\partial_{j}v)\omega_{i}\Big\}W\\
=&\Big\{\frac{1}{2}(Lv)^{2}+\frac{1}{2}(\frac{1}{r}\Omega v)^{2}\Big\}W+f(\omega,\partial\phi)W\left(\begin{array}[]{ccc}(\partial\phi)^{k+1}(\partial v)^{2}\\
\tilde{Z}\phi(\partial\phi)^{k-1}(\partial v)^{2}\\
(\partial\phi)^{k}(\tilde{Z}v)\partial v\end{array}\right).\end{split}

(7.378)

This can be controlled on
\tilde{C}_{2\delta}
by

\Big\{(Lv)^{2}+(\frac{1}{r}\Omega v)^{2}+\delta^{k(2-\varepsilon_{0})}t^{-3/2}(\partial v)^{2}\Big\}W

(7.379)

with the help of (
6.355
) and
\tilde{Z}_{i}=\omega^{i}L+\frac{\omega^{i}_{\perp}}{r}\Omega
, where one has neglected the
unimportant constant coefficients in (
7.379
).

Integrating (
7.376
) on
D_{t}
and
utilizing the estimates in Proposition
7.1
and Corollary
7.1
,
then one can get by (
7.377
)-(
7.379
) and Gronwall’s inequality that for small
\delta>0
,

\begin{split}&\int_{\Sigma_{t}\cap D_{t}}W\big((\partial_{t}v)^{2}+|\nabla v|^{2}\big)+\iint_{D_{t}}\sum_{i}\frac{|\tilde{Z}_{i}v|^{2}}{(1+\tau-r)^{3/2}}W\\
\lesssim&\int_{\Sigma_{t_{0}}\cap D_{t}}W\big((\partial_{\tau}v)^{2}+|\nabla v|^{2}\big)+\iint_{D_{t}}W|\partial_{\tau}vg^{\alpha\beta}\partial_{\alpha\beta}^{2}v|\\
&+\int_{\tilde{C}_{2\delta}\cap D_{t}}W\big\{(Lv)^{2}+(\frac{1}{r}\Omega v)^{2}+\delta^{k(2-\varepsilon_{0})}\tau^{-3/2}|\partial v|^{2}\big\}.\end{split}

(7.380)

To close the bootstrap assumptions (
7.371
), one sets
v=\tilde{\Gamma}^{p}\Omega^{l}\phi

(p+l\leq 4)
in (
7.380
).
By (
6.355
),
(L\tilde{\Gamma}^{p}\Omega^{l}\phi)^{2}+(\frac{1}{r}\Omega\tilde{\Gamma}^{p}\Omega^{l}\phi)^{2}+\delta^{k(2-\varepsilon_{0})}t^{-3/2}|\partial\tilde{\Gamma}^{p}\Omega^{l}\phi|^{2}\lesssim\delta^{4-2\varepsilon_{0}}t^{-5/2}
holds
on
\tilde{C}_{2\delta}
. Therefore,

\int_{\tilde{C}_{2\delta}\cap D_{t}}W\big\{(Lv)^{2}+(\frac{1}{r}\Omega v)^{2}+\delta^{k(2-\varepsilon_{0})}\tau^{-3/2}|\partial v|^{2}\big\}\lesssim\delta^{4-2\varepsilon_{0}}.

In addition, on the initial hypersurface
\Sigma_{t_{0}}\cap D_{t}
,
one has
|\partial\tilde{\Gamma}^{p}\Omega^{l}\phi|\lesssim\delta^{2-p-\varepsilon_{0}}

for
0\leq p\leq 4-l
by (
2.52
). Hence, (
7.380
) gives that

\displaystyle E_{0,l}(t)\lesssim\delta^{4-2\varepsilon_{0}}+\iint_{D_{t}}|(\partial_{\tau}\Omega^{l}\phi)(g^{\alpha\beta}\partial_{\alpha\beta}^{2}\Omega^{l}\phi)|,\quad l\leq 4,

(7.381)

\displaystyle E_{p,l}(t)\lesssim\delta^{5-2p-2\varepsilon_{0}}+\iint_{D_{t}}|(\partial_{\tau}\tilde{\Gamma}^{p}\Omega^{l}\phi)(g^{\alpha\beta}\partial_{\alpha\beta}^{2}\tilde{\Gamma}^{p}\Omega^{l}\phi)|,\quad 1\leq k\leq 4-l.

(7.382)

It remains to estimate
\iint_{D_{t}}|(\partial_{\tau}\tilde{\Gamma}^{p}\Omega^{l}\phi)(g^{\alpha\beta}\partial_{\alpha\beta}^{2}\tilde{\Gamma}^{p}\Omega^{l}\phi)|

in the right hand sides of (
7.381
)-(
7.382
) and further obtain the global estimates of
\phi
in
B_{2\delta}
.

Theorem 7.1
.

When
\delta>0
is small, (
7.371
) in
B_{2\delta}
holds true.

Proof.

Acting the operator
\tilde{\Gamma}^{k}\Omega^{l}
on (
1.1
) and commuting it with
g^{\alpha\beta}\partial_{\alpha\beta}^{2}
yield

\displaystyle|g^{\alpha\beta}\partial_{\alpha\beta}^{2}

\displaystyle\tilde{\Gamma}^{p}\Omega^{l}\phi|\lesssim\sum_{\mbox{\tiny$\begin{array}[]{cc}p_{0}+\cdots+p_{k}\leq p,\\
l_{0}+\cdots+l_{k}\leq l\\
p_{0}+l_{0}<p+l\end{array}$}}\Big\{|\tilde{Z}\tilde{\Gamma}^{p_{1}}\Omega^{l_{1}}\phi|\cdot|\partial\tilde{\Gamma}^{p_{2}}\Omega^{l_{2}}\phi|\cdots|\partial\tilde{\Gamma}^{p_{k}}\Omega^{l_{k}}\phi|\cdot|\partial^{2}\tilde{\Gamma}^{p_{0}}\Omega^{l_{0}}\phi|

\displaystyle+|\partial\tilde{\Gamma}^{p_{1}}\Omega^{l_{1}}\phi|\cdots|\partial\tilde{\Gamma}^{p_{k}}\Omega^{l_{k}}\phi|\cdot|\tilde{Z}\partial\tilde{\Gamma}^{p_{0}}\Omega^{l_{0}}\phi|\Big\}+\sum_{\mbox{\tiny$\begin{array}[]{cc}q_{0}+q_{1}\leq p,\\
j_{0}+j_{1}\leq l\\
q_{0}+j_{0}<p+l\end{array}$}}|\tilde{\Gamma}^{q_{1}}\Omega^{j_{1}}h^{\alpha\beta}|\cdot|\partial^{2}\tilde{\Gamma}^{q_{0}}\Omega^{j_{0}}\phi|,

which can be treated case by case as follows.

1.

The case of
p=0
and
l\leq 4

(a)

If
l_{0}\geq\max\{l_{1},\cdots,l_{k}\}
, then
l_{1},\cdots,l_{k}\leq 2
and
l_{0}\leq 3
. Then by Proposition
7.1
,

\displaystyle|\tilde{Z}\Omega^{l_{1}}\phi|\cdot|\partial\Omega^{l_{2}}\phi|\cdots|\partial\Omega^{l_{k}}\phi|\cdot|\partial^{2}\Omega^{l_{0}}\phi|\lesssim M_{0}^{k}\delta^{k(5/4-\varepsilon_{0})}t^{-2}(1+t-r)|\partial^{2}\Omega^{l_{0}}\phi|,

\displaystyle|\partial\Omega^{l_{1}}\phi|\cdots|\partial\Omega^{l_{k}}\phi|\cdot|\tilde{Z}\partial\Omega^{l_{0}}\phi|\lesssim M_{0}^{k}\delta^{k(5/4-\varepsilon_{0})}t^{-1}|\tilde{Z}\partial\Omega^{l_{0}}\phi|.

In addition, due to
(1+t-r)|\partial^{2}\Omega^{l_{0}}\phi|\lesssim|\tilde{\Gamma}\partial\Omega^{l_{0}}\phi|+|\Omega\partial\Omega^{l_{0}}\phi|
and
|\tilde{Z}\partial\Omega^{l_{0}}\phi|\lesssim t^{-1}|\tilde{\Gamma}\partial\Omega^{l_{0}}\phi|+t^{-1}|\Omega\partial\Omega^{l_{0}}\phi|
, one has by the assumptions (
7.371
) and
\varepsilon_{0}\in(0,\varepsilon_{k}^{*})
that

\begin{split}&\iint_{D_{t}}|\partial\Omega^{l}\phi|\Big\{|\tilde{Z}\tilde{\Gamma}^{p_{1}}\Omega^{l_{1}}\phi|\cdot|\partial\tilde{\Gamma}^{p_{2}}\Omega^{l_{2}}\phi|\cdots|\partial\tilde{\Gamma}^{p_{k}}\Omega^{l_{k}}\phi|\cdot|\partial^{2}\tilde{\Gamma}^{p_{0}}\Omega^{l_{0}}\phi|\\
&\qquad\qquad\qquad+|\partial\tilde{\Gamma}^{p_{1}}\Omega^{l_{1}}\phi|\cdots|\partial\tilde{\Gamma}^{p_{k}}\Omega^{l_{k}}\phi|\cdot|\tilde{Z}\partial\tilde{\Gamma}^{p_{0}}\Omega^{l_{0}}\phi|\Big\}\\
\lesssim&\int_{t_{0}}^{t}\tau^{-2}E_{0,\leq 4}(\tau)d\tau+(M_{0}\delta^{5/4-\varepsilon_{0}})^{2k}\int_{t_{0}}^{t}\tau^{-2}E_{1,\leq 3}(\tau)d\tau\\
\lesssim&\int_{t_{0}}^{t}\tau^{-2}E_{0,\leq 4}(\tau)d\tau+\delta^{4-2\varepsilon_{0}}.\end{split}

(7.383)

(b)

If
l_{0}<\max\{l_{1},\cdots,l_{k}\}
, then
l_{0}\leq 1
. It follows from Corollary
7.1
that

\displaystyle|\partial^{2}\Omega^{l_{0}}\phi|\lesssim M_{0}\delta^{11/24-\varepsilon_{0}}t^{-1/2}(1+t-r)^{-1},

\displaystyle|\tilde{Z}\partial\Omega^{l_{0}}\phi|\lesssim M_{0}\delta^{11/24-\varepsilon_{0}}t^{-3/2}.

Similarly, with the help of (
7.371
) one can get

\begin{split}&\iint_{D_{t}}|\partial\Omega^{l}\phi|\Big\{|\tilde{Z}\tilde{\Gamma}^{p_{1}}\Omega^{l_{1}}\phi|\cdot|\partial\tilde{\Gamma}^{p_{2}}\Omega^{l_{2}}\phi|\cdots|\partial\tilde{\Gamma}^{p_{k}}\Omega^{l_{k}}\phi|\cdot|\partial^{2}\tilde{\Gamma}^{p_{0}}\Omega^{l_{0}}\phi|\\
&\qquad\qquad\qquad+|\partial\tilde{\Gamma}^{p_{1}}\Omega^{l_{1}}\phi|\cdots|\partial\tilde{\Gamma}^{p_{k}}\Omega^{l_{k}}\phi|\cdot|\tilde{Z}\partial\tilde{\Gamma}^{p_{0}}\Omega^{l_{0}}\phi|\Big\}\\
\lesssim&M_{0}^{k}\delta^{\frac{5(k-1)}{4}+\frac{11}{24}-k\varepsilon_{0}}\Big\{\iint_{D_{t}}\tau^{-1}|\partial\Omega^{l}\phi|\cdot|\frac{\tilde{Z}\Omega^{\leq l}\phi}{1+\tau-r}|+\iint_{D_{t}}\tau^{-2}|\partial\Omega^{\leq l}\phi|^{2}\Big\}\\
\lesssim&\int_{t_{0}}^{t}\tau^{-2}E_{0,\leq 4}(\tau)d\tau+\delta^{4-2\varepsilon_{0}}.\end{split}

(7.384)

(c)

If
j_{0}\geq j_{1}
, then
j_{1}\leq 2
and
j_{0}\leq 3
. Similarly to (
7.383
), one can show

\begin{split}&\iint_{D_{t}}|\partial\Omega^{l}\phi|\cdot|\Omega^{j_{1}}h^{\alpha\beta}|\cdot|\partial^{2}\Omega^{j_{0}}\phi|\\
\lesssim&(M_{0}\delta^{5/4-\varepsilon_{0}})^{k+1}\int_{t_{0}}^{t}\tau^{-(k+1)/2}\sqrt{E_{0,\leq 4}(\tau)E_{1,\leq 3}(\tau)}d\tau\\
\lesssim&\int_{t_{0}}^{t}\tau^{-3/2}E_{0,\leq 4}(\tau)d\tau+\delta^{4-2\varepsilon_{0}}.\end{split}

(7.385)

(d)

When
j_{0}<j_{1}
, then it holds that

\begin{split}&\iint_{D_{t}}|\partial\Omega^{l}\phi|\cdot|\Omega^{j_{1}}h^{\alpha\beta}|\cdot|\partial^{2}\Omega^{j_{0}}\phi|\\
\lesssim&(M_{0}\delta^{5/4-\varepsilon_{0}})^{k}(M_{0}\delta^{11/24-\varepsilon_{0}})\int_{t_{0}}^{t}\tau^{-(k+1)/2}E_{0,\leq 4}(\tau)d\tau\lesssim\int_{t_{0}}^{t}\tau^{-3/2}E_{0,\leq 4}(\tau)d\tau.\end{split}

(7.386)

Inserting (
7.383
) and (
7.386
) into (
7.381
) yields
